\section{Computational Information Value and Reusable Summaries}
\PROVED\ (exact generalized-support frontier; linear persisted summaries).
Let a fixed parity batch have row code \(C\le\mathbb F_2^b\) of dimension
\(r\), and let \(d_k(C)\) be its \(k\)-th generalized Hamming weight.  If
preprocessing persists at most \(L\) linear source bits while continuation
retains the original source and may adaptively probe raw coordinates, then
\[
 Q^*_{\rm lin}(L)=d_{r-L}(C).
\]
At each reachable leaf, exactness gives \(C\subseteq S+E_J\), so
\(C\cap E_J\) contains an \((r-L)\)-dimensional subcode supported on the
queried coordinates.  Conversely, store a complement of an attaining
subcode and read its support.  The theorem permits stored linear forms
outside \(C\) and adaptive continuation, but not arbitrary nonlinear
summary fibers.

\FALSIFIED\ (compact representation implies cheap construction).
At \(L=r-1\), the optimum is \(d_1(C)\), the minimum distance of \(C\).
Computing this value for general binary linear codes is NP-hard.  A supplied
attaining subcode is polynomially verifiable and executable, but finding it
is a separate \(P_{\rm discover}\) obligation.  In the finite nonlinear
control through \(b=4\), 1,672,200 one-bit summaries produced no advantage
over the linear frontier; this is \COMPUTATIONAL\ evidence only.  The
arbitrary-nonlinear frontier, strict22 authentication, and native tape/UTM
transport remain \OPEN.  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_2048_MAIN_GENERALIZED-SUPPORT-FRONTIER-REFEREE212.tex}.

\PROVED\ (batch answer-vector transcript bound; indexed binary-source model).
For a fixed batch of future contexts let \(R\) be the number of distinct exact
batch answer vectors induced by the source family.  If preprocessing uses at
most \(P_{\rm src}\) binary source probes and leaves at most \(L\)
source-dependent persisted bits, while the continuation uses at most
\(Q_{\rm src}\) binary source probes across the entire batch with arbitrary
adaptive caching and recomputation, then
\[
 2^{\min(P_{\rm src},L)+Q_{\rm src}}\ge R.
\]
All source-dependent side channels must be charged in the persisted state.
For a linear parity batch \(Wa\) this gives
\(\min(P_{\rm src},L)+Q_{\rm src}\ge\rank W\); coordinate batches attain
every point \(P_{\rm src}=L,\ Q_{\rm src}=\rank W-L\).
Thus multiplying a one-query probe lower bound by the number of contexts is
\FALSIFIED\ when retained-source access and persistent caching are allowed:
all \(b\) source bits can be read once and reused for any polynomial parity
batch.  Query input, output, arithmetic, verification and peak storage remain
charged.  This is not a SAT lower bound.

\PROVED\ (affine contextual rank potential).
For current certified source set \(a_0+U\) and target batch \(W\), the
independently computable potential \(\Phi_W=\dim W(U)\) drops by exactly zero
or one under one consistent certified linear fact.  Duplicates have value
zero; XOR shares exhibit complementarity, so initial singleton values are not
additive.  Acquisition, verification and application cost are separate from
\(I\mid_{v_{\Phi_W}}\).  Exact generalized-support refinement for rank
\(r<b\), authenticated SAT-context transport, nonlinear potentials, and
machine-model conversion remain \OPEN.  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1948_MAIN_BATCH-RANK-AMORTIZATION-REFEREE211.tex}.

\PROVED\ (exact finite interaction model; restricted application).
For a declared state, known certified facts, target, cost model and independently
defined potential, write \(I\mid_v\) with
\(v_\Phi(I\mid s,K)=\Phi(s,K)-\Phi(s,K\cup\{I\})\).
For a finite fact menu \(E\), the gain set function
\(g(S)=\Phi(s,K)-\Phi(s,K\cup S)\) has the unique interaction dividends
\[
 d(U)=\sum_{V\subseteq U}(-1)^{|U|-|V|}g(V),\qquad
 g(S)=\sum_{U\subseteq S}d(U).
\]
The marginal of fact \(I\) after \(S\) is the sum of precisely those dividends
containing \(I\) and contained in \(S\cup\{I\}\).  Positive higher-order
terms capture complementarity; negative terms capture redundancy.  Actual
marginals telescope along a certified execution, but initial singleton labels
are not additive.  An arbitrary normalized Boolean ledger on \(m\) facts has
\(2^m-1\) independent bits; fixed certified interaction order \(k\) leaves only
\(\sum_{j\le k}\binom mj=O(m^k)\) coefficients, whose discovery and bit lengths
must still be charged.

\PROVED\ (detached reusable-summary and retained-source quantifier firewall).
For source family \(\mathcal A\), future contexts \(\mathcal B\), and answer
vectors \(r_A=(D(A,B))_{B\in\mathcal B}\), any deterministic fixed-length
detached summary exact for all later contexts satisfies
\(L\ge\lceil\log_2|\{r_A\}|\rceil\).  For all pin contexts these vectors are
the boundary functions \(f_A(x)=\exists y\,A(x,y)\).  Report228 realizes
every \(b\)-bit boundary function by a canonical proper 3CNF
\(A_f\) of \(N_b=\Theta(b^2 2^b)\) bits, so a source-discarding summary
prepared before the pin and exact for all pins needs at least \(2^b\) bits.

\PROVED\ (same-family targeted-source escape, restricted promise).
After a canonical point continuation \(B_a\) reveals \(a\), a fixed
source-retaining controller reads the first literal word of block \(a\):
a boundary-variable identifier means \(f(a)=0\), while a private identifier
means \(f(a)=1\).  Thus one source-word probe and \(O(b)\) address/work
bits decide \(\operatorname{SAT}(A_f\wedge B_a)\).  On the native indexed
bit model the word's \(\Theta(b)\) bits and address arithmetic are charged;
recognizing an unpromised source costs a scan and nonmembers require exact
fallback.  Hence the detached all-query floor does not lower-bound a joint or
source-retaining solver, and a realized run is not charged for all
counterfactual pins.  This easy-family accessor is not an all-input ARC
successor.  Since at most \(2^{N+1}-1\) source descriptions have length at
most \(N\), storing the original source remains a legal control and counting
alone cannot force exponential summary length relative to \(N\).  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1748_MAIN_ARC-SOURCE-RETENTION-QUANTIFIER-FIREWALL229.tex}.

\FALSIFIED\ (unrestricted value policy).
Duplicate facts and two XOR shares respectively require negative redundancy and
positive synergy terms.  A policy that selects only a strictly positive current
singleton value can pay an arbitrarily costly direct-answer action while two
initially zero-valued XOR shares solve the target cheaply.  Value acquisition,
authentication, verification, application and score computation remain separate
charged costs.  These are policy and accounting theorems, not SAT lower bounds.
Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1848_MAIN_CONTEXTUAL-VALUE-DIVIDEND-REFEREE210.tex}.

\section{Known Unconditional Lower Bounds}
The strongest unconditional circuit lower bounds for explicit functions remain modest relative to the thresholds needed to separate P from NP.

\begin{itemize}
\item For general Boolean circuits over the $B_2$ basis, the best known lower bound for an explicit function in P is $3.5n - O(\log n)$ (Mishra--Santhanam, ECCC TR26-260, October 2026).
\end{itemize}

\section{Time-Space Tradeoffs}
\KNOWN\ (Fortnow--Lipton--van Melkebeek--Viglas, Williams).  
In the indexed read-only-input random-access model, SAT cannot be decided simultaneously in time $N^{t+o(1)}$ and space $N^{s+o(1)}$ whenever
\[
s < \beta(t) := \frac12\Bigl(\frac{t+2}{t^2}-t\Bigr)
\]
for the applicable range of $t$, and in particular cannot be decided in time $N^{2\cos(\pi/7)-o(1)}$ and space $N^{o(1)}$ (Williams). These are the strongest published simultaneous bounds used in the present investigation. Numerical evaluation confirms $2\cos(\pi/7)\approx 1.801938$. Buss--Williams established that this endpoint is optimal within the framework of alternation-trading proofs for the subpolynomial-space case.

\section{Constructive Single-Child Route and Oracle Firewall}
\PROVED\ (elementary sufficient theorem, peer review pending).
Let $\mathcal C$ be a closed encoded class containing an NP-complete language.
If one fixed deterministic total uniform algorithm physically produces, on
every valid nonbase $x\in\mathcal C$, one answer-preserving child
$G(x)\in\mathcal C$ with $|G(x)|\le |x|^\lambda$ for fixed
$0<\lambda<1$, and all construction/access cost is $O(|x|^c)$, then
\[
 T(N)\le N^c+T(N^\lambda)=O(N^c).
\]
Fixed bounded bases therefore give SAT in P.  Bare existence of such a map is
a reformulation rather than progress: if SAT were already in P, a decider
could output one of two fixed YES/NO bases.

\FALSIFIED\ (theorem transfer).
At OpenAI \texttt{math} commit
\texttt{adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a}, the unitary-synthesis
preprint proves a uniformly generated polynomial quantum skeleton with one
smaller quantum call per level, but the target chooses unrestricted Boolean
oracle contents.  The source explicitly gives no efficient classical
constructor for those contents and states that the generator never computes a
table entry.  The quantifiers $\forall x\,\exists f_x$ alone give no
classical upper bound: a one-query skeleton can use the hindsight bit
$f_x(0)=L(x)$ for any language $L$.  A valid classical repair needs one fixed
polynomial evaluator $E(x,q)=f_x(q)$, or a uniformly materialized and charged
oracle circuit; then ordinary composition is polynomial.

\PROVED\ (restricted representation audit, peer review pending).
For
\[
 F_t(z)=\bigwedge_i(z\vee A_i)\wedge
        \bigwedge_j(\neg z\vee B_j),
\qquad
 \exists z\,F_t=
 (\bigwedge_iA_i)\vee(\bigwedge_jB_j)
 =\bigwedge_{i,j}(A_i\vee B_j),
\]
with disjoint two-literal $A_i,B_j$, the auxiliary-free projection has
$t^2$ prime-implicate clauses, while a shared fan-in-two circuit has $4t-1$
gates.  Full Tseitin re-entry has $12t-2$ clauses and remains
$\Theta(t\log t)$ bits, the same exponent as the original selector.
Reintroducing one selector can reproduce the original encoded instance exactly.
Thus compact representation is useful but supplies no fixed-power, recursively
closed shrink on this family.  Exhaustive checks covered 69,904 assignments;
finite evidence remains \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.

\PROVED\ (restricted decision--context separation; independently refereed).
For the connected proper 3CNF family $F_{t,s}$ in report202, every
unaugmented source is SAT and therefore has one decision state and a constant
YES child on its recognizable promise class.  Yet four-pin continuations
$Q_i$ satisfy $\operatorname{SAT}(F_{t,s}\land Q_i)=s_i$, so any source-free
state supporting an adversarially selected continuation has $2^t$ values and
at least $t$ bits on some source.  This exact INDEX floor is for the richer
online query contract; it is \FALSIFIED\ as a lower bound on decision-only
contraction.  The promise class does not contain arbitrary SAT.

\OPEN.  The decisive constructive arrow is an unconditional, answer-oblivious,
all-input child constructor with physical encoded shrink, effective choices,
closure at a second genuine recursive level, and charged failed attempts,
storage, access, and model transport.  A local failure or a restricted
representation lower bound does not imply P$\ne$NP.

\section{Conditional Resource Contraction}
Under the temporary assumption that a fixed uniform deterministic total correct SAT solver $A$ exists with
\[
T_A(N)\le N^{K+o(1)},\qquad S_A(N)\le N^{L+o(1)}
\]
for unknown finite $K,L\ge 2$, the parameters $\lambda=1/(5K)$, $\zeta=0.1$ would, if an all-input transformation of the stated budget existed, yield a simultaneous profile
\[
t'\le 1.3-\frac1{5K},\qquad s'\le 0.2
\]
that lies strictly inside the FLMV forbidden region for every finite $K\ge 2$. Independent numerical verification confirms the membership (e.g., for $K=2$, $t'=1.2$, $\beta(1.2)\approx 0.511 > 0.2$; for large $K$, $t'\to 1.3$ and $\beta(1.3)\approx 0.326 > 0.2$). The recurrence
\[
t'\le\max(1.1,0.6+t/2),\qquad s'\le\max(0.5,s/2)
\]
has fixed point $(1.2,0.5)$ satisfying the tangent certificate $433t+216s<630$.  

\OPEN. The required universal all-input construction of $\le N^{1-\lambda+\zeta+o(1)}$ ordinary SAT children of length $\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$, with aggregate local work $\le N^{1.1+o(1)}$ and live parent space $\le N^{0.2+o(1)}$, including every discovery, correctness, access, memory and combination cost, remains unproved. Numerical membership alone does not establish a contradiction. Primary literature supplies no such universal fully charged transformation. Exact-(2,2)-CNF-3 is NP-complete, while Horn and 2-SAT are in P and may serve as restricted test cases. Reconfirmed 2026-10-29 01:17 UTC (GROK): no new primary source closes the transformation; numerical membership verified.

\RESTRICTED. On Horn and 2-SAT instances, polynomial-time algorithms exist independently of any hypothetical solver $A$. Thus restricted contractions are feasible in principle without black-box access to $A$, but this does not transfer to the NP-complete case.

\FALSIFIED\ (restricted model). Literal clause partitions fail on expander graphs. After Gaussian elimination of a Tseitin affine shell conjoined with an arbitrary non-affine core $Q(B)$, the surviving core retains an encoding length that can be $\Theta(N)$, forcing parent-sized queries to $A$ that exhaust the local-work budget $N^{1.1+o(1)}$.

\begin{remark}[Residual Core Size]
Let $F=\mathrm{Tseitin}(G,c)\land Q(B)$ where $G$ is an expander of linear size and $Q(B)$ is an arbitrary exact-(2,2)-CNF-3 formula on $|B|=\Theta(N)$ variables. Gaussian elimination eliminates the affine shell in linear time and produces an equisatisfiable residual instance whose encoding length remains $\Theta(N)$ whenever $Q$ is not itself reducible by the same linear algebra. Under black-box access to $A$ the residual must be submitted essentially intact.
\end{remark}

\section{Proposed Mechanisms (2026-10-28)}
The following falsifiable mechanisms address the open all-input transformation:
\begin{enumerate}
\item Explicit cost accounting for black-box discovery and verification of partitions or projections on ordinary 3-SAT instances; falsify if any concrete uniform procedure meets the $N^{1.1+o(1)}$ local work and $N^{0.2+o(1)}$ space bounds on a positive-density family of hard instances.
\item Residual core analysis after affine/XOR elimination on Tseitin formulas conjoined with an arbitrary non-affine core $Q(B)$; quantify description size and evaluation cost under black-box $A$. The high-treewidth affine shell disappears linearly, but $Q(B)$ can remain an arbitrary NP-complete instance of parent size $\Theta(N)$.
\item Circuit-simulation overhead via Pippenger--Fischer before transferring any conditional decision-circuit lower bound; falsify if the simulation exhausts the margin to the FLMV region.
\item Near-boundary UNSAT certificate portfolios for the seed $T_A\le N^{1.802+o(1)}$, $S_A=N^{o(1)}$; construct minimally unsatisfiable exact-(2,2)-CNF-3 instances forcing aggregate verification outside the van Melkebeek window via $\sum m_i^d\le M^{d-1}V$.
\item Black-box query complexity lower bounds for discovering useful semantic children on expander Tseitin instances.
\item Adaptive reconfiguration and state-dependent overhead accounting when the solver $A$ is adaptive or memoizing.
\end{enumerate}
No mechanism has been established as a fully charged universal transformation.


\section{Moving-Guard Physical Coefficient Firewall}
\PROVED\ (direct compiler).
An exact boundary relation represented by forbidden rows can be
compiled to proper 3CNF by a fresh ternary implication chain per row.
For width $w=\alpha\log_2N+O(1)$, direct guard materialization costs
$N^{\alpha+o(1)}$ physical bits.  Thus logarithmic width must always
carry its coefficient, retained-source size, relation acquisition,
compiler, and recursive re-entry ledger.  The restricted four-end
consecutive-chain construction has $\alpha=0$ and a square-root
retained suffix, so it survives; no comparable arbitrary-SAT order or
summary constructor is known.  A compact circuit may beat the direct
truth table, but its construction, evaluation and Tseitin re-entry are
then separate charged obligations.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-AFFINE-CUBE-COVER261: source-relative cover versus power-short raw cofactors (30 October 2026, 20:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted syntactic-cover counting, independent referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL paired SAT/UNSAT controls; universal arbitrary-decider ARC OPEN.}
For $b\ge2$ source-disjoint three-variable XOR-parity blocks with alternating target parities, each block consists of four proper signed ternary clauses, each variable occurs twice positive and twice negative, and the original formula $F_b$ has $4^b$ models despite both coherent $0^{3b}$ and $1^{3b}$ assignments failing. For a model-compatible source-variable cube $\rho$, raw syntactic restriction leaving $c$ nonempty original clauses retains at most $2^{\lfloor c/2\rfloor}$ original models; hence any \emph{source-relative model-cover} family of $q$ such children with $c\le s$ satisfies
\[
q\ge 4^b/2^{\lfloor s/2\rfloor}.
\]
With explicit source length $N=\Theta(b\log b)$, children of physical length $\le N^\lambda$ for fixed $\lambda<1$ have $s=o(b)$, forcing $q\ge2^{2b-o(b)}$ \emph{only for this source-cover raw-cofactor architecture}. This rules out a purported polynomial-query syntactic cover compressor, not any semantic simplification, authenticated SAT witness, XOR/2SAT recognizer, or arbitrary SAT decider. The family is an intentionally easy affine adversarial control; no SAT lower bound follows. Each local original-clause S/U and its complement is tracked, all four pairwise extreme modes are infeasible within a block, and this does \emph{not} imply formula UNSAT. The companion UNSAT source adds the four opposite-parity original clauses on one block, with a seven-step source-resolution refutation.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} Full producer checked 729 and 19,683 coherent cubes exhaustively at $b=2,3$, plus 1,800 seeded cubes at $b=4$, and 376/744/1,240 clause-pair extreme modes on matched SAT/UNSAT sources, with zero failures. Standard UP-DPLL used SAT/UNSAT nodes $(5,7),(7,7),(9,7)$ while the fully enumerated source-cover experiment demanded $16,64,256$ ordinary guest calls on each label and 960/5,376/27,648 repeated source-literal reads for the paired sources. These are selected host-language operation counts, not native FLMV running-time or peak-space claims. The detailed report is
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_2000_LSPV-1_ARC_AFFINE_CUBE_COVER261_PUBLIC.tex};
the independent checker is
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_2000_LSPV-1_ARC_AFFINE_CUBE_COVER261_compact.js};

\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_2000_LSPV1_ARC_AFFINE_CUBE_COVER261_summary.json}.

\textbf{Integration and OPEN arrow.} LSPV-5's global residual-cover theorem is a correctness gate rather than a polynomial-cost guarantee. LSPV-2's guarded proof export, LSPV-3's direct-cofactor overlap, LSPV-4's realized query/space tariffs, and LSPV-1's paid batch source selector remain restricted, noncontradictory components. The affine MCSP no-go stays restricted. The missing load-bearing theorem is a uniform hereditary source-authenticated arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ that avoids this syntactic cover blowup on easy structural sources \emph{and} works on every other SAT/UNSAT input, with all discovery, source, guest, physical child, failed strategy and simultaneously live writable-tape costs inside the actual FLMV envelope. P versus NP remains OPEN.




\paragraph{ARC-CUBE-FAITHFUL-PROJECTION259: exact contextual encoding and paid certification (30 October 2026, 18:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary source-projection theorem and KNOWN quantified-CNF hardness transfer, independent referee pending.}
For original CNF $F(X)$ and an auxiliary encoding $E(X,Y)$, correctness
on \emph{every} coherent source partial cube $\rho$ is equivalent to
\[
\forall a\in\{0,1\}^X:
 F(a)\longleftrightarrow\exists b\in\{0,1\}^Y E(a,b).
\]
With this pointwise source projection, any source-only clause $D$ satisfies
$E|_\rho\models D\iff F\models B_\rho\vee D$, where $B_\rho$
blocks $\rho$. Root equisatisfiability alone does not license
guarded deductions exported from an encoded residual. In the proper-3CNF
example $F\equiv w$, $E$ has an auxiliary projection
$w\wedge(z\vee x_1\vee\neg x_2)$: the source endpoints
$x_1x_2=00,11$ are extendible, but the interior source pin
$(x_1,x_2,z,w)=(0,1,0,1)$ satisfies $F$ and refutes $E$.
Thus endpoint tests cannot certify safe switching. Via a polynomial
source-preserving reduction from $\forall X\exists Y$-3SAT,
recognizing \emph{generic} all-cube faithfulness for proper ternary
CNF pairs is $\Pi_2^P$-complete even with both root formulas SAT.
This is a certification/discovery obstacle, not an unconditional
arbitrary-SAT lower bound, and syntactically certified encodings escape
generic verification.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} The independently replayed suite tested all 256
signed three-variable CNF subsets, finding 81 universally extendible
and 175 nonuniversal instances; 8,192 exact complete-source
projection checks used 9,218 decision calls and 20,613 DPLL nodes.
Three same-source SAT/SAT-bad, SAT/SAT-faithful and UNSAT/UNSAT
controls exhaustively checked 243 partial cubes each, with respectively
6, 0, 0 contextual mismatches. The original clause-indexed S/U and
four-mode compatibility audits, both coherent endpoint assignments,
complements, intermediate flips and a source-indexed proof blocker are
retained only as adversarial controls. The encoded trial used more
selected DPLL work on both labels; no native FLMV time or peak
writable-space improvement was measured.

\textbf{OPEN:} A single hereditary, fully paid, all-input ordinary
SAT arbitrary-decider $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ producing physically
power-short ordinary children, maintaining sound source-cube semantics
under all adaptive policy switches, and reaching the same-machine
actual-FLMV simultaneous time--space forbidden region, remains
unconstructed. P versus NP remains OPEN. Verified sources:

\paragraph{ARC-ASSUMPTION-SCOPE258: source-authenticated conditional clause reuse (30 October 2026, 17:25 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN propositional deduction/assumption rule with independently
checked source-paired COMPUTATIONAL controls; referee review pending.}
For any original CNF $F$, coherent partial assignment $\rho$, source
blocking clause $B_\rho$ and candidate clause $D$,
\[
F\upharpoonright\rho\models D
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
F\models B_\rho\vee D,\qquad
F\upharpoonright\rho\text{ UNSAT}
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad F\models B_\rho.
\]
A residual policy may export a source-checked conditional clause
to a different policy or restart only after adding its assumption
guard. UNSAT needs a genuine replayable source refutation; a bounded
search timeout is UNRESOLVED. The tiny SAT formula
$(x\vee y)\wedge(x\vee\neg y)$ is UNSAT under $x=0$ and therefore
produces the sound global blocker $x$; unguarded export of the local
units $y$ and $\neg y$ falsely turns the original SAT source into
UNSAT. An original-clause pair sharing same-polarity $x$ and
opposite-polarity $y$ has no SS/SU/US/UU simultaneous extremes,
yet both original clauses are disjunctively SAT when $x=1$.
The full eight signed ternary-clause UNSAT source has minimum UP
split-refutation depth two, so bounded local proof recognition is
sound but incomplete. These are standard logical rules, not a new
algorithm or hardness result.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 256 proper signed three-variable original
formula subsets, 27 coherent cubes and 27 target clauses each:
186,624 independently checked contextual/global entailment
equivalences and 1,492,992 complete valuations. An independent
Node.js checker agrees. On 23 additional original SAT/UNSAT
sources (15 YES, eight NO, with 12 variable/polarity renamings),
39 checked source UNSAT subtree certificates and 53 unsuccessful
SAT residual trials were charged. Certified guarded learning
preserved every source model. UP-DPLL nodes fell from 107 to 52,
but selected charged logical events \emph{rose} from 4,466 to 15,793.
Input scans, source clause/proof checks, failed trials, guard writes
and final DPLL were counted; native FLMV machine operations and
simultaneous peak writable bits were \emph{not} measured. Original
clause-indexed S/U targets, inversions, coherent all-zero/all-one,
complement and flip counts, and source-indexed compatibility matrices
remain adversarial controls.

\textbf{OPEN:} the uniform hereditary all-input ordinary SAT
$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ power-short physical-child compiler for arbitrary
correct deterministic $A$ of unknown finite exponents, together
with fully charged dynamic policy acquisition, both-label joins,
guest simulation, original-source access and same-machine
simultaneous actual-FLMV time/space contraction. The restricted
affine MCSP no-go remains restricted. Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
is proved. Readback-verified public sources:

\paragraph{ARC-JUMP-RESET-BISIM256: indexed-jump invariant and native transport firewall (30 October, 16:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted input-head subsystem, mathematical review pending.}
For an honestly bounded remaining total of $B$ ordinary head moves on a complete
ordinary input of length $m$, representing exact positions below $m+B$ and all
higher positions by FAR preserves all read observations \emph{only when each
indexed jump resets the quotient from any prior state}. This uses
$m+B+1$ logical states. A previous HEAD-HORIZON255 proof sentence claiming
\emph{no later} in-range read is possible after FAR was false: an indexed
jump to address zero can reenter immediately. Its actual algorithm already
resets on jumps, so the restricted theorem is repaired rather than retired.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT conditional on index-tape erasure retaining the tape-head
coordinate:} a sparse high-address guest can perform $q$ jumps using
$H+2q+O(1)$ operations while a naive full-address rescan pays
$\Omega(qH)$ inspections; this is a simulator-architecture counterexample,
\emph{not} a general SAT lower bound or established interpretation of every
FLMV machine variant. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 17,342 random head-instruction
equivalences, 560 original-clause SS/SU/US/UU checks, 2,754 higher-order
extreme comparisons, 112 coherent complement pairs and five source
resolution-cube checks; both SAT and UNSAT labels retained. Paid full-child
head wrapping increased selected work on all tested cases, and the constructed
child remains parent-sized. Predecessor evidence remains under its original
strict status. Standalone checked public report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_1600_LSPV-1_ARC_JUMP_RESET_BISIM256_PUBLIC.tex}.
The fully charged, hereditary, all-input ordinary SAT $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$
power-short child generator and simultaneous actual-FLMV exponent
contraction remain \OPEN. P versus NP remains OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-1.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted), independent review pending.}
Any child plus sidecar that permits complete reconstruction of the
$2^m$-member SAT or UNSAT source fibers in ARC209 must retain
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ bits.  This does not constrain lossy
answer-preserving compression.  Composing an all-input embedding of
size exponent $\alpha$ with a child reduction of exponent $\lambda$
gives a closed power shrink only when $\alpha\lambda<1$, up to
polylogarithms.

ARC210 independently confirms the static connected-interface bound and
constructs a connected strict exact-$(2,2)$ torus family with
$\Theta(\sqrt{N/\log N})$ syntactic frontier but an explicit
satisfying assignment.  Thus large static or moving syntactic width is
not a SAT runtime lower bound.  An exact projection to the empty
frontier has a one-bit extensional representation, while uniformly
constructing that bit for all SAT is equivalent to deciding SAT; summary
size and acquisition remain distinct.  Uniform arbitrary-SAT lossy
contraction remains \OPEN.  Reports:
\texttt{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-29\_0100\_LSPV-1\_ARC\_SOURCE\_LOSSLESS\_FIREWALL209.tex}
and
\texttt{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-28\_1912\_LSPV-1\_ARC210\_CONNECTED\_TORUS\_SEMANTIC\_FIREWALL.tex}.

\paragraph{ARC211 static-interface sharpness, restricted.}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED (independently reviewed; marker repair pending).}
For every integer $r\ge2$, an explicit connected proper strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ source has $n=9r^2+3r$, a fixed syntactic modular
decomposition with $b=k=3r$, and exact minimum private clause-hitting
parameter $q=r$ in each module. Thus $b+q=4r=\Theta(\sqrt n)$;
the inherited static lower bound has the correct square-root
\emph{exponent}. Literal swapping preserves every signed occurrence,
and the period-three $110$ assignment satisfies every instance.
Consequently no larger static-coverage exponent follows from the
stated connectedness/degree hypotheses alone, and this easy
counterexample gives no decision-time lower bound.
The supplied archive's original $4680/7191/139$ audit counts
were reproduced byte-for-byte; the independent ARC211
reproducer checked 2560 source clauses and 4672 exhaustive

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a conditional sufficient theorem:} a single

and a strictly decreasing nonnegative integer rank initially bounded

\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED (known OBDD phenomenon, explicit grid proof):}

instance. This is not the signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF source

Finite audit: $12954$ balanced-cut checks, $110$ sampled interface

\paragraph{ARC213: exact 2SAT/XOR compatibility firewall (28 October, 22:00).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, KNOWN THEOREM (Schaefer mixed-CSP context), peer review pending.}
Every signed 3-clause
$\ell_1\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3$ has the exact existential
projection
$\exists y_1y_2y_3
\bigl[\bigwedge_{i=1}^3(\ell_i\vee y_i)\ \wedge\
(y_1\oplus y_2\oplus y_3=0)\bigr]$.
This uniform explicit reduction gives $3m$ binary clauses, $m$
disjoint XOR3 constraints and $3m$ fresh bits from an arbitrary
$m$-clause 3CNF, preserving both SAT and UNSAT. Consequently,
even the restricted mixed 2SAT-plus-XOR language is NP-complete,
although each component alone is polynomial-time decidable.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} inference of joint satisfiability from
separate binary-CNF and XOR consistency. All eight signed clauses on
three source variables make the joint formula UNSAT, while its
binary part is SAT with all fresh bits one and its XOR part SAT
with all fresh bits zero. This is not an unconditional time lower
bound against adaptive coupled solvers. The reduction physically
\emph{grows} the 86-byte sample to 352 bytes and gives no
$N^\lambda$ child. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 512 local gadget truth
rows, 256 source subsets, 2048 source assignments, 8768 small
complete auxiliary checks and 69904 previously studied pivot
assignment controls passed. Exact adaptive all-input compatibility
acquisition, recursive net shrink, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP
remain \OPEN. Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-28_2200_LSPV-1_ARC213_MIXED_2SAT_XOR_COMPATIBILITY.tex}.

\paragraph{ARC214: mixed 2SAT/XOR treewidth preservation (28 October, 23:00).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the explicitly declared mixed primal-constraint
graph, independent review pending.}
For the ARC213 clause gadget with each private auxiliary in one binary
clause and one ternary XOR constraint, contracting each auxiliary
$y_i$ into its source variable recovers exactly the source primal graph.
Conversely, to every supplied source tree decomposition of width $w$,
attach a three-bag path of scopes
$\{a,b,c,y_1\}$,
$\{b,c,y_1,y_2\}$,
$\{c,y_1,y_2,y_3\}$
at a source bag containing clause variables $a,b,c$.
Consequently, writing $P$ for primal scope graphs with parity-triangle
edges included,
\[
\operatorname{tw}(P(F))\le
\operatorname{tw}(P(G_F))\le
\max\{\operatorname{tw}(P(F)),3\}.
\]
For source treewidth at least three, the optimal widths are equal. The additive constant is \emph{sharp}: two distinct proper signed clauses $(a\vee b\vee c)$ and $(\neg a\vee b\vee c)$ have source primal width two, but their mixed graph contains a subdivided triangular prism and hence a $K_4$ minor, giving width three. Omitting XOR-scope edges falsely lowers the width to one.
Known exact Boolean-factor dynamic programming is polynomial on
polynomially constructible logarithmic-width decompositions, but
\emph{no all-input small-width decomposition or rank descent is provided}.
The gadget adds $3m$ variables and does not physically shrink
arbitrary instances. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent reproducer
checked 95 valid lifted decompositions, 543 actual edge contractions,
1752 original assignments with 38719 auxiliary probes, 92 joint
factor-elimination trials including a complete eight-clause UNSAT
control, and 8684 candidate table rows, plus an explicit sharpness control. Source-only archive audit
counts from ARC210 remain prior results, not rerun in ARC214.
The all-input answer-preserving single-child compressor, ARC-EDGE-90
and P versus NP remain \OPEN.
Report: \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-28_2300_LSPV-1_ARC214_MIXED_GADGET_TREEWIDTH_SANDWICH.tex}.


\paragraph{ARC215: exact two-language shared-interface projection (28 October, 23:37 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED}, independently reviewed.
For mixed 2CNF and affine GF$(2)$ constraints with $b$ variables
appearing in both subsystems, explicitly eliminate all private variables
using sound binary resolution and Gaussian elimination. The complete,
physically encoded joint residual has at most
$O(b^2\log(b+2))$ bits, is uniformly polynomial-time constructible,
and preserves \emph{every} assignment to the shared interface.
Hence a first power-short child is obtained if
$b\le N^{1/2-\epsilon}$, while $b=O(\log N)$ permits fully
charged polynomial enumeration. The latter property is not guaranteed
for arbitrary mixed instances; ARC213's NP-hardness gadget has
linear shared overlap.

\textbf{FALSIFIED}: a first shrink by this particular projection
compiler does not force a second shrink. An explicitly padded mixed
family projects to an unbounded shared core $K_b$ for which the
next projection is the exact fixed point $\mathcal P(K_b)=K_b$.
The four reproducible controls give physical
(parent, child, second-child) byte counts
$(1527,60,60)$, $(2641,88,88)$, $(4869,144,144)$
and $(9769,280,280)$; $1266$ small SAT/UNSAT boundary-relation
comparisons passed. Reexecuting the user's original connected-interface
audit reproduced its raw JSON byte for byte.
The source-level and mathematical conclusions are independent:
these are restricted compiler upper bounds, not an unrestricted
SAT algorithm, lower bound or P-versus-NP decision.
Independent LSPV-5 review adds a necessary firewall: every name shared by
the two component languages must remain in the boundary, or the conjunction
must be projected jointly.  Separately forgetting a shared $z$ is unsound
already for the 2CNF unit $(z)$ together with the affine row $z=0$.
Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-28_2337_LSPV-1_ARC215_TWO_LANGUAGE_EXACT_INTERFACE_PROJECTION.tex};

or a charged ARC/FLMV same-machine contradiction, remains

\paragraph{ARC216: affine-nullity firewall (29 October, 00:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED, independently reviewed.}
Mixed 2CNF/XOR satisfiability is decidable in
$\operatorname{poly}(N)2^r$ time when the affine subsystem has nullity
$r$, by enumerating a basis parameterization and solving the resulting
2CNF.  This is polynomial only for $r=O(\log N)$; ARC213 has
$r=2m$, so the bound does not solve its NP-hard family.  Substituting a
general affine parameterization into a binary clause need not remain
2CNF (the resulting relation can violate majority closure), which
falsifies the unqualified Gaussian-elimination-leaves-2SAT shortcut.
The peer reproducer passed 720 random cases (141 SAT, 579 UNSAT).

\paragraph{ARC217: guarded one-bit contextual projection (29 October, 01:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED, independent review pending.}
A uniform $O((n+m)\log(n+m+2))$-bit mixed 2CNF--XOR3
construction maps any $3$CNF $F$ to an instance $H_F$
which is \emph{always satisfiable}, with a fixed witness at
the control bit $g=1$, but whose exact externally projected
one-bit relation is
\[
 \exists x,y,t\,H_F(g,x,y,t)
 \equiv
 \begin{cases}
 1,&F\in\mathrm{SAT},\\
 g,&F\notin\mathrm{SAT}.
 \end{cases}
\]
The local per-clause gadget uses $(\ell_i\vee y_i)$ and
$y_1\oplus y_2\oplus t=t\oplus y_3\oplus g=0$.
Thus deciding whether $g=0$ extends is NP-complete, and
testing whether the guaranteed-satisfiable instance entails $g=1$
is coNP-complete.  Contextual exact-summary \emph{acquisition},
even when the external interface has one bit, cannot be treated
as free.  Importantly, the binary/affine syntactic overlap is
$3m$ fresh $y$ variables, not one; the pinned affine nullity is
$2m$, so ARC215/ARC216 are unaffected.  Since $H_F$ itself is
always SAT, a \emph{decision-only} successor may output fixed
YES; no all-input SAT-compression or unconditional lower bound
follows.  Finite checks passed: $256$ signed-clause subsets,
$4096$ global guard rows, and $240$ random formulas
($208$ SAT, $32$ UNSAT), plus local and hostile controls.
The original connected-interface archive replay remains
byte-identical ($4680/7191/139$ counts).
Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-29_0100_LSPV-1_ARC217_GUARDED_ONEBIT_CONTEXT_PROJECTION.tex}.
Unconditional all-input hereditary contraction, affordable
source-authenticated moving summaries, ARC-EDGE-90 and
P versus NP remain \OPEN.

\paragraph{ARC220: paid dual-parameter source-certified certificate selector (29 October, 04:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED (independent review pending).}
One uniform deterministic parser computes a polynomial-time min-fill
primal elimination order and, if its actual induced width is at most
$h=\lfloor\log_2N\rfloor$, constructs an exact Davis--Putnam resolution
refutation or checked SAT assignment with at most $n3^h$ derived
clauses and $n9^h$ candidate resolution pairs.  On width-route failure,
it searches a hitting set for every ternary source clause, charging
the complete $O(3^h\operatorname{poly}(N))$ discovery tree.  If found,
the program exhaustively examines at most $2^h$ residual 2CNFs,
returning a checked SAT assignment or a fully source-indexed
BRANCH/EMPTY/SCC-path UNSAT certificate.  If both caps fail, it
returns \texttt{UNKNOWN}, never an answer-class guess.
The entire one-program computation is polynomial-time under both
answer restrictions; source parsing, failed trials, proof output,
access costs, and simultaneous peak workspace are charged.

The recognized island admits one physically serialized constant YES or
NO child of at most $86$ bytes, and directly terminating base cases;
for $N\ge512$ this is at most $N^{3/4}$ with genuine two-level closure.
An unrecognized input instead retains an \emph{identity} child of
length $N$, so there is no all-SAT contraction and no proof of $P=NP$.
The exact signed-$(2,2)$ syntactic hitting-set floor
$\tau_3(F)\ge n/3$ remains a separate restricted obstacle to this
particular backdoor route, not a SAT runtime lower bound.

The reproducible program checked $733$ inputs ($561$ SAT, $172$ UNSAT),
including all $256$ signed three-variable ternary-subsets; $1029$
genuine resolution steps, $1952$ candidate pairs, $833$ hitting nodes,
$508$ 2SAT leaves, $8266$ implication edges, $119$ rejected forged
pivots, $53$ rejected forged source-path indices, three malformed
encodings and both-polarity two-level physical children.  It reran the
previously authenticated Connected Interface Audit copy exactly
($4680/7191/139$), without claiming fresh Library rematerialization.
The implementation is a \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} source checker, not
kernel verification or native FLMV machine-transition measurement.

\paragraph{ARC223: independent-affine-interface projection to exact 2CNF
(29 October 2026, 07:30 Los Angeles; PROVED/RESTRICTED, referee pending).}
For source-authenticated XOR3 quartets together with arbitrary unary/binary
outsider clauses, compute a consistent affine parametrisation
$x_i=c_i+\langle v_i,t\rangle$ over $\mathbb F_2$ and collect the
\emph{distinct nonzero} vectors $\mathcal V$ observed in the outsider clauses.
The affine projection onto these observed variables is 2CNF-definable
(equivalently majority-closed) \emph{if and only if}
$\mathcal V$ is linearly independent. Independent forms become freely
choosable new Boolean coordinates. This yields a deterministic polynomial-time
source-authenticated 2SAT quotient with a checked SAT assignment or
GF(2)/implication-cycle UNSAT certificate on both answer classes, including
complete discovery, failed trials, readback, bit-cost and physical output.
Recognised sources with $N\ge512$ yield a fixed 18/19-byte YES/NO child
and directly terminate one recursive level later.

A connected grid of authenticated parity quartets coupled through
nonentailed binary clauses has no input units, unbounded primal width,
linear syntactic strong-2CNF backdoor and more than logarithmically many
outsider clauses, yet falls into this new restricted tractable class;
it is a counterexample to an inference from these syntactic measures to
general decision hardness, \emph{not} a lower bound. A dependent-form mixed
gadget still returns UNKNOWN. The theorem does \emph{not} give all-input
SAT contraction, a general resolution refutation, or a classical polynomial
SAT oracle.  Finite tests are COMPUTATIONAL and separate from the elementary
algebraic proof.  The exact Library Connected Interface Audit was freshly
retrieved, and all 4,680/7,191/139 controls reproduced with identical JSON.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC224: cyclic-affine-core source certificate theorem (29 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED; independent mathematical referee pending.}
For complete source-authenticated ternary XOR quartets together with arbitrary
binary/unit clauses, write the Gaussian parametric forms as
$x_i=c_i+\langle v_i,t\rangle$.  On the distinct nonzero forms $D$
observed by 2CNF, let $\rho=\operatorname{rank}(D)$ and $c$ be the
number of coloops (elements whose deletion decreases rank); define
$h=\rho-c$.  All coloops occur in every basis, and nonbasis observed forms
avoid those coordinates. Enumerating the $2^h$ non-coloop basis settings
leaves ordinary 2CNF on the coloop coordinates. One fixed deterministic
program discovers/validates full SAT witnesses or source-authenticated
UNSAT implication certificates in $2^h\operatorname{poly}(N)$
charged bit time. The program handles both answer classes without a free
answer bit and emits one directly solved terminal child only if
$h\le\lfloor\log_2 N\rfloor$; otherwise it returns UNKNOWN.
A connected family with large primal width and large syntactic
2CNF backdoor, with dependence excess $2^h-h-1$, remains polynomially
decidable when $h=O(\log N)$. It is not strict exact-$(2,2)$.

4,680 boundary checks, 7,191 discovery checks, 139 brute-force checks.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC226: proof-system firewall and exact one-child contraction equivalence (29 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED elementary equivalence, known resolution barrier; P versus NP OPEN.}
For a canonical decision class $\mathcal C$ polynomial-time equivalent to SAT, with
fixed directly solvable YES/NO terminal encodings and a uniformly polynomial-cost
single-child answer-preserving successor $K:\mathcal C\to\mathcal C$ satisfying
$|K(x)|\le |x|^\lambda$ for all sufficiently long inputs and a fixed
$0<\lambda<1$, $K$ exists if and only if $P=NP$. The forward direction
uses $O(\log\log N)$ charged recursive levels, and the reverse uses a
hypothetical polynomial SAT decider to produce the constant-size YES/NO
child. This equivalence is \emph{not} an unconditional construction; it
corrects the earlier mistaken suggestion that existence of the formal
successor is logically unnecessary when $P=NP$. Haken's established
$2^{\Omega(h)}$ ordinary-resolution refutation lower bound for
$PHP_{h+1}^h$ \emph{unconditionally falsifies} polynomial ordinary-resolution
UNSAT-certificate coverage on all CNF instances; it does not exclude
stronger proof systems or polynomial-time SAT decision. A separately
source-inspected September 2026 parity-resolution preprint has accessible
Lean code but no new kernel replay in ARC226. Exact
\path{Connected_Interface_Audit.zip} was reexecuted with byte-identical
results (4680/7191/139), giving only a static interface coverage
bound, not runtime hardness. Finite executable controls and all
single-tape, two-tape, indexed-RAM and fixed-UTM accounting are separated.
The original unconditional SAT contraction and same-machine ARC/FLMV
contradiction remain \textbf{OPEN}. Source:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-29_0930_LSPV-1_ARC226_PROOF_SYSTEM_CONTRACTION_FIREWALL.tex}.

\paragraph{ARC227: one-parity 2SAT and a single dependent affine interface (29 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED; independent referee pending.}
A nonempty majority-closed Boolean solution relation $R$, translated
by an arbitrary solution $a$, is closed under bitwise AND. The
minimum meet-generators $g_i$ of its active coordinates span the
entire affine-difference space, because the principal-upset incidence
rows on the quotient coordinate-implication poset are upper triangular
and full rank. For a source 2CNF, $O(n^2)$ pinned ordinary 2SAT
queries compute these generators. A single arbitrary-length XOR
condition over all names is satisfiable exactly when the base model
or some genuine generator-translated model meets that parity;
exhaustive pinned-query replay certifies the NO case. Extending
ARC223, authenticate signed XOR3 quartets and project onto the
distinct observed nonzero affine forms $\mathcal V$ used by the
binary clauses. If $e=|\mathcal V|-\operatorname{rank}(\mathcal V)\le1$,
the projected affine image is either unrestricted ($e=0$) or exactly
one parity hyperplane ($e=1$), yielding a uniform
polynomial-time source-checked SAT/UNSAT solver with one fixed
terminal YES/NO child on recognized physical inputs. It still
returns UNKNOWN on unrestricted inputs. Dense connected sources with
$d=45,46$ observed forms, large width/backdoors and opposite SAT
answers passed finite tests. Independent 4-variable majority-relation
enumeration (all 65,536 relations) and 1,200 randomized
2CNF/parity comparisons passed; these tests are
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}, not kernel proofs.

\paragraph{ARC228 correction and charged full-interface composition (29 October 2026, 12:00 PDT).}

Theorems 2 and 17) give deterministic $N^{O(e)}$ time for 2CNF
with affine codimension $e$. Every fixed $e$, including $e=2$,
is polynomial, while their Theorem 19 establishes W[1]-hardness
for the growing parameter, not a non-FPT theorem.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as originally worded:} ARC227's

ARC228 repairs it by including every unary and binary coordinate
or absorbing all units and recomputing the affine basis.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, RESTRICTED:} With a verified tree,
source-exact constraint ownership, running intersection,
fixed local affine ranks $e_t$, and full shared interfaces
$b_t=O(\log N)$, exact raw-table composition costs
$D+\operatorname{poly}(N)\sum_t2^{b_t}(|V_t|+1)^{O(e_t)}$.

if every local 2CNF-plus-affine module has uniformly fixed

compiled to a verified 2CNF and inserted into their parent.

of $4^q$ raw centre entries; it also solves the SAT twin.

\paragraph{ARC233 discoverable local-rank block-cut frontier (29 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for a source-deletion-hereditary restricted typed 2CNF-plus-affine class; independent referee pending.}

assign each original source constraint once, and require coefficient rank
of the affine rows owned by each block to be at most a fixed $e_0$.
Each subtree joins through a single articulation variable and its
exact message is a unary CNF constraint. A source-authenticated
deterministic controller using the \emph{known} Arvind--Guruswami
fixed-codimension 2-Sub-SAT solver requires at most $2B+1$ local
calls on $B$ blocks and
$T(N)\le D(N)+(2B+1)N^{O(e_0+1)}+\operatorname{poly}(N)$ bit time,
with polynomial peak workspace. The block rank cap survives deletion
of whole typed constraints. This is an explicitly \emph{discovered}
restricted composition theorem and neither a strict exact-$(2,2)$
reentry result nor an arbitrary-input SAT contraction.
A positive binary-clause $K_q$ with $q$ private XOR3 leaves has

checked controls: 350 correct recognized random mixed formulas,

decomposition matches, and both answer classes for $q=4,8,16,32$.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC236: charged ternary-message hitting-cover frontier (29 October 2026, 18:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent referee pending; P versus NP OPEN.}
For a source-authenticated running-intersection tree whose edge separators have size at most
three, each module owns 2CNF plus at most fixed affine codimension $e_0$.
After computing exact child tables, let $h_t$ be the minimum hitting-set size
of the supports of non-bijunctive ternary child messages, and $s_t$ the parent separator size.
Any Boolean relation on three variables becomes exactly 2CNF after one variable is fixed.
The hitting set is discovered by charged three-way branching in $O(3^{h_t}\mathrm{poly}(N))$
time, and the remaining exact local joins require at most $2^{s_t+h_t}$
deterministic fixed-codimension 2CNF--affine calls. Hence, with polynomial
decomposition discovery/verification, $h_t=O(\log N)$, $s_t\le3$, and fixed $e_0$,
a single algorithm decides SAT and UNSAT and reconstructs verified witnesses
in polynomial time and peak RAM space, even when the full union of exceptional
child separator names is linear. Covering \emph{all} syntactic width-three
child edges, rather than only semantically bad ones, provides a deletion-stable
certified subfamily when the original bag directory is retained.
This is a restricted algebraic/backdoor extension of ARC229 and LSPV-4
HYBRID-BAD-MESSAGE228, not general SAT coverage. The prior ARC230 rank-one
width-three star NP-completeness result precludes an edge-width-only tractability
conclusion; neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ follows.
The illustrative per-node algorithmic work proxies
$W_{\mathrm{union}}=2^{s+u}Q$ and $W_{\mathrm{hit}}=3^h+2^{s+h}Q$
cross at $u=\log_2(2^h+3^h/(2^sQ))$; this is not a universal time-space
lower bound or a same-machine measured crossing.
Exact finite controls: 256 ternary relations, 1,536 pins, 256 exhaustive
three-name source subsets, 600 randomized formula comparisons, ten matched
large SAT/UNSAT stars, four corrupted-source rejections. The full reproducer
and raw results were read back with exact Git blob SHA checks.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC237: sharp nonlinear-preprocessing one-probe frontier (29 October 2026, 19:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, RESTRICTED; independent referee pending.}
For the canonical unary-CNF source $A_a(X)=\bigwedge_{i=1}^b(x_i=a_i)$
and an arbitrary parity continuation $B_{w,t}$ chosen \emph{after}
preprocessing, an arbitrary deterministic \emph{nonlinear} fixed-length
$L$-bit summary, even with unrestricted temporary preprocessing,
cannot answer every later parity mask using at most one additional
original-source sign-bit probe unless
\[
 \boxed{L\ge b-\lfloor\log_2(b+1)\rfloor.}
\]
This lower bound is sharp for every $b$: store $b-r$ dot products
with a basis of $\ker G$, where $r=\lfloor\log_2(b+1)\rfloor$ and
the public $r\times b$ matrix $G$ contains every nonzero $r$-bit
column. For future mask $w$, $Gw$ identifies either a stored
kernel parity or one original sign $a_i$ to probe. The lower bound
uses arbitrary nonlinear summary fibers: if the affine span of one
fiber has dimension $r_s$, then $2^{r_s}$ parity classes modulo
constants must fit among at most $b+1$ one-probe coordinate classes,
so every fiber has size at most $b+1$. In the indexed bit-RAM
construction, $P=O(b^3)$ conservative bit operations (including $b$
source reads), persistent $L=b-r$, $Q\le1$ original-source probe,
online work $O(b^3)$, and temporary workspace $O(b^2)$.
The original $N=\Theta(b\log(b+2))$ source bits remain physically
present. \textbf{No} one-tape/multitape runtime or unrestricted
SAT hardness bound is implied. The prior LSPV-2
CONTEXTUAL-VALUE-HAMMING235 proved a sharper per-mask theorem for
fixed \emph{linear} summaries; ARC237 extends the sharp $Q\le1$
storage endpoint to all nonlinear preprocessors, without external
mathematical priority claims. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 87,380 exhaustive
$(a,w)$ checks for $b\le8$, 256 nonlinear 1-bit source maps for
$b=3$ (exactly two succeed), and conditional value/synergy controls.
Full GitHub report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-29_1900_LSPV-1_ARC237_NONLINEAR_ONE_PROBE_VALUE_FRONTIER.tex}.
The case $Q\ge2$ against nonlinear preprocessing, arbitrary SAT
contraction, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.


\subsection*{ARC238: adaptive nonlinear contextual-summary probe frontier}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; independent referee review pending.}
For a unary signed source $A_a(X)=\bigwedge_{i=1}^b(x_i=a_i)$ prepared
before a later arbitrary parity context
$B_{w,t}(X,Z)=[\langle w,X\rangle=t]$, suppose every bit of the
source-dependent persistent summary is counted in length $L$.
An online exact deterministic decoder may adaptively probe at most
$Q$ named original sign bits, with unbounded other local computation.
For every nonlinear preparation and adaptive decoder,
\[
 2^L\sum_{j=0}^{Q}\binom bj\ge2^b,
 \qquad
 L\ge b-\left\lfloor\log_2\sum_{j=0}^{Q}\binom bj\right\rfloor.
\]
The proof uses the degree-$Q$ Walsh-character span of adaptive
coordinate decision trees on each summary fiber: all restricted parity
characters span the full real function space of the fiber. Thus each
fiber contains at most $\sum_{j=0}^{Q}\binom bj$ sources. The $Q=1$
case was already ARC237; the general $Q$ inequality is the new scoped
claim. The known binary Golay $[23,12,7]$ perfect code meets equality at
$(b,L,Q)=(23,12,3)$; source-authenticated direct products attain
$(L,Q)=(12r,3r)$ at $b=23r$ with polynomially charged indexed RAM work.
A reproducible finite certificate checked all 2,048 Golay syndromes,
5,000 source/query comparisons and 3,289 small linear codes, plus
nonlinear one-bit encoders and contextual-XOR controls.
All finite observations are \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}, not an unrestricted
machine lower bound. The contextual value
$v_\Phi(I\mid s,K)=\Phi(s,K)-\Phi(s,K\cup\{I\})$ has redundancy,
positive XOR synergy and potentially negative storage-penalized values;
costly acquisition and score computation are charged separately.
This source-retained probe model does not lower-bound a solver that
sees $A,B$ jointly, unrestricted SAT, single-tape head crossings,
two-tape writes or a fixed universal interpreter. Both $P=NP$ and
$P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC239: construction--storage--continuation triple frontier (29 October 2026, 20:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted two-stage original-sign-bit probe model;
independent referee pending).}
For unary source $A_a=\bigwedge_{i=1}^{b}(x_i=a_i)$ and future parity
contexts $B_{w,t}$, every fixed uniform exact offline/online pair whose
arbitrary nonlinear, adaptive preprocessing reads at most $p$ original
sign fields, retaining $L$ source-dependent bits, and whose continuation
reads at most $Q$ further named fields satisfies $p+Q\ge b$.
Strong same-source proof: each offline transcript leaves a free subcube
on its unread fields, and a future mask supported on those fields forces
all of them to be read on that same source. This is an acquisition
frontier, not general SAT running-time hardness.
If the offline read positions are fixed \emph{independently of the source},
then also
\[
 Q\ge b-p,\qquad
 2^L\sum_{j=0}^{Q-(b-p)}\binom pj\ge2^p.
\]
The proof combines forced reads of the $b-p$ untouched fields with
ARC238's prior all-adaptive Fourier dimension bound on the $p$
preprocessed fields. The stronger inequality is \emph{OPEN} for
source-adaptive offline read positions; do not silently transfer it.
The known ARC237/Hamming sharp one-extra-probe endpoint is
$L=p-\lfloor\log_2(p+1)\rfloor$, $Q=b-p+1$.
Example $(b,p,L,Q)=(15,11,8,5)$ is constructively attained.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 44 complete fixed-read parameter controls
($757{,}304$ source/query comparisons), $1{,}081{,}344$ additional
15-bit comparisons, 272 exhaustive nonlinear small-preprocessor maps,
and adaptive-source-address/XOR-value controls passed.
The original source remains legally readable; source probes are neither
all charged bit-RAM time nor native one-tape crossings. Acquisition,
verification, stored bits, full source movement, and failed strategies
remain separate same-realization charges. P$=$NP and P$\ne$NP remain

\paragraph{EXTREME-SIGNED-CYCLE240 (29 October, 21:56 PDT; source-indexed signed extremes).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, elementary identities; independent referee pending.}
For each original proper signed width-three clause \(C_j\), the local
all-literals-true target \(S_j\) and all-literals-false target \(U_j\)
are complementary \emph{partial cubes} on the original variables,
not independently chosen signed-occurrence bits.
Any two original clauses sharing variable \(x_i\) have compatible
local extreme labels \(e_j,e_k\in\{0,1\}\) precisely when
\(e_j\oplus e_k=\nu_{ij}\oplus\nu_{ik}\), with \(\nu_{ij}\)
the negated-occurrence indicator. Thus \(SS,UU\) require no
opposite-sign overlap, and \(SU,US\) no same-sign overlap.
A signed clause-overlap multigraph retaining \emph{every} shared-variable
parity edge characterizes global extreme patterns: a chosen clause set
admits one legal all-extreme assignment iff every induced signed cycle
has parity zero. Let \(\tau_{\mathrm{bal}}\) be the minimum number
of clause vertices deleted to balance this graph. Then
\[
\min_a\bigl(N_1(a)+N_2(a)\bigr)=\tau_{\mathrm{bal}},
\qquad
\max_a N_3(a)=\max_a N_0(a)=\alpha(H_F),
\]
where \(H_F\) is the standard opposite-polarity conflict graph
(Kullmann). The first equality follows by deleting the middle clauses
from any assignment and conversely realizing all undeleted balanced
extreme labels; the second follows from conflict-independent sets
being precisely those whose literals can all be simultaneously true.
This is \emph{not} a SAT or UNSAT certificate or an efficient
algorithm for general SAT. An odd-parity three-clause signed cycle
has pairwise compatible \(SU/US\) labels but cannot have all
three clauses extreme, although its zero and one assignments can
both satisfy the formula; a mixed same/opposite overlap pair can have
all four extremes impossible while its \emph{disjunctions} both hold.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, deterministic finite controls.} The seed-20261029
reproducer passed 12,640 exhaustive source-indexed pair-state comparisons,
700 exact signed-graph assignment checks, 22,400 complement checks,
66,816 per-clause flip-count identities, and 240 extremum/graph tests.
Source-authenticated width-\(\le2\) resolution can certify forbidden
partial cubes, e.g.\ clauses \((x_1\lor x_2\lor x_3)\) and
\((x_1\lor x_2\lor\neg x_3)\) imply \((x_1\lor x_2)\).
In a fixed-order unit-propagating DPLL control, saturated resolution
reduced an 8-clause UNSAT case from 31 recursive states to one but
increased the instrumented work from 1,056 to 36,670 primitive charges;
hence search-node savings alone are not net work savings.
No industrial CDCL, native tape runtime, or peak physical RAM
comparison is claimed. The complete local code and raw JSON are
not falsely represented as GitHub-published; the verified compact
GitHub checker and data excerpt are cited below. The original
unsimplified source remains available to every algorithm.
Both P$=$NP and P$\ne$NP remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\path{research/coordination/2026-10-29_2156_EXTREME-SIGNED-CYCLE240_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md}.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 source-indexed two-endpoint results ARC241--ARC245 (30 October 2026; integrity-audited).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; independent referee pending.}
Every original signed occurrence $x_{i,j}$ belongs to the same original
global $x_i$. Local extreme cubes $S_j$ (all three literals TRUE) and
$U_j$ (all three FALSE) invert to one another; the four pair modes
$SS,SU,US,UU$ are determined by shared-variable polarities.
Ordinary CNF satisfaction means $t_j\ge1$, not all-literals-TRUE.
For coherent assignments $t_j(\bar a)=3-t_j(a)$, and a partial flip
changes exactly the source-indexed true-literal counts it touches.
The user-directed experiment tracks both global endpoints, their
complements, intermediate multi-flips, actual witnesses and
proof-carrying forbidden cubes separately.

\textbf{ARC241 (restricted conditional extremal bounds).}
The earlier source-indexed two-sided first-moment SAT sufficient
condition $\Gamma=\mathbf E[N_0N_3]<\max\{1,2\mu-1\}$,
where $\mu=\mathbf E N_0=\mathbf E N_3$, is constructive by
conditional expectations; the fixed second-order Bonferroni
UNSAT-cube test requires exact hypotheses and is not general SAT.
These are event inequalities, not an all-algorithm lower bound.

\textbf{ARC242 (restricted paired NAE construction; known overlap).}
Balanced signed complementary-clause pairs give an explicit
polynomial SAT witness construction; an unbalanced signed-dual graph
can also be SAT, so failure of balance is \emph{not} an UNSAT
certificate. LSPV-5's independently checked bounded-occurrence
NAE-SAT(2) reduction provides broader known tractability and
supersedes any implied necessity of balance.

\textbf{ARC243 (strict exact-$(2+,2-)$ pin-count firewall).}
For $m=4n/3$, all coherent complete assignments satisfy
$\sum_j t_j=2n$; every model has $N_1=m/2+N_3$.
The proposed first-order source-occurrence partial-cube inequality
cannot refute a unit-propagation-saturated cube. Its equality
boundary reduces to recognized $2$CNF and is decided by standard SCC.
This is a proved restricted \emph{negative} result, not a hardness proof.

\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_0100_LSPV-1_BALANCED_OCCURRENCE_FIREWALL243_compact.py}.

\textbf{ARC244 (known Hunter forest, sharp restricted count).}
For proper original width-$k$ CNF with $m=2^k$ clauses and no
isolates in the original $UU$-compatibility graph, a constructive
Hunter source-event forest gives at least $2^{n-k-1}$ SAT models.
Every proper strict signed $(2+,2-)$ $n=6,m=8$ source has
at least four models, and four is sharp. The forest prepass on the
sharp SAT source eliminated DPLL branching but increased charged
selected work from 187 to 740. Hunter's inequality predates this
application. Readback-verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_0127_LSPV-1_EXTREME_FOREST_MODELCOUNT244_PUBLIC.tex}.

\textbf{ARC245 (mixed $U_j\cap S_k$ forest, constructive SAT
certificate, and UP barrier).}
For every proper signed original $3$CNF and each nonempty coherent
mixed extreme event $E_{jk}=U_j\cap S_k$ with $j\ne k$, deduplicate
event cubes with source indices retained. For any forest $T$ on
these event IDs, the pointwise certified inequality is
\[
 H_T(a):=1-\sum_u[E_u(a)]+\sum_{uv\in T}[E_u(a)\wedge E_v(a)]
 \ \le\ [F(a)\lor F(\bar a)].
\]
It follows that
$\#\mathrm{SAT}(F)\ge\max\{0,2^{n-1}\mathbf E H_T\}$.
A positive exactly computed $B_T=\mathbf E H_T$ gives a deterministic
polynomial-time source-verified witness by conditional expectation.
The forest is constructed, not supplied, with at most $O(m^4)$
bounded-width event comparisons and separately charged
reconstruction, probability bit operations, failed strategy work and
workspace. This transfers Hunter's \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM};
it does \emph{not} imply a universal SAT algorithm.
An explicit proper, but \emph{not strict22}, original $n=8,m=12$
source has both coherent endpoints failing, $47$ models,
previous $\Gamma=69/32>2$ and one-sided $UU$ forest $-1/16$,
but mixed forest $B_T=5/64>0$ and a verified witness.
One original-pivot non-tautological resolvent cube from
$(x\lor A),(\neg x\lor B)$ is always already rejected by
original-source unit propagation: falsifying $A\lor B$ produces
opposed units or an empty source parent. This \emph{falsifies}
claims of independent single-pivot cube pruning from local extremes.
Independent exhaustive controls passed $7{,}404$ source-pair modes,
$4{,}072$ triple modes and $206{,}685$ original-pivot partial cubes.
On the same $n=8$ source, the controller used $0$ versus $4$ DPLL nodes
but $24{,}633$ versus $284$ selected work events;
on W1 strict22 UNSAT, both explored $151$ nodes while the controller
cost $365{,}835$ versus $11{,}075$ selected events.
These are instrumented CPython selected operations, not native
Turing-machine runtime claims. Root source access, certificate
construction, fallbacks, UNSAT proof replay, verification and
same-execution peak RAM remain distinct charges.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 EXTREME-SELECTOR-COVER246: source-indexed multi-parent cubes (30 October 2026, 02:22 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; KNOWN THEOREM source conflict/hitting and resolution; COMPUTATIONAL matched controls; independent referee PENDING.}
For eight proper ORIGINAL 3CNF clauses, UNSAT holds iff the 28 original pairs' all-literals-FALSE $\mathsf{U}\mathsf{U}$ extreme targets are pairwise incompatible. With at most two original occurrences of either sign per variable, at most $24$ of $28$ pairs can conflict, proving every such eight-clause source SAT and forcing an UNSAT strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ ORIGINAL subcore to have at least nine clauses.  This internal bound is nonsharp: the SAT~2024 theorem $\mu(3,2,2)=20$ and the connected W1 witness give the exact strict22 minimum $20$ (see REFEREE218).  Shortened pinned clauses do not inherit either original-source guarantee. This is weaker than ARC244's four-model theorem for full six-variable strict22 sources. Each signed occurrence $x_{i,j}$ retains its original $S_j,U_j$ and inverted target with four-way pair compatibility, while coherent full assignments and flip trails track $N_0,\dots,N_3$ separately. For eight complete private selector modules, two original ternary clauses per pattern $b\in\{0,1\}^3$, authenticated resolution requires eight private-pivot eliminations, four $x_3$ eliminations, two $x_2$ eliminations and one $x_1$ elimination to derive $(s)$; deleting a pattern produces a coherent witness with $s=0$. Mirroring derives $(\neg s)$, yielding a 31-step whole-source UNSAT proof despite root UP seeing no unit. This is standard multi-parent resolution, not unrestricted SAT polynomiality. Fully authenticated connected chains of two groups per selector are recognized/decided in polynomial time; the tested general controller charges group discovery, failed methods, proof construction/replay, all source reads, witness verification and complete UP-DPLL fallback. Same-source CPython selected operations: matched $G_{+-}$ UNSAT $k=1$ baseline $615147$ versus selector $1308$, $k=8$ baseline $2032396$ versus selector $10520$; $G_{++}$ SAT $k=8$ baseline $135788$ versus $85977$, but $k=1$ worsens $1864\to2047$ and strict22 W1 UNSAT worsens $11825\to12005$. These are not Turing-machine bounds nor CDCL comparisons. \paragraph{EXTREME-CLIQUE-IE247: conditional original-source U-cube count and charged forced literal.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (classical inclusion--exclusion, independent referee pending).}
For any globally coherent original-variable pin cube $\rho$, let $R=F|_\rho$ have $u$ free variables and residual source clauses $D_j$; let $H_\rho$ connect two residual clauses exactly when their local all-literals-FALSE $U_j$ targets conflict on some shared original variable. Every independent set $J$ of $H_\rho$ imposes consistent unit demands and permits $2^{u-|\cup_{j\in J}\operatorname{var}(D_j)|}$ completions. Therefore
\[
\#\mathrm{SAT}(F|\rho)
=\sum_{J\in\mathcal I(H_\rho)}(-1)^{|J|}
2^{u-|\cup_{j\in J}\operatorname{var}(D_j)|}.
\]
Exact zero rejects all completions of $\rho$; this is known inclusion--exclusion, not a new unrestricted SAT method.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, independently checked.} On the original connected strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF \texttt{SWAP6} with 15 original variables, 20 source clauses and 54 verified models, the residual pin $x_2=0$ gives exact coefficients $(A_0,\dots,A_5)=(16384,40960,36608,14336,2452,148)$ and alternating sum zero, so $F\models x_2$. Original-source unit propagation under that pin does not refute, and the residual binary-only 2SAT is satisfiable. The UNSAT W1 original-source sign twin and both coherent $0^{15},1^{15}$ endpoint histograms were separately verified. Explicit original S/U targets and inversions, indexed SS/SU/US/UU, coherent flip trails, source witnesses/refutations and independent integer coefficient replay are retained in the report bundle. Same-source fixed-order UP-DPLL versus exact-U-cube-prepass controller: W1 UNSAT $151\to47$ nodes and $15356\to6996$ selected CPython logical events; SWAP6 SAT $78\to14$ and $7402\to3337$; dense planted SAT worsens $621\to7397$. These are not TM-time or industrial CDCL comparisons.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT architecture-specific barrier.} Explicit enumeration of all compatible source-U subsets requires at least $2^{\lceil m/2^k\rceil}-1$ nonempty terms for any proper width-$k$ formula, and at least $2^{\lceil4n/21\rceil}-1$ for strict22, so the naive exact counter is not a polynomial general SAT algorithm; compact cancellation and other methods are unrestricted. \paragraph{LSPV-1 EXTREME-BALANCE-UP-FIREWALL248: two additive original-clause extreme prunes are dominated by unit propagation (30 October 2026, 04:04 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted to the defined counting tests; independent referee pending.}
Let $\rho$ be a coherent partial assignment of original source variables; let $Z$ count original clauses with no pinned true literal after restriction. For every free original variable $v$, let $p_v,q_v$ be its positive and negative counts in those residual clauses. The sign-sensitive upper capacity $H=\sum_v\max\{p_v,q_v\}$ obeys
\[
  H<Z\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\text{ordinary source UP from $\rho$ refutes $\rho$}.
\]
Indeed, at every noncontradictory UP fixed point every residual clause has at least two free literals, hence $H\ge\tfrac12\sum_v(p_v+q_v)\ge Z$. A deficit $H<Z$ persists under each forced unit assignment until an empty source clause appears. The same domination holds when $H$ is applied to any subset of the residual clauses.

For a proper uniform width-$k$ sign-balanced CNF with $d_v$ positive and $d_v$ negative original occurrences for each variable, set $B=m(k-2)/2$, $D=\sum_{v\text{ free}}d_v$ and $\Theta=\sum_j\max(0,a_j(\rho)-1)$, where $a_j(\rho)$ is the number of already pinned-true literals of original $C_j$. Exact source-occurrence conservation gives
\[
 \boxed{\Theta=B-D+Z,\qquad H\le D,\qquad
 \Theta>B\Longrightarrow H<Z\Longrightarrow\mathrm{UP}\text{ contradiction}.}
\]
For strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ proper 3CNF this is $\Theta=m/2-2u+Z$, with $u$ free original variables. Hence neither additive test can reject a new partial cube after unit propagation, despite being logically sound earlier in the search. No claim is made about correlated higher-order deductions, resolution, or arbitrary SAT algorithms.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL / FALSIFIED/RETIRED.}
The source-indexed tester generated both local all-TRUE and all-FALSE original-clause targets with inversions, checked $SS/SU/US/UU$ modes, coherent $0^n/1^n$ and multivariable flip trails, non-tautological original-source resolvent forbidden cubes, and SAT/UNSAT DPLL controls. Independent controls: 1,632 clause-pair modes, 65,600 coherent complete assignments, 72 source-resolvent cube tests, 28,287 sampled partial cubes and 17 balanced-family instances. Of 12,390 $H<Z$ triggers, zero survived UP closure. On W1 strict22 UNSAT and SWAP6 strict22 SAT, a post-UP redundant counting controller retained respectively $151$ and $78$ DPLL nodes, but increased selected logical events $13{,}286\to17{,}941$ and $6{,}741\to9{,}322$. Tests are not general machine lower bounds. Original parent clauses of sharp strict22 source $C_3=(x_6\vee\neg x_3\vee x_5)$ and $C_4=(x_6\vee x_3\vee\neg x_4)$ give source resolvent $(x_6\vee x_5\vee\neg x_4)$ and forbid $x_4=1,x_5=x_6=0$, already refuted by UP. This falsifies any alleged post-UP extra pruning by these additive criteria.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 EXTREME-ONE-ROUND-2SAT-FIREWALL251 (30 October 2026, 06:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted, independent referee pending).}
For any proper CNF and any set of non-tautological resolvents each obtained by \emph{exactly one binary resolution from two ORIGINAL source clauses}, ordinary unit propagation on source plus those resolvents is logically dominated by repeatedly performing original-source unit propagation and exact entailment in the currently residual source clauses of length at most two. Indeed, if a one-round resolvent becomes unit, its two original parent clauses become residual binary clauses compelling the same unit; an empty resolvent becomes conflicting source pivot units. This does \emph{not} establish a running-time bound on SAT, and does not extend without proof to two-step resolution with derived parents. On proper signed strict exact-$(2+,2-)$ width-three CNF, there are exactly $4n$ original opposing-parent pivot candidates and at most $4n$ mixed-extreme width-three resolvents, with $O(N)$ indexed-word acquisition and no deduplication.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT source-specific / COMPUTATIONAL.}
An explicit six-variable, eight-clause strict22 SAT control contains original-source $C_1,C_4$ whose $SS,SU,US,UU$ local all-TRUE/all-FALSE extremes are all infeasible, but whose resolvent $R_1=(\neg x_1\vee x_2\vee\neg x_6)$, followed by $R_2=\operatorname{Res}_{x_6}(C_0,R_1)=(\neg x_1\vee x_2\vee\neg x_4)$, forces $x_2=1$ under coherent $\rho=\{x_1=1,x_4=1\}$. Source UP, the complete first-round mixed-resolvent UP, and iterated exact source residual binary closure infer no extra literal; all four genuine SAT models extending $\rho$ have $x_2=1$. Classical failed-literal probing already detects it, so this is a separation of \emph{defined propagation methods}, not a novel universal SAT shortcut. Twelve SAT/UNSAT source audits and 72,057 independent checks cover original signed S/U occurrence targets with inversions, original SS/SU/US/UU pair matrices, actual coherent endpoints/complements/flips and source-authenticated prohibited cubes. The fixed-order source UP-DPLL on W1 UNSAT gives $143$ nodes/$16{,}091$ selected events versus $65$ nodes/$12{,}053$ with a fully charged mixed prepass, while several SAT controls incur overhead. These counts are not Turing-machine lower bounds.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} single-generation source-resolution unit consequences exceed full residual-source 2SAT propagation. The next \textbf{OPEN} lemma asks for a polynomially recognizable unbounded strict22 SAT/UNSAT class where depth-two correlated deductions exceed UP, full residual binary implication and cheap failed literal, with demonstrably reduced \emph{total charged work}. \paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-GUEST-QUERY-SPECTRUM250: completed-call size-frequency firewall (30 October 2026, 12:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted opaque, timing-insensitive completed guest-CALL model, independent review pending.}
Suppose an arbitrary fixed correct SAT guest $A_0$ with unknown polynomial exponents $(K_0,L_0)$ can be DELAY-wrapped to make each ordinary child call of size $m$ require at least $m^D$ physical steps for any fixed integer $D$. For a fixed code-opaque, guest-timing-insensitive hereditary compiler claiming
$K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda\max(K_0,D)\}$ on every wrapper, $0<\lambda<1$, any infinite source sequence with $N^{\theta-o(1)}$ completed guest calls each of length $N^{\eta-o(1)}$ requires
\[
\theta+D\eta\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda\max(K_0,D)\}
\quad\text{for each fixed integer }D.
\]
Consequently $\eta\le\lambda$, and if $\eta=\lambda$ then $\theta\le\gamma$. The full frequency condition is
$\limsup_N\log_N\sum_i m_{i,N}^{D}\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda\max(K_0,D)\}$.
This strengthens the preexisting single-long-query DELAY firewall only for the declared completed-call interface, \emph{not for arbitrary code-inspecting, timing-dependent or preempting SAT compilers}. A simultaneously buffered $N^\theta$ disjoint physical child payloads of $N^\eta$ bits requires $N^{\theta+\eta-o(1)}$ counted writable bits. A separate \textbf{PROVED/EXACT syntax-only} strict exact-(2,2) cofactor bound shows pinning $k$ variables leaves at least $4n/3-4k$ untouched original ternary clauses; producing a physical $N^\lambda$ direct clause-list child in that declared fixed-ID representation requires $k\ge n/3-o(n)$. Globally recognizable easy signed22 orbit families escape this local architecture and do not become hard.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} same-source 48-case certified 2SAT/Horn/XOR/endpoint/cofactor-DPLL controls agreed with 2,164,676 exhaustive complete-assignment checks, including W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT (54 models), 1,466 source signed occurrences, 12,148 original SS/SU/US/UU pair modes, and 1,785 exact22 cofactor pin tests. W1 plain UP-DPLL has 151 nodes; the full two-child strategy costs 302 nodes and more source/guest work. Symbolic DELAY counts are not physical guest execution. \paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-KRAFT-CERTIFIED-COVER252: exact explicit direct-cofactor prefix-mass firewall (30 October 2026, 13:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted architecture, independent referee pending.}
For an explicitly unfolded, source-authenticated UNSAT decision/forced-pin tree on proper original width-three CNF with $m$ clauses, maximum occurrence degree $\Delta$, fixed physical clause width $h_n$ and parent bit length $N$, every logical guest leaf of a pure syntactic restricted ordinary child of length at most $N^\lambda$ pins at least
\[
k_N=\max\{0,\lceil(m-\lfloor N^\lambda/h_n\rfloor)/\Delta\rceil\}
\]
original variables. Explicitly expanding certified forced-literal opposite-NO branches gives a partition into prefix cubes, hence
$\mu_{\mathrm{source\,NO}}\ge1-g2^{-k_N}$ for $g$ logical guest leaves. In strict signed $(2,2)$-3CNF, $k_N\ge n/3-o(n)$. A cached physical guest may serve many logical leaves, so its actual call count cannot replace $g$; a symbolic DAG need not enumerate its unfolding. For arbitrary 3CNF $G$, four-clause unit-forcing gadgets certify $1-2^{-t}$ source-NO mass while preserving satisfiability equivalence to $G$, falsifying any inference from near-total NO mass to cheap SAT decision. These are \textbf{restricted necessary conditions, not an arbitrary-algorithm SAT lower bound}. Complete source-indexed SAT/UNSAT, affine/2SAT/Horn and endpoint-complement controls agree with an independent checker; no matched total-work improvement is claimed.
