\paragraph{LSPV-3 MULTI-ANCHOR246 (30 October 2026, 03:24 PDT): certified four-anchor exclusion.}
\PROVED\ (restricted, independent referee pending).
For an original-variable partial cube $\rho$, designate $V$ to be any
\emph{subset} of the free variables.  If $k$ source-authenticated certificates
each force $d_H(b|_V,a_i)\ge r_i$ for every full satisfying extension $b$
of $\rho$, a capped-distance dynamic program tests joint feasibility in
$O(A+k|V|\prod_i(r_i+1))$ indexed-RAM operations, where $A$ includes
radius-certificate acquisition, source access and checking.  Critically,
every named source witness clause for an excluded nearby $V$ assignment
must be supported entirely in $\operatorname{dom}(\rho)\cup V$; otherwise
one may not disregard other free variables.  If the all-threshold
distance state is unreachable, the \emph{entire} original-variable cube
$\rho$ is forbidden.  Four radius-two anchors $111,100,010,001$
have empty joint feasibility despite every proper subfamily feasible.
The original proper-3CNF source
$G^+(h;x,y,z)\land G^-(h;u,v,w)$ with twelve guarded clauses per
module supplies two authenticated whole cubes $h=0$ and $h=1$
and an exact restricted UNSAT certificate.  It is not signed exact22:
an ordinary pinned 2SAT-SCC solver finds this contradiction
more cheaply, so neither SAT complexity breakthrough nor novel
post-2SAT inference is claimed.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ (matched input; source-indexed controls).
Eleven SAT/UNSAT sources passed 6,968 pair-extreme compatibility
checks, 992 triple-mode checks, coherent endpoints and complements,
original-occurrence $S/U$ targets and inversions, intermediate flip
trails, signed $N_0,\ldots,N_3$, source resolvent-cube replay and
independent witness/UNSAT proof-tree verification.
Guarded UNSAT selected costs:
UP-first DPLL 35 nodes/1935 events,
lean four-anchor 1536 events,
mandatory full clause-extreme diagnostic 15600,
and 2SAT SCC 310.  W1 strict22 UNSAT instead cost baseline
11857, lean 11909, full diagnostic 22029, without DPLL improvement.
These are instrumented heterogeneous logical events rather than

\textbf{SIGNED-NAE-EULER247 (30 October, 04:51 PDT): PROVED/EXACT,
restricted; independent mathematical referee pending.}
For a proper signed 3CNF consisting of pairs $(D_j,\overline D_j)$
and with every original variable occurring in exactly two distinct
base clauses, a deterministic signed Euler-trail construction produces
and source-verifies a full SAT assignment.  Its original-variable dual
multigraph has degree three at clause vertices; adding a hub edge per
vertex gives an Eulerian even graph.  Removing hub edges yields variable
trails.  Assign successive variable bits so two paired signed literal
truths at each base clause are opposite.  Every base clause and
complement then have one TRUE and one FALSE literal.
The fully charged recognizer, adjacency and Euler construction,
assignment, source reads, and final witness replay take
$O(M+n)$ indexed word-RAM work and words with normalized integer IDs
and stable bounded-key radix sorting (or $O(M\log M)$ comparison sorting).
No hidden SAT answer or witness is supplied.  The statement covers
signed frustrated incidences but not arbitrary strict exact-$(2,2)$ CNF.
The known monotone NAE special case is not claimed as novel.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT complement-invariant extension; FALSIFIED/RETIRED
fixed two-witness policy.}
For SAT complement-invariant $H$, any one nonempty added clause $C$
remains satisfiable by choosing a witness of $H$ or its full complement.
Two added clauses can reject a particular chosen witness/complement pair
even while the extended formula is SAT (explicit paired $K_4$ control).
This does not establish UNSAT or solve the general case.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ 13 clause-indexed SAT/UNSAT hostile controls and all
4096 signed $K_4$ incidence assignments passed independent exhaustive
checks.  Checks include original-occurrence local $S_j/U_j$ targets,
both inversions, indexed $SS/SU/US/UU$ pair matrices, 504 signed
occurrences, 4976 four-mode pair records, 133712 coherent pair trials,
2045824 complement literal-count checks, 189076 source-resolvent
forbidden-cube extensions and 103696 complete formula masks.
K4 signed SAT baseline: UP-first DPLL 6 nodes/256 instrumented events;
lean Euler controller 141; mandatory complete clause-extreme audit
14792.  W1 strict22 UNSAT: 151 nodes/11935 baseline events versus
12016 lean and 105645 full audit.  All failed detectors and fallback
work are charged; explicit quadratic pair-matrix output is not
part of the linear witness algorithm.  These event counts do not
measure native RAM/TM time or industrial CDCL.  The full local
reproducibility package (producer SHA256
\texttt{0427120316da65512b80b53e4f9d01d0874e3fc979c7948cf088f675c02db332},
raw SHA256
\texttt{d33235c7a81202f1b062975115da73e0d2b937260dd896c3d72c286bc1d776a2})

\paragraph{LSPV-3, ADAPTIVE-SELECTOR-GATE255 (30 October 2026, 10:43 PDT; opaque-policy theorem, independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only within an explicit opaque finite-policy interface:} if $m$ certified silent methods are indistinguishable through their first $B-1$ charged steps, determining whether any answers by $B$ needs $mB$ candidate steps in the all-silent worst case. A source-reading or code-inspecting SAT solver may bypass this interface, so this is neither general SAT hardness nor an arbitrary-algorithm lower bound. \textbf{KNOWN bounded restart scheduling, charged restricted upper bound:} geometric $1,2,4,\ldots$ time slices find a certified method finishing in $t$ steps using fewer than $4mt$ candidate steps plus generator, checking, replay, and declared space-cap overhead. The complete DPLL fallback guarantees both SAT and UNSAT correctness but not polynomial worst-case time or FLMV sublinear space. \textbf{CONDITIONAL, previously present in CONTRACT1:} under the still-unconstructed arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ ARC recursion with selector multiplier $N^\eta$, the formal fixed time is $6/5+2\eta$; at actual FLMV $c=241/200$, $d=11642479/23232400$, strict interior needs $\eta<1/400$. This arithmetic does not supply a compiler. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the 11-method source-verified portfolio was slower in selected work than UP-DPLL on W1 UNSAT (65,725 vs. 11,555), SWAP6 SAT (32,577 vs. 5,925) and signed-renamed easy strict22 orbit (13,374 vs. 1,606), but beat UP-DPLL on a canonical source-known easy exact22 orbit (556 vs. 1,897). Same-source YES/NO, Horn, Krom, affine, source S/U and complement checks passed independent finite verification; no CDCL or native FLMV bit-space comparison was run. Forward/reverse memo counts and M22 parameters are not mandatory resources for arbitrary solvers. The all-input hereditary source-authenticated ARC successor and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \OPEN. \subsection*{LSPV-3 exact acquisition countercontrol: TSEITIN-SELECTOR-GATE255 (2026-10-30 11:29)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted canonical-source theorem, independent review pending.}
For every connected simple cubic graph with $v$ vertices and an arbitrary vector of charge bits $b$, the exact four-clause-per-vertex 3CNF Tseitin source has SAT iff $\bigoplus_t b_t=0$; every proper subset of complete charge blocks is SAT; the same condition equals even $N_0(0^e)$ \emph{only after an exact source XOR-grammar verification}. A deterministic exact decider for this canonical fixed-graph promise must inspect one charge-dependent source bit in every one of its $v=\Omega(N/\log N)$ source blocks on every input, by a one-charge-flip indistinguishability adversary. The input-read bound is tight and does not force high workspace, arbitrary SAT runtime or mandatory per-child re-reading: a shared one-bit parity summary suffices for repeated decision queries. Under the expressly \emph{no-sharing} architecture in which each of $N^{3/5}$ child operations must separately decide complete global parity, accumulated parent source probes are $N^{8/5-o(1)}$, outside the proposed FLMV $c=241/200$ box. That is a \emph{restricted architecture falsifier}, not an ARC obstruction for arbitrary children. The family is signed exact-$(4+,4-)$, not strict22.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} one uniform charged Krom/Horn/dual-Horn/XOR-certified/fallback DPLL controller passed both SAT and UNSAT cases, 10,872 proper-subset rank tests, 64 exhaustively enumerated small charge vectors, 384 single-charge toggle controls, 32 polarity-and-variable renamings, and 240 random mixed-CNF truth-table controls. On odd-charge six-vertex source, 400 selected controller events versus 3,137 ordinary DPLL events; no CDCL or FLMV-machine measurement is claimed. Original signed22 W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT both have $N_0(0^{15})=1$, \textbf{falsifying} unguarded endpoint parity; their source does not match the cubic XOR grammar and falls back. Source-query acquisition, recognition storage and witness reconstruction are charged; this implementation has $O(N)$ writable indexing and does \emph{not} achieve the proposed ARC space bound.
\textbf{OPEN:} hereditary all-input arbitrary-$A$ SAT-to-ordinary-SAT power-shrinking compiler with fully charged source access and simultaneous FLMV time/space. Full role report: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1129_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_SELECTOR_GATE255.tex}; checked compact source: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1129_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_SELECTOR_GATE255_check.py}; summary data: \path{results/raw/clause_extremes/2026-10-30_1129_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_SELECTOR_GATE255_summary.json}.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 SAT-ONLY-SELECTOR-FIREWALL256 (30 October 2026, 12:24 PDT; independent review pending).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM, elementary self-reducibility.}
A uniform polynomial-time total selector that, on every ordinary SAT
residual, chooses at least one consistent original-variable assignment
preserving SAT whenever the source is SAT (with no selection promise on
UNSAT inputs) exists exactly if SAT is in P. Iterating the selector and
checking the complete original-source assignment gives a total both-label
polynomial decider; conversely a polynomial SAT decider can test the
zero cofactor before choosing the safe side. This does \emph{not}
supply a selector or show P=NP.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted architecture, review pending.}
A purported arbitrary-$A$ ARC stage that invokes a correct guest
polynomial SAT decider $A$ on a direct cofactor of physical size
$\Theta(N)$ cannot uniformly meet
$K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda K\}$ for all polynomial guest
exponents $K$ when $\lambda<1$: legal DELAY wrappers of the
guest incur $\Theta(N^K)$ on this full-parent call. This rules out
that particular parent-sized guest policy, not arbitrary adaptive
source-aware choices. The strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ direct
one-pin cofactor retains at least $m-4$ untouched source clauses,
as already proved by LSPV-2 COFACTOR-VOLUME-ARC252.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent exhaustive checks of all 256
proper signed three-variable clause subsets validate the safe-selector
logic; 15 SAT cases invalidate blind first-zero selection. Authentic
strict22 W1 (UNSAT) and SWAP6 (SAT, 54 models), signed renamings,
Horn, Krom, XOR, and original-clause $S/U$ extremal controls were
tested. Same-engine selected work on W1 was 15,451 standard DPLL
versus 14,343 charged guest-selector events; on SWAP6 it was 7,518
versus 15,575, with full source access and guest outputs paid.
These are not native FLMV time--space measurements.
The complete four-page PDF, source, experiment implementation and
raw data are persistently stored in Library
\texttt{libfile\_a5b1e65258988191b2c78f2fb370609f}.

\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1224_LSPV-3_SAT_ONLY_SELECTOR_FIREWALL256_PUBLIC.tex}.

\[
 K'\le\max\{\alpha_0,\gamma+\lambda+\delta_s,\gamma+\delta_e+\lambda K\},
 \qquad L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda L\}.
\]
With parameters $(\alpha_0,\gamma,\lambda,\sigma)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$, formal fixed point is
$K_*=\max\{11/10+\delta_s,6/5+2\delta_e\}$, so the \emph{upper-bound recurrence} is strictly within the actual FLMV box at $c=241/200$, $d=11642479/23232400$ if $\delta_s<21/200$ and $\delta_e<1/400$. The guest-step margin was known in CONTRACT1; the additive tariff is a new explicit accounting distinction, not an ARC compiler or arbitrary-machine lower bound. If scans reread the full parent or guest bit access repeatedly pays parent-scale work, the small-child premise fails. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 58 source-authenticated SAT/UNSAT controls (52/6), including known heterogeneous Horn/XOR one-variable backdoors, strict22 W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT, signed variants, finite GF(2)/Horn/Krom and exhaustive random 3CNF; an independent checker validated original source clause extremes, witnesses and exact rational FLMV tail. \paragraph{LSPV-3 ARC-SIGNED-OVERLAP-CHILD-GATE258 (2026-10-30).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted direct-cofactor original-source accounting; independent review pending.}
For a proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF, a coherent partial assignment of $t$ variables has exactly $2t$ assigned-TRUE source occurrences. Writing $s_j$ for their multiplicity in original clause $C_j$, $D=\#\{j:s_j>0\}$ for deleted clauses, and $\Omega=\sum_j(s_j-1)_+$ for repeated coverage, one has the exact state-dependent identity
$D=2t-\Omega$.  A further pin $x=b$ changes the deleted-clause count by $2-h_x(b;\rho)$, where $h_x$ counts the already deleted source clauses among the two satisfying occurrences of $x=b$; both updates use authenticated original occurrences and must charge acquisition.  Combining the previously verified LSPV-4 signed22 $D\le2t$ bound with the previously verified LSPV-1 explicit prefix-cylinder Kraft theorem gives a physically serialized direct-cofactor child-size requirement and corresponding source-certified NO-cube mass bound. This is \emph{not} a lower bound on arbitrary SAT algorithms, semantic compilers, or cached DAGs.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, exact-source finite controls.}
On the $15$-variable W1 UNSAT/SWAP6 SAT signed22 twins (20 clauses, 349-bit declared source encoding, 18-bit child cap, 33-guest cap), a source-conflict-aware degree policy produced a complete W1 refutation with $413$ original-clause NO cubes and no guest, and produced $16$ verified short ordinary SAT residual children for SWAP6, with $412$ source-NO cubes. Their fully charged \emph{instrumented} controller totals were 81,850 and 86,368 selected events versus UP-first DPLL 15,193 and 7,633. The alternate first-variable SWAP6 policy yielded $36>33$ children. A family of easy connected strict22 formulas with a polynomial-time source-known witness remains a hostile counterexample to treating large direct-cofactor guest counts as SAT hardness. Independent finite checker passed 184,300 checks; original $S/U$ occurrence targets, source-indexed four-mode pair matrices, coherent endpoints and partial flips remain adversarial controls. Full raw data and checker: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1424_LSPV-3_ARC_SIGNED_OVERLAP_CHILD_GATE258_summary.json}; complete archive in persistent Library \path{/P-vs-NP/2026-10-30_1424_LSPV-3_ARC_SIGNED_OVERLAP_CHILD_GATE258_COMPLETE.zip}.
\textbf{OPEN:} The uniform hereditary both-label arbitrary-decider $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$, genuine power-short ordinary SAT child construction on \emph{all} inputs, charged policy discovery, source/random-access guest simulation and simultaneous FLMV time-space contraction. No $P=NP$ or $P\ne NP$ conclusion follows.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 FRONTIER-TWO-SWEEP259 (2026-10-30).}
\textbf{KNOWN DP SPECIALIZATION / EXACT source-proof, independent review pending.}
For a paid original-clause order $\pi$, the cut boundary
$B_i=\operatorname{Var}(C_{\pi(\leq i)})
\cap\operatorname{Var}(C_{\pi(>i)})$ and local union
$U_i=B_{i-1}\cup\operatorname{Var}(C_{\pi(i)})$
support exact forward and reverse Boolean satisfiability messages.
Joining those messages with the actual signed original $C_{\pi(i)}$
determines in one bidirectional computation every globally supported
source-clause variable pattern and every backbone literal.
Unrealizable patterns give source-authenticated forbidden partial cubes,
including on satisfiable formulas.  Work is
$O(N+D_\pi+\sum_i2^{|U_i|}\operatorname{poly}(|U_i|+\log N))$,
where clause-order discovery, scanning, proof replay, and source access
are charged; retaining all tables has generally
$\Omega(\sum_i2^{|B_i|})$ states.  This specializes the established
project--join/full-interface DP method, not a novel all-input SAT algorithm.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL / negative uniform-cost gate.}
An independently replayed 27-formula experiment contained 22 SAT and
five UNSAT cases; all 25 implemented DP runs matched exhaustive model
enumeration, with 17,856 independently replayed table rows.  On the
15-variable strict22 SWAP6 SAT twin with 54 models, two sweeps certify
$x_2=x_{15}=1$ and 50 nontrivial original-clause-scope forbidden
patterns; 46 survive original UP and have satisfiable residual binary-only
2SAT.  In particular the SAT-satisfied cube
$(x_2,x_8,x_9)=(0,0,0)$ is globally forbidden, proving
$F\models(x_2\vee x_8\vee x_9)$, but its acquisition is not free.
Measured Python median milliseconds with policy selection and proof
checking were 122.962 adaptive versus 5.919 baseline for SWAP6,
and 113.405 versus 13.561 for W1.  The easy source-known signed22
orbit exhibits width 13,15,17 with only 21,27,33 baseline DPLL
nodes, falsifying width as mandatory arbitrary-solver hardness.
Two dense hostile controls triggered paid greedy discovery and DPLL
fallback.  Original clause $S/U$ targets, their inversions, indexed
SS/SU/US/UU modes, coherent endpoints and complement/flip trails
remain adversarial checks only.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 source-frontier recursive replay (RECURSIVE-FRONTIER-REPLAY260).}
\textbf{KNOWN METHOD SPECIALIZATION, exact restricted proof, independent review pending.}
For the source-authenticated original-clause order, let $B_i$ be the prefix/suffix shared variables, $U_i=B_{i-1}\cup{\rm Var}(C_i)$, and $w=\max_i|U_i|$.
The previously established exact Boolean forward/reverse frontier-support identities admit a Hirschberg-style divide-and-conquer replay: on interval $(a,b]$, compute global midpoint prefix and suffix messages and recurse on both sides. At each singleton join the original clause with its actual global boundary messages; this emits exactly all $H_i(u)$, so unsupported original-source local patterns are formally forbidden partial cubes for SAT and UNSAT instances alike. A \emph{streaming} implementation retains $O(2^w(w+\log N)\log(m+1)+S_B+\log^2(N+2))$ simultaneous writable bits while paying $O(N+\log(m+1)\sum_i[d_{i-1}+d_i+2^{|U_i|}{\rm poly}(|U_i|+k+\log N)]+mk2^k)$ indexed-word time; here $d_i$ and $S_B$ explicitly charge source-boundary discovery and access, and every streamed output symbol is billed. Retaining all output in RAM removes the low-space claim. The recognizer for the numeric source-band grammar $|v-i|\le b$ charges the initial source scan and $O(b^2k)$ source-occurrence work per boundary, but low numeric band is not invariant under variable renaming and not arbitrary hardness.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, adverse fully charged same-source controls.}
Independent full-table/global-mask replay and small exhaustive source-oracle checks agree on 23 original formulas and 9,958 checked records, with two corrupted outputs rejected. W1 strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ is UNSAT and SWAP6 is SAT with 54 models; SWAP6 has 70 globally unsupported local patterns of 160. Band128 SAT/UNSAT recursive replay uses 1,917,887/1,915,434 selected host operations versus full DP 839,166/837,844 and UP-first DPLL 61,057/17,566. W1/SWAP6 replay uses 1,604,689/1,602,849 versus DPLL 19,962/9,548. Band128 SAT replay has a conservative 111 live-table-entry envelope versus 2,115 full retained entries, but W1's loose replay envelope is \emph{worse} than retaining its tables. Original clause-indexed signed $S/U$ targets, inversions, extreme compatibility and coherent complement flips were checked as adverse controls, not used as semantic oracle decisions. These instrumented events are not native FLMV machine costs. The full report, executable producer and independent verifier, raw output tables and hashes are archived in persistent Library \path{/P-vs-NP/2026-10-30_1624_LSPV-3_RECURSIVE_FRONTIER_REPLAY260_COMPLETE.zip}; the compact public LaTeX proof record is \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1624_LSPV-3_RECURSIVE_FRONTIER_REPLAY260_PUBLIC.tex}.
\textbf{OPEN:} this is not an ordinary SAT child of power-short physical length, and an all-input hereditary arbitrary-$A$ ARC contractor with fully charged source/output/guest access and simultaneous FLMV space remains unconstructed; both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 ARC-BRANCH-MASS-GATE261 (30 October 2026, 17:35 PDT; restricted theorem, independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted source-preserving cofactor-tree accounting.}
For strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ source $F$ on $n$ variables with $m=4n/3$ original clauses, a partial assignment fixing $t$ variables eliminates exactly $D=2t-\Omega$ clauses, where $\Omega=\sum_j\max(0,s_j-1)$ and $s_j$ counts fixed TRUE literals in the original $C_j$. In a complete binary source-authenticated cofactor partition with rejected cubes $P$, fully satisfied leaves $S$ and exact residual children $E$ containing at most $r$ original clauses, $t_*=\lceil(m-r)/2\rceil$ and
\[
\sum_{P}2^{-|\rho|}+\sum_{S}2^{-|\rho|}
+\sum_{E}2^{-|\rho|}=1,\qquad
\sum_{P}2^{-|\rho|}+\sum_{S}2^{-|\rho|}\geq1-|E|2^{-t_*}.
\]
Every inferred unit is paired with its \emph{source-justified} contradictory binary sibling, rather than charged as a free disappearance of assignment mass. This is a combination of the classical binary-tree Kraft equality and earlier signed-overlap accounting, not a novel SAT lower bound. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} same-source W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT (0 and 54 models) satisfy the independently replayed coverage; at the 8-original-clause cutoff, max-occurrence selection gives respectively 3 and 11 physical residual children, but their encodings remain 86--117 bits versus the proposed 19-bit child limit. The SAT orbit21 signed22 family has an explicit witness despite local nonshrinkage. All source scans, policy tests, failed rules, proof checks, guest DPLL calls, physical child writes and live records are charged in the declared instrumented word-event model; this is not native FLMV work/space. Report: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1735_LSPV-3_ARC_BRANCH_MASS_GATE261.tex}; code and verifiers: \path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1735_LSPV-3_ARC_BRANCH_MASS_GATE261.py} and associated \texttt{verify} scripts; summary: \path{results/summaries/arc/2026-10-30_1735_LSPV-3_ARC_BRANCH_MASS_GATE261_summary.json}. The hereditary arbitrary-$A$ SAT successor and simultaneous FLMV contraction remain \OPEN; $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 RUP-CORE-REUSE262 (30 October 2026, 18:24 PDT; restricted source-authenticated theorem, independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted fixed-CNF proof invariant:} If unit propagation under a current branch-decision set $D$ conflicts relative to the original source and earlier RUP-validated learned clauses, the assumption-negation clause
$L_D=\bigvee_{\ell\in D}\neg\ell$ is a globally implied, RUP-checkable source consequence, safe to retain after backtracking.  A deletion-based UP check can produce an inclusion-minimal contradictory assumption set; its acquisition, unsuccessful deletions and replay are charged.  This is classical clause learning, not a new SAT method.  A per-leaf naive indexed-word bound is $O((d+2)nL)$ for depth $d$ and current total literal count $L$; the number of leaves remains potentially exponential.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} An independently replayed 60-formula both-label control (50 SAT, 10 UNSAT) checked 1,952 learned RUP clauses, 3,574 conflict leaves and 7,829 proof-tree nodes.  A specially ordered but easily recognized Krom UNSAT family at $k=9$ decreased from 2,047 baseline DPLL nodes and 622,845 selected operations to 21 nodes and 28,107 operations under full assumption-core minimization; ordinary 2SAT SCC instead decides the same easy family in only 356 selected events.  W1 strict22 UNSAT decreased from 143 to 45 nodes but cost rose from 20,186 to 68,010 events; SWAP6 strict22 SAT decreased from 78 to 32 nodes while cost rose from 10,483 to 37,555 events.  These are complete source-checking word-level instrumentation and Python memory observations, \emph{not} native FLMV machine complexity or industrial CDCL comparisons.  Original signed clause $S/U$ extremes, flips and polarity renamings remain adversarial controls.  More compact learned certificates do not by themselves create smaller ordinary SAT children or a uniform adaptive policy.

\textbf{ARC bridge OPEN.}  The result permits source-authenticated RUP cache reuse across backtracking in a fixed formula but supplies neither a hereditary, both-label, arbitrary-$A$ SAT-to-shorter-SAT contractor nor the same-machine $(241/200,11642479/23232400)$ forbidden FLMV pair. \paragraph{LSPV-3 ARC-PARITY-MOMENT-CAMOUFLAGE264 (2026-10-30 20:24 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted known parity/linear-algebra specialization, independent mathematical review pending.} For a connected cubic graph on even $q$ vertices, four original proper 3CNF clauses per vertex encode signed XOR charges on $n=3q/2$ edge variables. Changing the overall charge parity flips SAT to UNSAT while keeping the unsigned source-support hypergraph identical and preserving every raw and factorial moment of the globally coherent false-original-clause count $N_0$ through degree $q-1$; the $q$th factorial moments differ by $q!/2^{q-1}$. The SAT source has $2^{n-q+1}$ models; the UNSAT source has none. This is the familiar affine parity syndrome property specialized into an explicit arbitrary-order falsifier for a \emph{moment-only} SAT policy; it is not a lower bound on arbitrary algorithms. Source-authenticated XOR recognition and paid Gaussian elimination decide both labels. As a strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ adverse pair, the W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT twins have identical unsigned clause supports and equal first two $N_0$ moments but different cubic moments and 0 versus 54 models.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} The independent checker exhaustively replayed 664624 complete assignments over 16 original sources (8 SAT, 8 UNSAT), 23136 source-indexed $SS/SU/US/UU$ extreme modes, 1038 signed occurrences, 83095 complement checks, 72 authenticated parity blocks and four source-derived odd-charge UNSAT XOR proofs, rejecting three corrupt records. For prism $q=6,8,10,12$, UP-DPLL SAT/UNSAT selected events were 448/2865, 736/9065, 1095/26113, 1525/70825; a paid XOR-aware controller used 220/137, 286/177, 352/217, 418/257. These are explicit Python work proxies, not native FLMV bit-machine transitions. Complete source $S_j/U_j$ target inversions, coherent endpoints and Gray flips were retained as adversarial controls; both endpoints fail on all eight parity formulas. The controller creates no ordinary power-short child, and its all-input fallback is not polynomially bounded.
\textbf{ARC OPEN.} The only load-bearing arrow remains a uniform hereditary arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ controller with exact both-label source-relative cover and physically smaller ordinary SAT children, satisfying simultaneous native-resource bounds $K'\le\max\{11/10,3/5+K/2\}$, $L'\le\max\{1/2,L/2\}$ on the actual FLMV machine. Low-order moment camouflage does not establish hardness of SAT; the earlier affine MCSP theorem remains restricted. P versus NP remains OPEN. Compact report and independent checker:

\textbf{AFFINE-FULLPORT-LEDGER217 (PROVED/EXACT restricted algebra;
COMPUTATIONAL audit; mathematical referee review pending).}
For an authenticated affine equation system, eliminate discarded variables
over $\mathbf F_2$ and retain the \emph{joint} affine projected relation
on the $w$ surviving variables, represented by at most $w$ independent
XOR equations.  An explicit proper-3CNF parity-chain compiler has
$O(w^2\log(w+2))$ output bits, including auxiliary variables, complete
DIMACS headers, and all clauses.  Within this already tractable grammar,
choosing $w=\lfloor n^{1/4}\rfloor$ yields
$N_{\rm child}=O(N^{1/2}\log N)\le N^{3/4}$ for sufficiently large
canonical parents.  Physical coefficient tests show that attempting a
second direct power-short contraction on a small child can fail; the
uniform restricted decider uses a separately charged finite-size
base/fallback instead of asserting a nonexistent size inequality.
The independent reproducer passed 110,276 assertions (320 source
cases, 4,686 exact projection and 4,686 compiled fixed-boundary checks).
The source Library static-boundary audit was rerun byte-for-byte.
Singleton boundary marginals \emph{do not} preserve correlation:
$\{00,11\}$ and $\{01,10\}$ have identical individual supports.
Pure affine projection cannot capture an arbitrary 3-clause (seven
satisfying assignments), and mixed 2CNF-plus-XOR is NP-complete; no
unrestricted semantic contraction follows.  A deliberately conservative
fixed-$k$ implementation has $T_k=O(N^5)$ and $S_k=O(N^2)$,
while its source-inclusive one-tape simulator has
$T_1=O(N^7)$ and $P_1=O(N^2)$, without any RAM or FLMV
lower-bound transfer.  The next target is exact full-correlation
interface discovery for \emph{arbitrary} signed SAT at charged
polynomial total cost.  Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

(PROVED/EXACT restricted; independently rerun).}
For $r$ recognized Boolean guards, first-true case expansion yields at most

$(7/4)^r$ affine pieces in that representation. This is an
architecture-specific representation lower bound: the same function has a
linear CNF description and is trivially satisfiable. Hence it neither proves
general SAT hardness nor supplies arbitrary-instance affine decomposition.
The 2,587-check audit passes; unrestricted acquisition remains \OPEN.

G_F=\bigwedge_i(\neg y\vee\ell_{i1}\vee u_i)

\textbf{PROJECTED-EXCESS-SCC222 (PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
For an explicitly encoded system $F=E\land B$ with affine GF$(2)$
equations $E$ and signed width-at-most-two clauses $B$, parse and
Gaussian-eliminate the affine-incidence components, including singletons.
If $x_{C_i}=a_i+L_i z_i$, let $P_i$ contain \emph{all} $B$-mentioned
variables in $C_i$, including internal binary clauses, and define
$r_i=\operatorname{rank}(\pi_{P_i}L_i)$ and
$e_i=\max(r_i-1,0)$. Partition the graph of binary interactions between
affine components into connected clusters $D_j$ and put
$K_j=\sum_{i\in D_j}e_i$. A uniform source-authenticated solver
branches on $r_i-1$ exposed coordinates in each component and leaves
at most one Boolean quotient bit per component. Every residual $B$
is then 2CNF, so paid SCC decisions and witness reconstruction give
$\operatorname{poly}(N)(1+\sum_j 2^{K_j})$ time and polynomial
peak workspace. This strictly improves the \emph{bound for this
explicit algorithm} over branching on full affine component nullity,
and complements the LSPV-3 projected-rank/treewidth table bounds.
A dense complete-interaction family with $q$ components and
$h=q^2$ private pairs per component has old excess $q^3$,
exposed excess zero, and a directly materialized ordinary 2CNF child
of physical length $O(N^{2/3}\operatorname{polylog}N)
\le N^{3/4}$ eventually, with a closed restricted-base decision.
The same family is not strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF, and the
construction does not contract arbitrary SAT. An internal-clause
counterexample shows that ignoring binary constraints inside affine
components is unsound. Conservatively, for
$R=1+\sum_j2^{K_j}$, fixed two-tape sequential-input
construction time is $O(RN^6)$ with $O(N^2)$ workspace;
one-tape simulation gives $O(RN^8)$ time and $O(N^2)$
source-inclusive footprint, with no exact fixed-UTM exponent claimed.
The independent Python audit passed 6,659 assertions; the
original connected-interface audit reproduced byte-for-byte.
Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ follows. LSPV-1/LSPV-5
handoff was published and verified.
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29_0607_LSPV-4_PROJECTED_EXCESS_SCC222.tex}.

information theorem; independently refereed).}
For connected proper 3CNFs $F_{t,s}$, four pinned variables define queries
$Q_i$ with $\operatorname{SAT}(F_{t,s}\land Q_i)=s_i$.  A deterministic
source-free state answering a later adversarial $Q_i$ therefore needs at least
$t$ bits on some source.  If the source remains readable, a scan uses only
logarithmic auxiliary space; writable-cell consequences require fixed heads,
finite alphabet and polynomial time.  All unaugmented sources are SAT, so this
is not a decision-only contraction or runtime lower bound.
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29_0933_LSPV-4_FOUR_PIN_ONLINE_CONTEXT226.tex}.

\subsection{LSPV-4 HYBRID-BAD-MESSAGE228: exact exceptional-union interfaces (October 29, 2026)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT on an authenticated restricted module-tree grammar, independent referee pending; unrestricted SAT OPEN.}
Extending LSPV-1 ARC229's exact bijunctive-message closure, at each node $t$ retain non-bijunctive child relations as full explicit separator tables, absorb each exactly certified 2CNF-definable child message into the local binary clauses, and enumerate only
\[
 E_t=S_t\cup\bigcup_{\text{non-bijunctive child }u}S_u,\qquad h_t=|E_t|,
\]
where $S_t$ is the \emph{parent-edge separator}, not the complete full shared-variable interface.
For polynomially authenticated running-intersection trees with once-only source constraint ownership and local affine coefficient rank $e_t$, deterministic exact bottom-up SAT/UNSAT decision and SAT witness reconstruction use conservatively
\[
 D(N)+\operatorname{poly}(N)\sum_t\left[
 2^{h_t}(N_t+1)^{O(e_t+1)}+2^{|S_t|}\operatorname{poly}(|S_t|+1)
 \right]
\]
indexed-RAM bit time, with all failed local routes and full tables charged. Uniformly fixed $e_t$, logarithmic $h_t$ and $|S_t|$, polynomial decomposition discovery and polynomial node count imply polynomial total computation on this \emph{restricted} verified class even when full interfaces are linear. A rank-one star of $q$ equality leaves and $h$ odd-parity leaves has full centre interface $2q+3h$ but exceptional union $3h$; its SAT value is the centre target parity $\beta=h\bmod2$. A two-odd-parity-child overlap has sum of separator sizes six but exceptional union three. When $h$ grows, this particular controller has a potentially exponential branch-mass bound; no algorithm-independent hardness follows. The original fixed-$e$ local algorithm is \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (Arvind--Guruswami IPEC 2021); no new $e=2$ claim is made. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 259 randomized small-tree differential controls, 24 SAT/UNSAT stars, four source-validation failures correctly rejected, 278 exhaustive arity-at-most-three relation recognitions, and overlap controls passed. The local reproducibility bundle contains the extended Python source and raw results; a compact independent JavaScript check and raw results are on GitHub. The four-pin online lower bound remains source-free and contextual, not decision-only SAT hardness. No ARC/FLMV forbidden-region transition or strict exact-$(2,2)$ universal decomposition coverage has been proved.
Report: \texttt{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29\_1336\_LSPV-4\_HYBRID\_BAD\_MESSAGE228.tex}.
Next: prove uniform polynomial discovery and strict22 coverage with bounded $e_t$, $|S_t|=O(\log N)$ and $h_t=O(\log N)$, or exhibit a restricted structural counterfamily.

\paragraph{LSPV-4, HEAD-WINDOW-INFO-VALUE236 (29 October 2026, 18:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independently reviewed here.}
For the declared one-read-only-sequential-head source model, a source-independent
prepared head position, \(L\) complete retained noninput bits and \(t\)
independent sign fields spaced by \(g\), the exact optimum worst future
singleton-context head movement is
\[
 H_{\min}=\max\{0,\lceil g(t-L-1)/2\rceil\}.
\]
The proof injects each source into retained state plus the radius-\(H\) source
window; storing \(L\) signs and centering the head on the remainder attains the
bound.  A source-dependent initial head position must have its address charged.

not a total SAT-time or RAM lower bound.  Report:

\paragraph{LSPV-4, BATCH-SWEEP-VALUE237 (29 October 2026, 19:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; independent referee pending.}
For a public read-only serial source with $t$ independent sign bits at spacing $g$,
one fixed source-independent prepared head position, and a complete $L$-bit
persistent state, a deterministic non-resetting \emph{batch} continuation
reconstructing every four-pin INDEX-style SAT-context answer has exact worst-case
post-head-travel frontier
$H_{\mathrm{post}}^\star=g\max(0,t-L-1)$.
The same fully charged prefix strategy attains this bound while every exact
preparation-plus-continuation execution has total input-head movement at least
$g(t-1)$, also attained. This batch theorem is distinct from the radius-half
single-query bound in HEAD-WINDOW236 and does not transfer to unrestricted
SAT decision time, source-rewriting machines, or indexed RAM.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} A claim that reading one bit decreases
$\Phi_W(K)=\log_2|\pi_W K|$ by at most one \emph{pointwise}.
The Horn 2CNF $\bigwedge_{j=2}^{t}(x_1\lor\neg x_j)$ has
$2^{t-1}+1$ models; the rare observed $(x_1=0)\mid_{v}$ lowers
logarithmic support by $\log_2(2^{t-1}+1)>t-1$, whereas expected
improvement under the uniform model prior is binary entropy $\le1$.
These are contextual knowledge scores, not general lower bounds on steps.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 3068 exhaustive prefix-trajectory checks,
48 frontier rows, and 12 Horn-skew rows.
\textbf{OPEN:} immediate-response adaptive sequence frontier with cache/write
costs, general SAT, and P versus NP.

\textbf{ONEWAY-RANK-WINDOW238 (PROVED/EXACT, restricted source-present
single-query interface; independent referee pending).}
Let $a\in\mathbb F_2^t$ be encoded as independent signs spaced $g$
physical source cells; let a future selected parity query belong to a
public family $W$.  Before the query, an arbitrary deterministic preprocessor
leaves a complete $L$-bit source-dependent state and a source-independent
initial head position.  With one unchanged read-only source head moving
only right by at most $H$, put $r=\min(t,1+\lfloor H/g\rfloor)$.
The exact minimum summary length, including nonlinear preparations, is
\[
 L^*_W(H)=\min_{0\le u\le t-r}
 \operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb F_2}
 (W_{[t]\setminus\{u,\ldots,u+r-1\}}).
\]
Necessity is the $2^{\operatorname{rank}}$-response count on source
vectors agreeing inside the reachable window; sufficiency stores a basis
of projected parity masks and performs a charged one-way source sweep.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} target answer rank alone determines
physical continuation cost.  Rank-one sparse and dense parity contexts
with $L=0$ require respectively $0$ and $g(t-1)$ head movements.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 150 seeded mask families, 54,856 exhaustive
source/query answers, 256 nonlinear one-bit summary maps, and writable
source-compaction controls.  Source retention, public query-description
length, Gaussian elimination, input-head repositioning, peak writable
state and construction time are charged. The theorem is not an
unrestricted SAT lower bound, does not transfer to RAM or writable
source, and leaves P versus NP \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 STRICT22-CUBE-SPECTRUM239 (29 October 2026, 21:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; independent referee pending.}
For the preexisting connected proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
cyclic source $F_r$ of $3r$ variables and $4r$ clauses, exact transfer
matrices establish
\[
\#\mathrm{SAT}(F_r)=5^r+1,\qquad
Q_j(F_r)=\frac{5^{r-1}-1}{2}\quad\text{for all source }j.
\]
Every strict exact22 source has exactly $P=4n=3m$ raw opposing
source-occurrence pairs and first-generation non-tautological
resolvents of width at most four.  In $F_r$, of $12r$ raw
pairs, $10r$ are tautological and $2r$ are distinct non-merge
four-literal resolvents. Their unrestricted forbidden-cube union
has exact density $1-(\lambda_+^r+\lambda_-^r+1)/4^r\to1$,
$\lambda_\pm=(3\pm\sqrt{17})/2$, even though
their naive summed weight is $r/8$.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} distinct-resolvent cube coverage
guarantees marginal unit-propagation work savings.  Every derived
clause on this family is non-merge and UP redundant with parents;
the source has explicit polynomial-time SAT witness $u_i=1,v_i=0$.
For any full assignment to strict22, $N_2-N_1=-3D$,
$E+3|D|\le m$, and the cheaper full-complement violation count
is $(E-|D|)/2$.  These are diagnostics, not an answer oracle.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} an independently executed GitHub JavaScript
checker verified 37,376 complete assignments and all 19,683
$r=3$ partial UP states; separately run Python checked 800 further
seeded partial states, SAT/UNSAT controls, and same-run
UP/eager/failed-literal/2SAT-Horn-XOR-aware portfolio cost and
peak-space ledgers.  The Python selected-work proxy is not an
exact Turing-machine step count.  \textbf{OPEN:} all-input
adaptive SAT search; P versus NP.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 SPARSE-EXTREME-SLACK244 (30 October 2026, 01:40 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks; general complexity OPEN.}
For a proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF, exactly two same-polarity and four opposite-polarity source-occurrence pair events arise per original variable. Therefore only at most $6n$ original clause pairs have a nondefault local $SS,SU,US,UU$ extremal compatibility mode. A sorted sparse source-index table encodes the complete four-mode relation in $O(n\log m)$ bits and can be compiled in $O(N+n\log n)$ indexed word-RAM operations, in contrast to an \emph{optional} $4\binom m2$-bit explicit dense matrix. Actual connected strict22 $H_{200}$ has $n=1200,m=1600$, exactly 5200 nondefault pairs; dense-mode bitpacked payload 5,116,800 bits versus a conservative sparse record payload 124,800 bits. These are implementation-specific representation costs, not all-algorithm SAT resource lower bounds.

For coherent original-variable pinning $\rho$ fixing $k$ coordinates, at a nonconflicting original-source unit-propagation fixed point define $Z$ uncovered original clauses, $H_2,H_3$ original clauses with respectively exactly two and three pinned-TRUE occurrences, $w$ uncovered residual ternary clauses, and $e$ free occurrence count in already-satisfied clauses. The exact \emph{signed-occurrence slack} $d=m/2-H_2-H_3$ satisfies $\boxed{2d=w+e}$. Selecting one free variable per uncovered ternary clause yields a certified strong 2SAT backdoor of size $h\le w\le 2d$, with polynomial acquisition and charged $2^h$ leaf search, no universal polynomial bound. On the previously verified connected SAT family $H_r$, pinning the two original variables $x_{j,5}=x_{j,6}=0$ in every block gives a source-UP-saturated UNSAT partial cube with $d=4r$, $w=0$, $e=8r$. Nevertheless a non-tautological original-parent resolution DAG with at most five steps proves the width-at-most-four source clause
$R_j=x_{j,5}\lor x_{j,6}\lor x_{j-1,5}\lor x_{j-1,6}$; its falsification excludes this entire original-variable cube. Thus linear signed-occurrence slack does not force large proof or search work, even on connected strict22 SAT sources. This is an explicit restricted falsification of any such intrinsic-cost proposal.

Full original-source-indexed local SAT/UNSAT targets and inversions, globally coherent endpoints and flips, proof provenance, W1/S15/CUBE9/XOR/Horn controls, and simultaneous same-source baseline/prepass ledgers are reported in
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-30_0140_LSPV-4_SPARSE_EXTREME_SLACK244_PUBLIC.tex},
with independent compact checker
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/LSPV4_SPARSE_EXTREME_SLACK244_independent_check.js}
and readback-verified machine-readable results
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/LSPV4_SPARSE_EXTREME_SLACK244_public_results.json}.
Finite selected-operation comparisons did not demonstrate a net time or peak-space advantage, and proof assistant/referee review is pending. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\textbf{CONFLICT-CONSENSUS-TWIN245 (PROVED/EXACT restricted twin; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks; independent referee pending).}
The new original-source-indexed experiment distinguishes the local all-literals-TRUE $S_j$ and all-literals-FALSE $U_j$ occurrence targets, their inversions, four-way source-clause extreme compatibility, and actual globally coherent endpoints, complements and single-use variable flips.
In every strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF all original variables have conflicting local $U_j$ occurrence demands, so the proposed nonconflicting-variable inversion stage makes no progress, although ordinary SAT clauses may all be satisfied.
An explicit 15-variable 20-clause UNSAT original W1 source and a SAT signing obtained by reversing only the two $x_6$ occurrences in original clauses $C_9,C_{13}$ have the same unsigned indexed clause supports, signed-degree quotas, endpoint four-class histograms and \emph{aggregate} pair-sign overlap histogram. Both coherent endpoints fail; SAT twin has 54 exact models, including mask 19186. The independent checked W1 original-source UP-DPLL refutation has 76 conflict leaves and 228 unit propagations, with all $2^{15}$ assignments separately exhausted. Repeated disjoint copies provide an infinite paired family with the same coarse diagnostics but opposite SAT/UNSAT answers. The \emph{full indexed} four-way pair matrices differ at four source pairs, so no full-information indistinguishability or SAT lower bound is claimed.
The proposed SAT-key and false-clause-repair single-use coherent walks both fail on the SAT twin, returning UNKNOWN rather than UNSAT, with ordinary proof-carrying DPLL fallback. Fully charged selected-operation work baseline/controller is 16359/23576 for W1 UNSAT, 8075/15217 for its SAT twin, and 310/1824 for connected strict22 H1 SAT; controller memory peaks were also separately measured. These events are neither CPU instructions nor all-machine lower bounds.

\textbf{LSPV-4 NAE-ENERGY-TWIN246 (2026-10-30 02:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary NAE/complement-phase identity; COMPUTATIONAL connected strict22 SAT/UNSAT twin; independently checked, P versus NP OPEN.}
For every proper original 3CNF, \(E_F(a)=N_0(a)+N_3(a)\) counts original clauses violating the signed not-all-equal predicate. Writing \(s_i=2a_i-1\), it has the exact quadratic signed-pair form
\[
E_F(a)=m/4+(1/4)\sum_{i<k}W_{ik}s_i s_k,\qquad
W_{ik}=\sum_{j:\,i,k\in C_j}\sigma_{ij}\sigma_{kj}.
\]
Negating \emph{all three literals} in any selected original source clauses leaves this entire function unchanged for every coherent full assignment; \(E_F(a)\le1\) certifies that at least one of \(a,\bar a\) satisfies the original CNF, but \(E_F(a)\ge2\) gives no UNSAT conclusion.

A verified connected proper duplicate-free strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ UNSAT source W1 (\(n=15,m=20\)) and its SAT signing \(G\), obtained by inverting all literals in zero-indexed original clauses \(T=\{0,1,2,4,7,9,12,14,16,17\}\), retain identical original unsigned clause supports and two positive/two negative occurrences per variable. Within the selected clauses every original variable has one positive and one negative occurrence. Exhaustive coherent assignment checks show \(\#\mathrm{SAT}(W1)=0\), \(\#\mathrm{SAT}(G)=270\), with verified \(G\) witness mask 26; nevertheless \(E_{W1}(a)=E_G(a)\) for \emph{every} one of the \(32768\) assignments and \(\min_a E(a)=2\). Their aggregate four-mode pair histograms coincide, but the \emph{full original-clause indexed} SS/SU/US/UU matrices differ on \(26/190\) pairs, preventing any full-information indistinguishability claim. A source-parent resolvent from \(G\) original clauses \(C_0,C_2\) proves the forbidden global partial cube \(x_1=0,x_3=1,x_{10}=1,x_{13}=0\) by incompatible forced values of \(x_{14}\).

Same-input charged UP-DPLL versus endpoint-\(E\) prepass/fallback versus strictly \(E\)-decreasing pair-indexed walk/fallback takes selected events \(11782/11966/12239\) on W1 UNSAT and \(989/1173/1446\) on \(G\) SAT. The independent W1 76-leaf refutation replay adds 6303 checker events for every complete UNSAT method. Separate same-run Python peak-memory values and all fourteen hostile controls are in the complete raw artifact; selected events are not native machine instructions. This \emph{falsifies E-only SAT/UNSAT decision power} on connected strict22 sources but does not imply any all-algorithm SAT lower bound or P-versus-NP resolution.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 BUCKET-FLIP247 (30 October 2026, 03:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent mathematical referee pending.}
On proper original strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF, keep the original
signed occurrence index and the exact per-clause truth counts
$t_j\in\{0,1,2,3\}$.
The global-variable flip gain $g_i=R_i-B_i$ is the number of FALSE original
clauses repaired minus uniquely-TRUE clauses broken.
Each variable has four original source occurrences, hence
$g_i\in[-4,4]$, so nine intrusive gain buckets select a strictly
improving flip in constant worst-case indexed word-RAM time after
$O(N)$ source preprocessing.
Each flip updates four original clauses, at most 24 gain-contribution
arithmetic events and nine variable bucket entries.
Since each strict flip reduces $N_0$ by at least one, at most $m$ flips
occur, giving $O(N+m)$ \emph{prepass} work; failure returns
UNRESOLVED, never UNSAT.
The full explicit $m$-field truth vector at all $q+1$ assignments costs
$\Omega(m(q+1))$ trace writes, whereas a source-indexed initial vector
and at most four changed clause records per flip use $O(m+q)$ retained
records; re-expansion pays the output cost.
The theoretical constant-time step assumes an indexed word-array assignment;
Python's full arbitrary-precision integer masks are not constant-time native
operations, and the experimental ``delta'' objects retain masks for checks.
The optimal $O(m+q)$ trace omits repeated full masks, recording only the
initial assignment, flipped variable and changed source-clause counts.
No Turing/native time or space bound is inferred from the Python event ledger.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 13 indexed SAT/UNSAT controls,
1,744 independently checked pair extreme modes, 65,664 coherent
complement checks, 768 independent gain checks, and a 7-size product
scaling.  On $r$ independent signed paired-$K_4$ blocks, the
untraced strict-descent controller uses exactly $278r$ selected
source/bookkeeping actions for $2r$ successful flips, while an
expanded source-indexed truth-count log grows quadratically.
The matched $W_1$ strict22 UNSAT baseline/fallback work is
$16{,}238$ versus $16{,}876$ selected events,
with additional source-authenticated contradiction replay common to both.
On planted dense SAT the prepass plus fallback costs $1{,}296$ versus
$2{,}274$ for the declared baseline.
These are selected logical operations, not native/Turing instructions,
and no uniform speedup is proved.  Full source-indexed local $S_j/U_j$
targets and inversions, four-mode $SS,SU,US,UU$ matrices,
$0^n/1^n$, complements, multi-flips, resolvent forbidden cubes,
and proof replay are contained in the persistent reproducibility bundle.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 COMPLEMENT-GRAY248 (30 October 2026, 04:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted explicit-enumeration resource theorem,
independent mathematical referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL full 14-source
audit; P versus NP OPEN.}
For every original proper ternary clause $C_j$ and signed occurrence
$x_{i,j}$, the independently audited local all-literals-TRUE target
$S_j$ and all-literals-FALSE target $U_j$ invert one another; the
four original source-pair compatibility modes $SS,SU,US,UU$ retain
all shared variable identities.  The raw original-source data include
coherent $0^n,1^n$, intermediate one/multiple-variable Gray flips,
per-clause $t_j=0,1,2,3$, complementary four-bucket counts,
source-parent resolution proofs and forbidden partial cubes.
An original proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with $n$
variables has exactly $2^{n-1}$ complete assignment/complement orbits.
Fixing $x_n=0$ and using the standard reflected $(n-1)$-bit Gray
sequence visits one representative from each orbit with precisely
$2^{n-1}-1$ coherent global flips, updating exactly four original
clause truth counters per step.  The full explicit UNSAT traversal
therefore has exactly $4(2^{n-1}-1)$ affected counter writes, with
$O(N+2^{n-1})$ indexed word-RAM selected work and $O(N+n+m)$
untraced words.  Among explicit one-bit paths visiting all orbits
and explicitly maintaining the per-clause counter array, this number
of flips and writes is minimal; it is \emph{not} a lower bound on
arbitrary SAT solvers, which may reason symbolically or prune.
The complete $m$-counter output vector on each representative
costs $\Omega(m2^{n-1})$ output fields even when the maintained
working state is small.  On authenticated strict22 W1 UNSAT
($n=15,m=20$), the quotient traversal visits 16,384 orbits,
uses 16,383 variable flips and 65,532 source-counter updates,
versus 131,068 counter updates on the full unpaired Gray path;
materializing all quotient vectors costs 327,680 fields.
Fourteen coherent source-controlled cases passed 5,988 original
pair-mode checks, 536 triple-mode checks, 336,688
assignment/complement validations and 92 independently replayed
UNSAT UP--DPLL contradiction leaves.  The same-source W1 baseline
DPLL used 151 nodes/15,431 selected solver events, while a fixed
eight-resolvent prepass plus DPLL used 81 nodes/15,170
\emph{fully charged} selected events (including source-mode
construction and resolvent acquisition); independent proof replay
added 11,283 and 8,494 events, respectively.  On H1 SAT the
same prepass reduced nodes 7 to 5 but worsened charged work
288 to 1,400; no uniform speedup is established.  Python
\texttt{tracemalloc} on W1 showed baseline 60,536 allocated peak
bytes, minimal quotient 13,916, quotient retaining the dense
source audit 151,266 and resolution retaining it 214,237.
These are Python allocation snapshots, not Turing-machine space
bounds.  This result complements earlier BUCKET-FLIP247 and
SPARSE-EXTREME-SLACK244 without claiming Gray codes or source
resolution as novel SAT methods.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 NEAR-FULL-TRUE-FPT249 (30 October 2026, 05:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted source-indexed FPT corollary; independent mathematical review pending.}
For every proper original signed 3CNF $F$, define $G^-(F)$ on ORIGINAL clause indices,
with edge $(j,h)$ when local source-target mode SS is incompatible
because one ORIGINAL global variable occurs in opposite polarity.
Every ordinary SAT assignment has its middle-state clause set
$M(a)=\{j:t_j(a)\in\{1,2\}\}$ as a vertex cover of $G^-(F)$.
Source-indexed branching on an uncovered opposite-sign edge,
forcing all nonexceptional original clauses into their all-literals-TRUE
local S mode, and enumerating at most $3k$ remaining original variables,
decides SAT with $|M(a)|\le k$ in $O(N^2+2^kN+k16^k)$
deterministic indexed word-RAM time and $O(N+m^2+k)$ streamed working words.
This is a known Vertex-Cover FPT construction applied to the
known Kullmann clause conflict graph, not unrestricted SAT.
A greedy matching of size $k+1$ gives a source-authenticated budget-NO
certificate; it never alone certifies ordinary UNSAT.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT sharp strict22 firewall:} for every SAT assignment
of a proper signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ ternary source,
$N_0=0$, $\sum_jt_j=3m/2$, and $N_2+2N_3=m/2$, hence
$\boxed{|M(a)|=m-N_3\ge3m/4}$.
Connected strict22 SAT $H_1$ attains equality ($m=8$, four models
with $N=(0,6,0,2)$).
Therefore $k=o(m)$ excludes even SAT witnesses on ALL growing strict22 SAT families,
and failed bounded-middle search is \textbf{NOT} an unrestricted UNSAT proof.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 14 SAT/UNSAT adverse controls;
4,128 original source-clause SS/SU/US/UU modes,
472 exact triple modes, 70,536 coherent assignment/complement checks,
438 signed original occurrences, 57 independently matched budget decisions,
108 source proof checks; all passed, including W1 strict22 UNSAT
and signed-neighbor SAT (168 models).
Selected total work for $k=2$ prepass plus identical UP--DPLL fallback
versus baseline: all-positive SAT $43$ vs $94$;
one-sign-conflict SAT $49$ vs $100$;
$H_1$ SAT $322$ vs $236$;
W1 UNSAT $12290$ vs $12075$;
signed-neighbor SAT $1885$ vs $1670$;
odd-charge $K_4$ Tseitin UNSAT $1196$ vs $952$.
W1 separate source-UP UNSAT proof replay costs $6206$
additional declared checker events on either complete path.
No across-family total-work advantage or unrestricted SAT conclusion.
The mandatory per-occurrence local S/U targets, both inversions,
four indexed pair modes, all coherent $0^n/1^n$ and multi-flip trails,
$N_0,\ldots,N_3$, non-tautological source resolutions, forbidden cubes,
full SAT witnesses and DPLL UNSAT proof leaves were independently checked.
The 343KB GitHub compact raw retains all indexed records;
the larger raw JSON, PDF and code archive are in the persistent Library.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} treating vertex-cover budget NO as formula UNSAT
or assuming strict22 has a sublinear middle-clause SAT parameter.

\paragraph{LSPV-4: FLMV-CHILD-GATE251, 30 October 2026, 10:36 PDT.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (FLMV 2005):} The exact Theorem~5.7 forbidden envelope
is $d_*(c)=\max_{k\ge1}\min\{(1/f_k-c)/k,c^{-k}\}$,
where $f_0=1$ and $f_{k+1}=cf_k/(1+f_k)$.  The exact, infinite-tail-checked
value at $c=241/200$ is $d_*=11642479/23232400$ (attained at $k=2$);
$d_*-1/2=26279/23232400>0$.  The SAT time--space transfer
requires \emph{strictly} $K<c$ and $L<d_*$ on the \emph{same}
uniform deterministic indexed-input random-access Turing machine.
The proposed conditional fixed point $(6/5,1/2)$ is inside this forbidden
region, but no all-input transformer realizing it is known.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted physical-cofactor firewall (independent review
pending).} For a proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with $n$ variables,
$m=4n/3$ original clauses and a declared fixed-slot record of
$B=\Theta(\log n)$ bits, pinning $t$ globally coherent variables
can delete at most $2t$ source clauses.  Thus an ordinary plain-cofactor
child has $N_\rho\ge N-2tB$ bits and obtaining $N_\rho\le N^\lambda$ for
fixed $\lambda<1$ by this mechanism requires
$t\ge m/2-O(N^\lambda/\log N)=\Omega(n)$.
This is a source-specific \emph{compiler} obstruction, not an arbitrary
SAT time or space lower bound.  The earlier short virtual child descriptor
still describes a full-scale ordinary SAT input unless its physical
encoded length actually shrinks.

\textbf{CONDITIONAL resource type-check:} With $N^\gamma$ serial ordinary
children of length $N^\lambda$, $N^\alpha$ charged source/discovery work,
$N^\delta$ \emph{additive} per-child setup, $N^\varepsilon$
\emph{multiplicative} per-guest-step accessor slowdown, and retained-cache
space exponent $\rho$, the justified abstract upper profile is
$K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\delta,\gamma+\varepsilon+\lambda K\}$ and
$L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda,\lambda L,\rho\}$, assuming all other sources
of work and live tapes satisfy the same accounting.  For
$(\alpha,\gamma,\lambda,\sigma)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$, a setup
$\delta=3/5$ preserves formal time fixed point $6/5$, whereas a
per-step exponent $\varepsilon$ raises it to $6/5+2\varepsilon$.
At $c=241/200$ it is necessary for this sufficient recurrence that
$\varepsilon<1/400$; equality is not enough.  Retaining
$N^{3/5}$ disjoint full-sized $N^{1/2}$ children simultaneously would
require $N^{11/10}$ writable bits and is outside the budget.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Nine original-source SAT/UNSAT hostile controls
were checked against coherent clause-indexed $S/U$ target inversions,
four pair modes, $0^n,1^n$, complements, exact partial cofactors
and independently replayed DPLL contradictions.
Matched strict22 W1 UNSAT and its signed-swap SAT control have
$n=15,m=20$, each with 426 encoded source bits and 386-bit
one-pinned-variable children; exact model counts are 0 and 168.
Fully charged selected operations did not improve against DPLL on
these matched sources (W1 11555 versus 12058, signed swap
1505 versus 9642).  These operations are not physical FLMV
machine instructions.  The AFFINE-SMALLBIAS-DESCENT-GATE2
finding remains a \emph{restricted source-independent affine} semantic

\textbf{OPEN:} Uniform hereditary arbitrary-decider

\textbf{ARC-SELECT-INDEX252 (PROVED/EXACT, restricted checkpoint
directory; independent review pending).}
For a virtual child assembled by retaining original source-clause slots
in source order, a single-level block-prefix directory on an arbitrary
retention mask with $m$ slots, block length $h$ and
$w=\lceil\log_2(m+1)\rceil$ requires
$S_{\rm idx}=\lceil m/h\rceil w$ stored bits and, in the worst case,
$Q\ge h-1$ read-only mask-bit probes to answer one exact
$\operatorname{select}$ query. Hence
$S_{\rm idx}(Q+1)\ge mw$. With source size
$N=\Theta(m\log m)$, this restricted architecture cannot satisfy
both $S_{\rm idx}=O(N^{s+o(1)})$ and
$Q=O(N^{\varepsilon+o(1)})$ when $s+\varepsilon<1$.
The current hypothetical FLMV/ARC budget $s=1/2$,
$\varepsilon<1/400$ fails \emph{for this index alone};
no bound on all succinct rank/select indexes or SAT algorithms follows.
A periodic retention mask has zero-probe arithmetic addressing and
falsifies such an overgeneralization.
The original FLMV Theorem 5.7 infinite tail was independently
rechecked with exact fractions: at $c=241/200$, optimal $k=2$
gives $d_*(c)=11642479/23232400$ and a strict
space margin above $1/2$ of $26279/23232400$.
Both-label finite controls preserve original W1 UNSAT (zero
models), signed-swapped strict22 SAT (168 models),
H1 SAT, Horn and XOR SAT/UNSAT; 126 original-source
checkpoint configurations and 987 independently replayed select
queries passed. No physical FLMV machine is implemented; the
Python host materializes bitmap controls and charges are not
native TM step or space measurements.
The still-missing arrow is a \emph{uniform, hereditary,
source-authenticated} arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ with genuinely
power-short ordinary SAT children, paid indexed output-bit
access, selector discovery, failed trials, both-label decisions
and peak writable ancestor space. $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain
\textbf{OPEN}. Report:

\paragraph{LSPV-4, ARC-JOINT-SLOPE-FRONTIER255 (30 October 2026, 13:36 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} the actual Fortnow--Lipton--van Melkebeek--Viglas
Theorem 5.7 time--space envelope, with a certified infinite tail.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted algebraic consequence, independent referee
pending:} for every $6/5\le c\le c_0$, where
$c_0^3+c_0^2-c_0-2=0$ and $c_0\approx1.20556943040059$,
the full original FLMV envelope is
\[
d_*(c)=d_2(c)=\frac{2+c-c^3}{2c^2};
\]
all $k\ne2$ have $d_k(c)<1/2$ throughout this interval, and
$d_k(c)<1/2$ for all $k$ if $c>c_0$.
For a \emph{hypothetically constructed}, uniformly correct, all-input,
hereditary $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ satisfying the \emph{fully charged,
same-machine} recurrence
\[
K'\le\max\left\{\tfrac{11}{10}+\pi,\tfrac35+\delta+\tfrac K2\right\},
\quad
L'\le\max\left\{\tfrac12+\eta,\tfrac L2\right\},
\]
with $\pi,\delta,\eta\ge0$ for additive local work, repeated guest-step
slowdown and simultaneous counted scratch, respectively, its fixed point
lies \emph{strictly} in some actual FLMV forbidden region precisely when
\[
\eta<\tfrac1{90},\qquad
\delta<\frac{c_\eta-6/5}{2},\qquad
\pi<c_\eta-\tfrac{11}{10},\quad
c_\eta^3+(1+2\eta)c_\eta^2-c_\eta-2=0.
\]
\textbf{CONDITIONAL:} this is an algebraic budget, \emph{not} a constructed
compiler or a $P$ versus $NP$ proof.  At $c=241/200$,
$d_*(c)=11642479/23232400$ with optimal $k=2$, and simultaneous
boundary equality fails entry. A full $N$-bit original-source rescan
for each of $N^{3/5}$ children costs $\Omega(N^{8/5})$ and exceeds
this additive-stage budget.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, finite hostile same-source SAT/UNSAT controls:}
twelve signed CNFs, including strict22 W1 UNSAT (0 of $32768$ models)
and signed-swap same-scope SAT (168 models), retain the original
clause-indexed all-TRUE and all-FALSE local targets, both inversions,
SS/SU/US/UU compatibility, true-literal histograms, complements,

NO trees.  The independent checker passed 70,584 assertions, including

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-HEAD-SHADOW-GATE256 (30 October 2026, 14:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted abstract virtual-input head state, independent mathematical review pending:}
for a physical nonempty child ending in a nonblank input bit, the $r+1$
right-of-end input-head coordinates $m,m+1,\ldots,m+r$ are pairwise
distinguishable by at most $r$ ordinary left moves and one read. A post-jump
state-only abstraction that cannot reconstruct discarded addresses requires
$\lceil\log_2(r+1)\rceil$ bits for these coordinates; a saturated coordinate
with $O(\log(m+r))$ abstract bits is exact for all ordinary-move continuations
of length at most $r$ before a new indexed jump.
If the arbitrary guest's input-index tape uses at most $s$ binary positions
and it takes at most $T$ steps, the input-head coordinate has magnitude
at most $2^s+T+1$ and an exact binary shadow has
$O(s+\log T)$ bits. This closes only the \emph{abstract information-space}
accounting, with $s$ already charged to the guest's index tape;
native head-counter update time, arbitrary indexed jumps/erasure and all-tape
same-realization simulation remain \OPEN.
A naive scanner that rereads the entire $s$-bit address each guest ordinary
move can spend $\Theta(Ts)$ selected operations on a far-address alternating
walk while the original guest spends $O(T+s)$; this is a
\emph{restricted implementation falsifier}, not a machine-independent
simulation lower bound.
\textbf{KNOWN FLMV:}
exact rational Theorem~5.7 envelope and certified infinite tail
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, both optimal at $k=2$.
At $(c,d)=(241/200,11642479/23232400)$ the conditional
$(6/5,1/2)$ fixed point has \emph{strict} margins $1/200$ and
$26279/23232400$; a repeated guest-step multiplier needs
$\delta<1/400$ if the remaining hypothetical ARC resource coefficients hold.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
eight original signed-CNF SAT/UNSAT controls retain per-occurrence local
S/U extreme targets and inversions, original SS/SU/US/UU modes, true-literal
histograms, coherent endpoint/complement and multi-flip checks. Authentic
strict22 W1 UNSAT has $0/32768$ models, same-unsigned-support signed-swap
SAT $168/32768$. Main source-indexed checker passed $82{,}653$ assertions;
independent Python checker passed $132{,}796$ bounded-left/right-path and
FLMV/source-label assertions. Charged selected-event baseline versus exact
head wrapper on W1 is $14826/15583$ (151 DPLL nodes unchanged), on W1' SAT
$1737/2494$ (19 nodes unchanged). Additional complete original-source
truth-table checking charges $397554$ W1 and $403099$ W1' literal visits
to either method; these selected-operation counts are \emph{not native
FLMV timings}. The $s=32$, 512-step far-input-head control takes 673
selected guest/incremental events versus 17057 events for a naive
full-address rescanner. No physical power-short SAT child or complete
arbitrary-$A$ compiler is constructed. Restricted MCSP affine failure

\textbf{OPEN:} complete uniform indexed child-head simulator, all-input

These are interface/algorithm restrictions, \emph{not} mandatory costs of
arbitrary SAT algorithms; a selective probe or structured accessor might
avoid either route. LSPV-1 already established the $Q=0$ count, and
the broader permutation-index literature is credited.

\par\noindent\textbf{LSPV-4 ARC-RESPONSE-WIDTH258 (30 October 2026, 16:36 PDT): exact restricted child-response-width firewall, independent mathematical review pending.}
A deterministic one-pass, label-only decoder of $q$ exact child SAT/UNSAT outcomes in a \emph{fixed} prescribed order requires at least
$\lceil\log_2 w_D(k)\rceil$ counted mutable bits at cut $k$, where
$w_D(k)$ is the number of different residual functions induced by the $k$-bit child-label prefixes.
This is the established ordered-BDD residual-function theorem, not new general SAT hardness.
Blocked equality on $q=2h$ independently variable labels has $w_D(h)=2^h$ and demands $h$ bits;
paired equality permits a two-bit stream, while AND/OR/XOR need one bit.
Consequently, storing or irrevocably decoding $N^{3/5}$ independent labels that require $\Omega(N^{3/5})$ state
cannot fit the actual simultaneous FLMV work/index space exponent
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400\approx0.501131$, assuming that restricted interface.
A different order, replay or source reread may avoid this particular obstruction but must be fully charged.
The source-faithful finite original-clause S/U controls, both-label DPLL proof checks and selected-operation ledger
are COMPUTATIONAL only and not native FLMV timings.
The exact FLMV full-envelope optimum is $k=2$ at $6/5$ and $241/200$, with certified $k\ge4$ tail.
The purported all-input arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$, physically power-short ordinary-SAT child,
fully paid decoder-acquisition/guest source/time/index/ancestor space and hereditary closure remain OPEN.
P versus NP remains OPEN. Report:

history, must distinguish $\binom nt2^t$ states. For $t=\Theta(n)$ on a fixed
polynomial-time finite-tape FLMV machine, this demands $\Omega(n)$ counted space,
including ordinary head/index-coordinate information in the configuration audit.
On proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with $N=\Theta(n\log n)$, this is

target. This is NOT a lower bound on arbitrary SAT algorithms, on regenerable
state, or on source-aware decision procedures that never maintain this exact
interface. Each strict22 variable overlaps at most six original unordered
clause pairs, so at most $6n$ of $\binom m2$ original S/U four-mode records
are nontrivial; this sparse combinatorial representation need not be retained
in counted space. An eager full pair matrix and nine-source extreme audit did not
reduce UP-DPLL nodes and increased conservative charged selected work.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} W1 UNSAT has 0 models, same-support signed-swap W1 SAT
has 168; original clause complements, local S/U inversions, four pair extreme

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-COUPLED-CHILD-BUDGET260 (30 October 2026, 18:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted conditional recurrence algebra, independent referee pending; KNOWN FLMV; COMPUTATIONAL nine matched original-source SAT/UNSAT controls.}
For a hypothesized \emph{uniform, hereditary, all-input, both-label-correct}
ordinary-SAT contractor using at most $N^{3/5+\theta}$ serial
\emph{physical} ordinary children of at most $N^{1/2+\xi}$ bits,
with parent local cost $N^{11/10+\pi}$, independent per-child
policy cost $N^\tau$, per-guest-step slowdown $N^\nu$, nonchild
counted scratch $N^{1/2+\eta}$, and a counted child input buffer,
the \emph{conditional} simultaneous native-FLMV exponent recurrences are
\[
K'\le\max\{11/10+\pi,\,3/5+\theta+\tau,\,
              3/5+\theta+\nu+(1/2+\xi)K\},\quad
L'\le\max\{1/2+\eta,\,1/2+\xi,\,(1/2+\xi)L\}.
\]
Writing $\zeta=\max\{\eta,\xi\}$, this \emph{recurrence certificate}
enters some \emph{strict} true FLMV Theorem~5.7 SAT forbidden box
if and only if
\[
\zeta<1/90,\quad 11/10+\pi<c_\zeta,\quad
3/5+\theta+\tau<c_\zeta,\quad
\theta+\nu+c_\zeta\xi<(c_\zeta-6/5)/2,
\quad c_\zeta^3+(1+2\zeta)c_\zeta^2-c_\zeta-2=0.
\]
The exact entire infinite envelope equals
$d_2(c)=(2+c-c^3)/(2c^2)$ on $6/5\le c\le1.20556943\ldots$;
the child-only materialization inflation threshold is the unique
$\xi_*\approx0.0019122778182$ with
$5c_*^3+10c_*^2-11c_*-10=0$ and
$\xi_*=1/2-(3/5)/c_*$. Thus $N^{0.502}$ physical children
\emph{fail this particular recurrence certificate}, even with other
perturbations zero, while $N^{0.501}$ passes. This is not a theorem
against all possible compilers. Full source re-read per
$N^{3/5}$ children costs $\Omega(N^{8/5})$ source-bit accesses.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independently replayed $1{,}313{,}201$
checks across nine original-source SAT/UNSAT formulas, including strict22
W1 UNSAT (0 models) versus its same-support signed-swap SAT (168 models),
original occurrence $S/U$ inversions, $SS/SU/US/UU$ modes, coherent
endpoints, complement true-literal counts, multi-flip trails and an
H1 resolution-certified prohibited cube. Declared selected-logical-event
baseline/two-child UP--DPLL work W1 $10403/10430$, signed-swap
$1315/1298$, H1 $183/175$, XOR3-UNSAT $143/158$; these
are \emph{not native FLMV tape costs}. No physical power-short children

\medskip
\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-COVER-GATE-SCOPE262 (30 October 2026, 20:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted many-one cover-gate reduction, independent referee pending.}
For any proper 3CNF $H=\bigwedge_j(a_j\vee b_j\vee c_j)$, set
$F_H=\bigwedge_j[(y\vee a_j\vee z_j)\wedge(\neg z_j\vee b_j\vee c_j)]$.
Every $F_H$ is SAT by $y=1,z_j=0$, while
$F_H\models y$ if and only if $H$ is UNSAT. Hence even a
single-cube \emph{source-relative semantic cover} test is coNP-complete
on parents promised SAT. This is the known backbone/entailment
phenomenon applied to the LSPV-5 residual-cover gate, not an
unrestricted SAT lower bound. Exact SAT child answers cannot certify an
UNSAT parent without a source-relative cover proof, a complete
syntactic partition, or an exact uncovered-region fallback; witness
termination on the SAT side does not require cover.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent 10-source Python checker passed
135,508 groups; matched original W1/S15 strict22 sources have
$0/861$ models, but both transformed parents are SAT, and their
$y=1$ cover judgments are YES/NO respectively.
A separately executable compact checker passed 2,621,824 local
projection assertions and 7,280 four-way pair-mode checks.
The native FLMV bit-machine transition benchmark remains pending.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM/attribution scope:}
FLMV Theorem 3.4 is a specific linear-nondeterministic-time
multitape-to-SAT encoding, not a statement of the project's
generic external total-child-accessor head-shadow simulator.
The latter needs independent same-machine proof and attribution;
FLMV Theorem 5.7 itself is unaffected.
The exact $k=2$ infinite-tail audit again gives
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL/OPEN:} no uniform hereditary
arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ ordinary SAT contraction exists yet.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN.}
