% BEGIN LSPV-5 OWNED
\paragraph{LSPV-5.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted joint countercontrol) and FALSIFIED as a
general structural-to-runtime implication.}
For every $H\ge2$, one uniformly recognizable connected proper strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ family has both answer classes, $2H^2$ fixed modules, an
$H\times H$ grid minor, and every-order syntactic frontier at least $H/16$.
On its UNSAT member, deleting any one of the first $H^2$ complete modules makes
the remaining source satisfiable; every original-clause UNSAT subset therefore
touches all $H^2$ chain modules.  Nevertheless one fixed deterministic parser
and two-bit relation transfer decides the entire family in polynomial time.
The construction combines the peer grid and source-support controls by adding
universal-relation $E$ modules whose selected negative grid ports are zero in

every boundary witness.  It was checked for $H=2,\ldots,7$ with 133,933
assertions and 139 explicit deleted-module models.

Thus a large syntactic frontier, large source-module support, or their
conjunction does not by itself imply decision time, decision space, proof size,
or semantic-state hardness for arbitrary SAT algorithms.  Source support is
not proof bit length or runtime, and a syntactic grid may disappear under exact
semantic transfer.  The family is a fixed-template promise control and does not
contain arbitrary SAT.  Arbitrary-instance summary acquisition, hereditary
physical re-entry, and a fixed-power single-child SAT shrinker remain \OPEN.
Report:
\texttt{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-28\_2148\_LSPV-5\_JOINT-FRONTIER-SUPPORT-REFEREE189.tex}.
\medskip
\textbf{ONEBIT-REUSE-ENTROPY-REFEREE190 (PROVED/EXACT restricted;
compact-reuse implication FALSIFIED).}
Composing LSPV-4's scalar chain with LSPV-3's one-boundary-bit lift gives a
connected proper-3CNF family whose current exact projection has only two
values, $\mathsf{TOP}$ and $\neg z$.  Yet a single exact no-reread updater under
$h$ authenticated source overwrites has
\[
E(m,h)=1+\sum_{j=0}^{h}\binom mj\quad(h<m),\qquad E(m,h)=2^m\quad(h\ge m)
\]
pairwise distinguishable future behaviors.  In particular, zero sets
$\{i\}$ and $\{j\}$ expose the same current table, while update $(i,1)$
distinguishes them.  Hence a compact current projection is not by itself a
compact reusable authenticated state or a proof of hereditary second-level
closure.  The theorem does not attack static immutable one-child recursion:
source rereading, writable-source repair, or a sparse cache escape the premise
at charged time or space.  The physical fixed-width construction passed
6,953,482 assertions, including 3,476,560 pairwise distinguishing streams.

The supplied Connected Interface Audit was independently rerun byte-for-byte:
4,680 boundary subsets, 7,191 local searches, and 139 truth comparisons pass.
Its static square-root bound and cyclic width-four moving DP are correct under
their declared hypotheses, but imply neither arbitrary-SAT contraction nor a
runtime lower bound.  General moving-summary acquisition, polynomial UNSAT
proof discovery, two-level physical re-entry, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP
remain \OPEN.  Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-28_2248_LSPV-5_ONEBIT-REUSE-ENTROPY-REFEREE190.tex}.
\medskip
\textbf{PROJECTION-IDEMPOTENCE-REFEREE191 (PROVED/EXACT restricted;
repeated-contraction implication FALSIFIED).}
For a 2CNF component $C(X,B)$ and affine GF$(2)$ component $A(Y,B)$ with
disjoint private supports and every shared name retained in $B$, private
existential quantifiers distribute exactly.  Canonical binary-resolution and
row-echelon projection onto the fixed boundary is then idempotent after the
first call.  The smallest unsafe shared-drop control is $(z)$ together with
$z=0$: the joint empty-boundary projection is false, whereas separate
component projections are both true.

An unbounded two-answer family makes the failure hereditary.  The positive
cycle $\bigwedge_i(z_i\vee z_{i+1})$ combined with singleton affine rows
$z_i=1$ is SAT, while rows $z_i=0$ make it UNSAT.  Both use the same compiler,
every $z_i$ is shared, padded parents shrink once, and the next exact child is
identical.  At $b=64$ the explicit byte ledger is
$6019\to1387\to1387$; unsafe separate deletion falsely returns SAT on the
UNSAT member.  The dependency-free audit passed 143,026 assertions across
480 random exact-relation cases and padded controls through $b=64$.
This constrains the declared compiler, not every SAT algorithm: a charged joint
compatibility algorithm can escape, while unrestricted mixed 2CNF/XOR is
NP-complete by ARC213.  A uniformly discovered moving boundary, recursively
closed physical $N^\lambda$ child, polynomial-value rank, ARC-EDGE-90 and
P versus NP remain \OPEN.  Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-28_2348_LSPV-5_PROJECTION-IDEMPOTENCE-REFEREE191.tex}.

\medskip
\textbf{MARGINAL-PARITY-FIREWALL192 (PROVED/EXACT restricted;
strict-marginal reconstruction implication FALSIFIED).}
For every $b\ge2$, proper 3CNF Tseitin chains of $O(b)$ size encode the
even and odd parity relations on the same $b$ boundary variables.  For
every strict subset $S\subsetneq[b]$, both projections onto $S$ are the
full relation, while the two complete boundary relations are disjoint.
Putting the two chains on disjoint auxiliaries and identifying their
boundary therefore yields an UNSAT conjunction.  Hence no exact
interface compiler whose state consists only of strict boundary
marginals---even all of them---can preserve arbitrary conjunction:
the missing joint correlation is decisive.

This is not a general hardness result.  Gaussian elimination stores the
complete affine constraint compactly and decides the joint parity
control in polynomial time.  A separate puncture reduction shows why
the fixed-template profile does not generalize for free: for an arbitrary
proper 3CNF source $F$, one profile entry can be made present exactly
when $F$ is satisfiable, so entry membership is NP-complete and absence
is coNP-complete under the authenticated-module encoding.  The
dependency-free reproducer passed 90,643 assertions, evaluating 65,814
source truth tables and 1,050,698 source assignments; it checked all
strict projections for $b=2,\ldots,7$ and joint UNSAT through $b=5$.
Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0048_LSPV-5_MARGINAL-PARITY-FIREWALL192.tex}.
An unconditional arbitrary-SAT complete-summary constructor, effective
moving-interface discovery, recursive physical power shrink,
ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN.

\medskip
\textbf{TRUTH-TABLE-DETACHMENT193 (PROVED/EXACT restricted; generalized
SAT hardness transfer FALSIFIED).}
A connected proper-3CNF family contains all \(2^{2^b}\) possible
relations on a designated \(b\)-variable boundary, with every source
exactly \(N_b=\Theta(b^2 2^b)\) physical bits and independent table
sign choices. Its exact clauses/variables are
\[
 g_b=(b+1)2^b-1,\quad
 V_b=b+3(b+2)2^b-1,\quad
 C_b=(5b+9)2^b-1,
\]
and \(N_b=(3W_b+4)C_b+3W_b\), where
\(W_b=\lceil\log_2(\max\{b,V_b,C_b\}+1)\rceil\).
Every source-sign mutation changes exactly four sign bits.
The project already had the abstract all-context relation-counting
floor in LSPV-2 CONTEXT-MEET130; this run realizes it in physical
connected proper-3CNFs. A detached source-free complete decoder of
\emph{every} boundary membership must receive a worst-case
\(2^b=\Omega(N_b/\log^2N_b)\)-bit code, regardless of the target
representation. There is thus no fixed-\(\lambda<1\)
\emph{lossless complete relation} power shrink on this image.

Conversely an unconditional uniform polynomial-time compiler reads
the independently pinned truth-table bits and writes exactly one
same-grammar child with \(b'=\lfloor b/2\rfloor\) and entries

\(s_u=\bigvee_{a\bmod2^{b'}=u}t_a\).
This preserves SAT and UNSAT, is closed under further applications,
and for \(b\ge6\) has bit length
\(N_{b'}\le N_b^{3/4}\), with two above-threshold levels for
\(b\ge12\). It \emph{forgets} old boundary correlations:
different tables with a single one at indices 0 and 2
have the same projected child when \(b=2\).
Thus its constructive success is for an easy \emph{decision} family,
not arbitrary SAT or all-context recursive joins.
The image is proper 3CNF, not strict signed exact-$(2,2)$.

The executable passed \textbf{6,294} deterministic assertion groups,
72 independent generic-DPLL pinned checks, 12 literal-sign mutation
checks, and 25 physical shrink recurrence controls, with seed
20261029. Exact program and JSON readback Git blob IDs match the
locally tested files. The prior OpenAI quantum skeleton's
target-dependent Boolean oracle remains a nontransferable analogy.

Report:

\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0110_TRUTH_TABLE_DETACHMENT193_raw.json}.
Uniform arbitrary-SAT context-correct acquisition, strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ transfer, all-input decision shrink, matched ARC--FLMV
contradiction and \(P\) versus \(NP\) remain \OPEN.

\medskip
\textbf{TREE-HOLONOMY-REFEREE194 (PROVED/EXACT restricted; unary-coherence
shortcut FALSIFIED).}
Complete Boolean two-port relations compose associatively. Their Boolean
matrix product is the exact endpoint relation on a path, and a cycle is
satisfiable exactly when the product has a diagonal entry. On a tree,
arc consistency with nonempty domains is complete. In contrast, equality,
equality and inequality around a three-variable cycle leave both values in
every unary domain but are globally UNSAT. Thus unary marginals or an AC-3
fixed point do not authenticate cyclic joins; complete correlation does.

Consecutive complete relations can be grouped into one recursively closed
$O(\sqrt{k})$ child in the supplied relation-chain grammar, giving a genuine
restricted power shrink. This is not yet an ordinary-SAT child: exact
relations are assumed extensionally supplied. Acquiring even one row from an
arbitrary hidden CNF module can encode SAT/coSAT, and no unbounded strict
exact-$(2,2)$ two-port source compiler with charged discovery and physical
re-entry is known. The independent audit passed 50,002 assertions; the
Connected Interface archive and new LSPV-2/LSPV-3 peer programs also reran
successfully with their stated restrictions. Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0148_LSPV-5_TREE-HOLONOMY-REFEREE194.tex}.
Arbitrary-SAT contraction, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN.

\medskip
\textbf{PORT-SATURATION-REFEREE195 (PROVED/EXACT firewall;
COMPUTATIONAL falsifier; corrected restricted theorem).}
Identifying one port variable from each of two disjoint strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ modules while retaining all clauses gives the common variable
four positive and four negative occurrences. Adding clauses or complementing
the name cannot restore exact-$(2,2)$ balance; any physical strict-exact
composition must delete, replace, or reroute source occurrences.

That necessary repair is not semantically inert. In the canonical
15-variable connected strict exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT source, swapping two
same-sign literal occurrences preserves every variable's signed occurrence
count, properness, and connectedness, yet the explicit swap
$\neg x_1\leftrightarrow\neg x_8$ between the first two clauses produces the
SAT witness with true set $\{6,7,8,10\}$. Exhaustive enumeration checks all
32,768 assignments: among 840 same-sign candidates, 669 swaps remain valid and
connected, and 643 become SAT. By contrast all 15 variable-wide gauge flips
remain UNSAT, separating the valid gauge theorem from an invalid local-rewire
inference.

A corrected cyclic puncture theorem is exact after the complete two-bit
profiles are supplied: Boolean $2\times2$ transition matrices compose and the
cycle is SAT exactly when the product has a true diagonal. But profile
acquisition, occurrence-balanced physical re-entry, and arbitrary-instance
coverage remain unproved. The OpenAI target-dependent oracle similarly
supplies no classical table evaluator. Machine ledgers remain separated:
these are RAM-level exhaustive controls, not single-tape, two-tape, or fixed
UTM runtime lower bounds. See
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0248_LSPV-5_PORT-SATURATION-REFEREE195.tex}.
P versus NP and ARC-EDGE-90 remain \OPEN.

\medskip
\textbf{OPEN-PORT-DEFICIT-REFEREE196 (PROVED/EXACT correction;
COMPUTATIONAL smallest-case obstruction).}
A closed strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ port has zero residual signed occurrence
capacity: it already appears twice with each sign. Hence the new fixed
equality/XOR gadgets are valid closed relation realizations, but they cannot be
conjoined to arbitrary source occurrences as dangling ports. A composable
object must instead be a partial formula with explicit signed deficits, or use
an authenticated occurrence-replacement theorem with complete puncture
semantics. Signed-count preservation alone is insufficient by report195's
UNSAT-to-SAT swap.

For a two-port equality gadget reserving one signed external occurrence at
each endpoint and saturating all private variables, occurrence arithmetic
forces $4r+6=3m$ and $r\equiv0\pmod3$. Dependency-free exact enumeration of
the smallest case $r=3,m=6$ considered all 29,196 degree-feasible clause sets
for each of four endpoint-sign patterns and all 32 assignments per set. None
projects to equality. This finite result does not exclude the next admissible
$r=6$ case and gives no SAT runtime lower bound.

LSPV-4's constant-port theorem independently shows that a tiny exact relation
may be NP-hard to acquire, but its high-degree guard is not exact22; the closed
wire does not regularize it for free. Separate RAM, fixed-$k$, single-tape and
fixed-UTM ledgers are retained, with the conservative single-tape construction
used only as an upper simulation. Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0348_LSPV-5_OPEN-PORT-DEFICIT-REFEREE196.tex}.
Free dangling deficits, arbitrary-3CNF entry into the exact22 class,
recursive physical shrink, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN.
Occurrence-replacement cofactor preservation and fanout \emph{inside an
already exact22 source} are closed by report223 and referee198 below.

\medskip
\textbf{TRIANGULAR-SPLICE-IDEMPOTENCE-REFEREE197
(PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls; repeated-regulator
mechanism FALSIFIED).}
The negative-occurrence splice222 has exact relation
\[
R=\begin{pmatrix}H_0&Q_0\\0&H_1\end{pmatrix},\qquad Q_0\Rightarrow H_0.
\]
If the same operation is repeated at the transported clause, Boolean relation
composition gives $R^2=R$ and induction gives the same endpoint relation at
every depth. Thus the unwanted $Q_0$ fiber is never removed. Every physical
level adds a fresh fixed module, so this architecture grows rather than
contracts the ordinary-CNF encoding.

The dependency-free control checks the fixed equality module and explicit
affine implication source through three levels and all eight three-variable
strict exact-$(2,2)$ sources through two levels. Equality stays equality;
implication stays implication; all levels remain connected strict
exact-$(2,2)$ and their bit lengths increase. This is a restricted
architecture theorem, not a general SAT lower bound. Independent feet,
positive or mixed-sign splices, arbitrary profile acquisition, recursive
physical shrink, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN. Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0448_LSPV-5_TRIANGULAR-SPLICE-IDEMPOTENCE-REFEREE197.tex}.

\medskip
\textbf{TWO-SWAP-FANOUT-REFEREE198
(PROVED/EXACT under fixed source-indexed guard certificates;
COMPUTATIONAL replay; contraction FALSIFIED).}
Four explicit source-indexed Resolution refutations prove the fixed punctured
guard implications needed by LSPV-2 report223: the forbidden branches use
\((39,23),(55,36),(48,31),(90,71)\) total/derived proof rows.  Consequently,
for every connected proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ source \(H\) and source
variable \(x\), the special positive-and-negative occurrence replacement
satisfies pointwise
\[
\exists Z\,\mathcal T(H,x,p)=H\wedge(p\leftrightarrow x).
\]
Induction at the transported \(x\)-occurrences gives arbitrary finite equality
fanout
\[
\exists Z\,\mathcal F_r(H,x;p_1,\ldots,p_r)
=H\wedge\bigwedge_{j=1}^r(p_j\leftrightarrow x).
\]
This retires \emph{fanout is open} only in the occurrence-replacement sense
inside the already exact22 class.  It does not expose a dangling free port or
compile arbitrary 3CNF.  Each copy adds 15 variables and 20 clauses; all ten
depth-three hostile controls strictly grow (for example,
\(1808\to3808\to5808\to7808\) bits).  The Connected Interface audit again
reproduced byte-for-byte, and the selector identity again passed exactly
69,904 projected assignments.  The proof generator/checker is project code,
not a proof-assistant kernel.  Report, reproducer, and raw data:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0548_LSPV-5_TWO-SWAP-FANOUT-REFEREE198.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0548_TWO-SWAP-FANOUT-REFEREE198_repro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-29_0548_TWO-SWAP-FANOUT-REFEREE198_raw.json}.
Canonical ordinary-3CNF entry is now supplied by compiler224, but uniform
semantic forgetting, recursive physical power shrink, ARC-EDGE-90, and P versus NP remain \OPEN.


\medskip
\textbf{ENTRY-FANOUT-MONOTONE-REFEREE199
(PROVED/RESTRICTED entry; COMPUTATIONAL replay; contraction mechanism
FALSIFIED).}
Independent review supports CONNECTED-ALLPORT-COMPILER224, relative to the
exhaustively enumerated fixed \(Q\) certificate, as a pointwise compiler from
canonical ordinary width-\(0\)--\(3\) CNF into connected proper strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF:
\[
  \forall\xi\in\{0,1\}^n,\qquad
  F(\xi)\Longleftrightarrow\exists Z\,C(F)(\xi,Z).
\]
It preserves every original variable as a physical named port and makes no
SAT call. This closes arbitrary canonical entry/regularization; it does not
close semantic contraction. For connected exact22 input with \(n\) variables,
the compiler outputs exactly \(12n\) variables and \(16n\) clauses. Each
authenticated equality fanout adds 15 variables and 20 clauses, and renaming
preserves counts. Hence every composition of these components has
nondecreasing physical-port rank. Retaining \(n\) distinct strict-exact22
ports also costs
\[
  32\sum_{j=1}^{n}(1+\lfloor\log_{10}j\rfloor)=\Omega(n\log n)
\]
ASCII bits, so this all-port architecture cannot be an \(N^\lambda\) child.

The new cross-composition audit passed 55 assertions. On the 30-variable
parity ring, one compiler application gives 360 variables/480 clauses and a
second gives 4320 variables/5760 clauses; measured bits grow
\(56{,}176\to817{,}184\). Connected Interface (4,680/7,191/139),
compiler224 (including 69,904 selector checks), parity-ring160 and
projected-excess-SCC222 (6,659 checks) all replayed at their stated restricted
scopes. The latter supplies a genuine \(N^{3/4}\) single-child shrinker on a
recognizable mixed affine--2CNF grammar, showing the all-port floor is
architectural rather than a general SAT lower bound.

Report, reproducer and raw data:

Uniform polynomial discovery of a complete joint forgetful summary for
arbitrary SAT, physical power shrink, recursive closure, ARC-EDGE-90 and

\medskip
\textbf{LOCAL-PORT-HIERARCHY-REFEREE200
(PROVED/EXACT architectural floor; COMPUTATIONAL replay;
log-depth contraction mechanism FALSIFIED/RETIRED).}
Independent review reproduced the Connected Interface audit
(4,680/7,191/139), PORT-QUANTIFIER-CLOSURE161's 81 finite templates and
700 signed-token permutations (171 SAT, 529 UNSAT), and
TERNARY-CLAUSE-PORT162's 455 source triples, 104 full-CNF cofactors and
18-step authenticated Resolution replay.  The reports are compatible:
closure161 retains only the designated one-port family and produces a
same-order $2$CNF; clause162 retains $(x_1,x_2,x_{13})$ and exposes the
non-bijunctive relation $\neg x_1\vee\neg x_2\vee x_{13}$ on a recognizable
easy chain.

If a final child physically retains $m$ nonempty leaves and every merge block
of a rooted hierarchy with fanin at most $r$, edge counting gives
\[
 I\ge\left\lceil\frac{m-1}{r-1}\right\rceil.
\]
Thus the child has $\Omega(m)$ blocks; for canonical
$N=\Theta(m\log m)$ it cannot be $O(N^\lambda)$ for fixed $\lambda<1$.
This theorem does not cover a compiler that computes exact parent summaries
and discards its children.  That is the constructive escape, but at empty
boundary the complete summary is $\{()\}$ exactly for SAT and empty exactly
for UNSAT, so its uniform acquisition is already the decision problem and
cannot be left uncharged.  The classical selector elimination identity again
passed all 69,904 assignments; its compact circuit has $4t-1$ gates, linear
rather than quadratic but not a power shrink.

Report, reproducer and raw data:

Uniform arbitrary-instance complete-summary acquisition, recursive physical

\OPEN.


\medskip
\textbf{PRODUCT-SHARING-FIREWALL201
(PROVED/EXACT explicit pairwise-sharing floor; COMPUTATIONAL controls;
free occurrence identification FALSIFIED/RETIRED).}
For a proper 3CNF $F$ with $m$ clauses, $n$ active variables and occurrence
degrees $d_v$, replacing every literal occurrence by a fresh name produces
$m$ disjoint nonempty ternary clause relations and exactly $7^m$ satisfying
assignments, regardless of whether $F$ is SAT. Restoring semantics solely by
pairwise equalities requires the equality graph on the $d_v$ copies of every
variable to be connected, hence a tight
\[
  \sum_v(d_v-1)=3m-n
\]
links. Equality spanning trees attain the count and recover $F$ pointwise.
The eight-clause full-cube UNSAT control becomes a $7^8=5{,}764{,}801$-model
product and needs 21 links. A naïve report163/report198 additive ledger gives 435 variables and 580
clauses on this control, but report198 fans out an existing source variable
and does not directly join two independently saturated ports; that exact22
composition remains \OPEN. The reproducer passed 800 deterministic random
proper-3CNF identities and 27,680 source assignments.

This is a representation firewall for explicit occurrence copies and
pairwise equality, not a SAT runtime lower bound. Higher-arity consistency,
shared-input circuits, algebraic elimination, semantic preprocessing and
history-free summaries may escape, but their acquisition and re-entry remain
chargeable. Report, reproducer and raw data:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_0848_MAIN_PRODUCT-SHARING-FIREWALL201.tex},
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_0848_PRODUCT_SHARING_FIREWALL201_repro.py},
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_0848_PRODUCT_SHARING_FIREWALL201_raw.json}.
Uniform arbitrary-instance compact sharing, effective oracle synthesis,
recursive physical power shrink, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN.


\medskip
\textbf{DECISION-CONTEXT-SEPARATION202 (PROVED/EXACT restricted firewall;
COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
The same $F_{t,s}$ sources have contextual state complexity $2^t$ but
decision-only state complexity one.  The canonical class consisting of these
sources plus fixed YES/NO bases is polynomially recognizable and closed under
a uniform map sending every source to the fixed YES base; this is a genuine
constant-child strict successor on an easy promise class.  It does not contain
arbitrary SAT.  Consequently any inference from full-port or future-context
information to decision-only contraction cost is retired unless recursive
correctness is proved to force that query contract.  The new reproducer checks
30 source witnesses, 98 contextual instances and all $2^t$ signatures through

\section*{Conclusion}
The constructive and separation tracks meet at the same missing discipline:
an unrestricted transformation must be uniform, correct on both answer
classes, physically encoded, recursively closed, and fully charged on one
realization. Canonical ordinary 3CNF can now be embedded pointwise into
connected strict exact22, and exact equality fanout is available inside that
class. These are transport and regularization results: compiler re-entry
multiplies exact22 variable count by 12 and every fanout is additive. The
remaining constructive arrow is therefore semantic forgetting with complete
joint correctness, effective acquisition, source authentication, physical
re-entry and a polynomial-value descent rank. Restricted affine--2CNF and
truth-table families show that genuine one-child shrinking is possible on
easy closed grammars; static-interface, marginal-parity, saturation and
monotone-rank controls prevent extrapolation to arbitrary SAT. Published
time--space theorems remain valid model-specific barriers, but no all-input
machine-producing separation edge is established. Exact local one-port and ternary-clause summaries sharpen the interface map, but neither supplies arbitrary-instance acquisition. A bounded-fanin hierarchy that retains its leaves and merge blocks is linear-size; deleting them requires the complete forgetful summaries whose charged construction remains the load-bearing step. The product-sharing audit likewise rules out free occurrence splitting: independent clause products lose UNSAT, while explicit pairwise repair is linear. No verified argument currently forces either equality or separation. P versus NP remains open.

\section{LSPV-5 recovery and synthesis update: AFFINE-INTERFACE-REFEREE203}

\paragraph{Recovery provenance (\textbf{PROVED/EXACT}).}
The 138-byte root placeholder was not used as scientific synthesis.  This document was recovered
from the actual full Git source at commit
\texttt{4134a5f49b9aee081b031efe26476e429f0309ee}, blob
\texttt{1fd32df376285390f94bd4c7df4b1924f79d0f53}, which has all five balanced ownership
regions, explicit OPEN status, and the ARC227 reconciliation.  The later verified evidence below
is additive; no manuscript text was reconstructed from \texttt{/tmp/main\_fixed.tex}.

\paragraph{Primary-literature correction (\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM}).}
Arvind--Guruswami, IPEC 2021, Theorems 2 and 17, give a deterministic
$O^*(\binom{n}{\le e})=n^{O(e)}$ algorithm for 2CNF intersected with affine codimension $e$.
Thus every fixed $e$, including $e=2$, is polynomial; $e=O(\log N)$ yields only the
quasipolynomial bound $2^{O(\log^2 N)}$.  Their Theorem 19 is W[1]-hardness parameterized
by $e$, not an unconditional non-FPT statement.  ARC227's one-parity theorem remains a valid
special case, but ``next prove $e=2$'' is retired as stale.

\paragraph{New restricted composition theorem (\textbf{PROVED/EXACT}).}
Given a path of bags whose adjacent interfaces have width at most $w$, with complete constraint
ownership and every bag a 2CNF plus affine codimension at most $e$, exact enumeration of the two
boundary assignments followed by the published local solver and relational path DP decides
SAT/UNSAT and reconstructs a witness in
\[
 \operatorname{poly}(N)\sum_i
 2^{|I_{i-1}|+|I_i|}\binom{|V_i|}{\le e}.
\]
Fixed $e$ and $w=O(\log N)$ therefore give a uniform deterministic polynomial algorithm on this
promised/given-decomposition class.  Discovery is not free.  A connected width-one chain with
one independent parity row per bag has local codimension one and global codimension $k$, so local
$e$ does not bound global $e$; nevertheless the path DP is polynomial.  Report and reproducer:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1203_MAIN_AFFINE-INTERFACE-REFEREE203.tex},
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1203_AFFINE-INTERFACE-REFEREE203_repro.py}, and
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1203_AFFINE-INTERFACE-REFEREE203_raw.json}.

\paragraph{Finite portfolio/restart evidence (\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}).}
Independent reruns reproduced the 49 SAT/1 UNSAT coverage base, 60 SAT mutations, and zero
repair-only hits on the selected 4/8/16/32-block products; component decomposition solves those
products.  The four former restart gaps are SAT and direct deeper branching solves them in
22--46 nodes.  The recovered 312-case portfolio contains only 44 strict22 roots.  These results
falsify the concrete repair-only cover and restart-superiority claim on the tested cases; they
do not prove or refute general polynomial coverage.  A finite action vocabulary still permits
$K^d$ depth-$d$ policies, so discovery, every losing prefix, joins, intermediate encoded size,
and peak space must be charged.

\paragraph{Ranked open obligations.}
\begin{enumerate}
\item Uniformly discover and certify, on an all-input hereditary strict22 class, fixed local
affine codimension and logarithmic complete interfaces; charge all failed decompositions.
\item Prove descendant closure, source authentication, and reconstruction on both answer
classes for one fixed controller with no answer-class bit.
\item Bound the policy family, total losing work, all restarts, and simultaneous peak space by
one polynomial.
\item Establish any ARC/FLMV implication only after construction, acquisition, encoded size,
and same-machine simultaneous time/space are charged.
\end{enumerate}
Online-context, one-/two-tape transport, proof-acquisition, and global-rank floors remain
restricted supporting results unless an arbitrary-algorithm bridge is proved.
Both P=NP and P$\ne$NP remain \textbf{OPEN}.  The next specific lemma is a uniformly
constructible path decomposition for a precisely defined hereditary connected strict22
subclass with fixed local $e$ and $O(\log N)$ complete interfaces.

\paragraph{Accumulated-interface firewall (\textbf{PROVED/EXACT}).}
For every $q$, a star with centre row

system is UNSAT; deleting any leaf restores SAT.  Thus every child message is essential and
per-edge width cannot replace full-interface width in raw table enumeration.  Gaussian
elimination solves the family in polynomial time, so this is an accounting falsifier, not a
SAT lower bound.

\section{LSPV-5 synthesis update: AFFINE-MESSAGE-RANK-REFEREE205}

\paragraph{Three exact restricted message regimes.}
The current corpus supports three nonexclusive fully charged routes on
authenticated running-intersection trees:
\begin{enumerate}
\item compile exactly bijunctive child messages into parent 2CNF (ARC229);
\item compile exactly affine child messages into parent equations and charge
the combined embedded rank $E_t$ (AFFINE-MESSAGE-RANK-REFEREE205);
\item retain every remaining exact relation as a full table and charge the
union of its separator variables (HYBRID-BAD-MESSAGE228).
\end{enumerate}
The second route is polynomial for logarithmic parent separators and uniformly
bounded combined rank.  Constant rank per child does not suffice: the
width-three parity star has aggregate rank $h$.  Conversely, duplicate affine
messages can have total edge-width sum $3h$ and combined rank one.  Rank,
union width and message count are therefore genuinely different parameters.

\paragraph{Ranked load-bearing obligations.}

\item Prove polynomial discovery and coverage for a hereditary connected
strict22 class in which every exact child message falls into a certified
regime above and the corresponding node parameter is polynomially bounded.
\item Find an exact polynomial representation when both exceptional-variable
union and aggregate affine rank grow, or exhibit a precise structural
counterfamily; pure affine rank growth is not hardness.
\item Charge all failed representations, losing policies, compatibility
joins, proof acquisition, intermediate size, witness reconstruction and peak
space for one answer-oblivious controller.
\item Keep ARC/FLMV, contextual information, proof-system and tape arguments
conditional or model-specific until an arbitrary-algorithm same-machine
bridge is proved.
\end{enumerate}
The next specific lemma is an all-input structural theorem for one precisely

\section{LSPV-5 time--space frontier synthesis: STATIC-PROXY-PARETO-REFEREE206}

\paragraph{Verified peer additions after run205.}
ARC230 proves that supplied width-three rank-one affine module stars are
NP-complete: each leaf exactly projects to a signed ternary clause.  Hence
maximum edge width and local affine rank alone are not a tractability
frontier; the root may retain the full source interface.  This is an

MATCHED-TORUS-BRIDGE232 constructs connected exact22 opposite-label sources
with matched signed occurrence-component spectra and growing treewidth, yet

It falsifies one tested portfolio and the inference from growing width to

BRANCHING-EXACT22-GRAFT167 constructs an unbounded connected two-answer
strict22 family with bag size at most 18 and binary adhesions.  Authenticated
tree DP/Davis--Putnam elimination gives polynomial decision, checked SAT

The latest verified LSPV-4 artifact remained HYBRID-BAD-MESSAGE228.  If a
newer scheduled body exists, it must be preserved and retried; no missing
scientific content is reconstructed here.

\paragraph{Corrected static proxy frontier.}
The necessary inequality $n\le3b+12bq$ permits the relaxed curve
\[
t(b)=b+\max\{0,n/(12b)-1/4\},\qquad s(b)=b.
\]
On its active branch, $t'(b)=1-n/(12b^2)$ and
$t''(b)=n/(6b^3)>0$.  Thus the unique continuous time minimum is at
$b=\sqrt{n/12}$, while first log-proxy equality occurs much later at
$b=n/3$.  At $n=1200$, the integer time minimum 20 occurs at $b=9,10,11$,
but $b=10,11$ are dominated by $b=9$ because they use more table space for
the same time.  The equality point $(400,400)$ is also dominated.

Under canonical $N=\Theta(n\log n)$, the optimum enumeration proxy is
$2^{\Theta(\sqrt{N/\log N})}$, still superpolynomial.  The equality point is
not efficient convergence.  Further, $2^b$ is the footprint of storing a
reusable boundary table, not mandatory decision space: a streaming exhaustive
scan uses $O(N+b+q)$ live bits.  No plotted point is a native FLMV pair without
a same-machine implementation.

\paragraph{Charged adaptive envelope.}
For deterministic certified methods $A_1,\ldots,A_m$, sequential attempts
obey $T\le D+\sum T_j$ and $S\le S_D+\max S_j$.  State-retaining dovetailing
obeys $T\le D+m\min_{j\text{ solves}}T_j$ but
$S\le S_D+\sum S_j$.  Consequently $(\min T_j,\min S_j)$ is not generally a
jointly achievable resource pair.  Exponentially many policies or restarts
also defeat a polynomial portfolio factor.

\paragraph{Ranked proof obligations.}
\begin{enumerate}
\item Construct an infinite two-answer source family realizing each claimed
frontier point; permitted points alone do not suffice.
\item Give simultaneous measured $(T,S)$ pairs for one declared machine,
including discovery, every failed route, source access, joins, reconstruction
and peak live state.
\item Prove a structural measure makes the portfolio sum, not only its best
member, polynomial on every input.
\item Bridge any resulting native pair to ARC/FLMV without importing table or
tape proxies across models.
\end{enumerate}
The next specific lemma is a realizable two-answer strict22 family with two
competing algorithms and a fully charged controller whose simultaneous Pareto
envelope is proved.  Both P=NP and P$\ne$NP remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\section{LSPV-5 source-normalized frontier update: REDUCTION-FRONTIER-TRANSFER-REFEREE207}

\paragraph{Exact transfer calculus.}
For an explicitly materialized one-child reduction, physical child size
$N^{\alpha+o(1)}$ rescales target time and space exponents by $\alpha$, while
reducer, reconstruction and materialization floors remain in the upper
envelope.  This is a composition theorem for a declared architecture, not a
lower bound against algorithms that bypass the child.  An on-demand encoding
requires a same-machine access implementation and charges every query.

\paragraph{Representation and proxy firewalls.}
Selector elimination proves that one fewer variable may have an explicit
child-size exponent tending to two.  The same projection has a linear circuit,
so the example proves neither inherent expansion nor general contraction; it
forces representation closure and access accounting.  Dividing the relaxed
static proxy by $\log N$ shows that both its optimum and its $q=0$ equality
point diverge in polynomial-exponent coordinates.  Consequently the plotted
proxy cannot be inserted into the native ARC/FLMV plane.

\paragraph{Fresh specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1's local-defect composition is a valid supplied-tree restricted upper
bound but duplicates the earlier hybrid theorem and leaves discovery open.
LSPV-2 supplies a realized connected exact22 family whose large proxy is
bypassed by a constant-state transfer solver.  LSPV-3 confirms that only a
global complete-state polynomial-value rank controls all adaptive actions;
resettable local ranks permit exponential work.  The latest fully verified
LSPV-4 source remains GRID229; a newer 15:36 body was absent and is pending
owner recovery.  No missing content was reconstructed.  P versus NP remains
\OPEN.


\section{Clause-Indexed Forbidden Cubes and Exact-Overlap Firewall}
\PROVED\ (restricted graph-indexed Horn family; independent referee pending).
For a connected simple graph \(H=(V,E)\), introduce one selector \(x_e\) per
edge and the connected proper Horn width-three source
\[
 F_H=\bigwedge_{e=\{u,v\}\in E}
 [(x_e\vee\neg u\vee\neg v)\land(\neg x_e\vee\neg u\vee\neg v)].
\]
The complete non-tautological first-generation opposite-occurrence resolvent
set is exactly \(R_e=(\neg u\vee\neg v)\).  Falsifying \(R_e\) gives the
two-parent RUP conflict \(x_e,\neg x_e\), certifying the whole cube
\(u=v=1\) impossible.  If \(n=|V|\), \(m=|E|\), and \(I(H)\) counts
independent vertex sets, then the exact forbidden-cube union is
\[
 U(F_H)=2^m(2^n-I(H)).
\]
Valiant's \(\#P\)-completeness of MONOTONE 2-SAT counting, variable
complementation, and one universal-vertex connectedization show that computing
\(U\) is \(\#P\)-complete under polynomial-time Turing reductions even on this
source class.  Yet \(F_H\) is satisfied by the all-zero assignment and is a
linear-time Horn instance.  This is a classification of an optional statistic,
not an unconditional FP lower bound or a SAT lower bound.

\PROVED\ (exact source-clause equity in the same family).
For edge \(e\), let \(t_e(H)\) count vertex subsets inducing exactly \(e\).
Both parent clauses have
\[
 Q_{e,+}=Q_{e,-}=2^{m-1}t_e(H)>0.
\]
Thus every source clause is critical although SAT is trivial.  On the star
\(K_{1,r}\), all \(r\) resolvents are distinct, raw cube weight is \(r/4\),
and exact union density is only
\(\frac12(1-2^{-r})<\frac12\).  Hence deduplication does not repair additive
pair scoring.

\FALSIFIED\ (free exact-overlap repair and intrinsic polarity value).
Exact \(Q_j\), distinct resolvent count, complement asymmetry, and raw
opposite-pair weight are diagnostics rather than general difficulty measures.
A ternary XOR quartet has twelve opposite pairs but no non-tautological first
resolvent and is solved by Gaussian elimination.  A uniform controller may
retain the Horn source, stream local RUP certificates, or switch to the
equivalent 2CNF graph without computing \(U\).

\COMPUTATIONAL.
The dependency-free run checked six connected graph sources and SAT/UNSAT XOR
controls: 700,752 assertion-accounted checks in 0.283 seconds.  Across the six
Horn inputs, source-UP, failed-literal, all-resolvent, paired-tail-2CNF and
recognized-Horn policies used respectively \(21,21,21,21,6\) search nodes and
\(672,1680,896,224,168\) literal reads.  Discovery, failed probes,
canonicalization, memo, witness checking, elapsed time and simultaneous Python
peak are recorded.  These finite measurements are not native tape or RAM lower
bounds.  The family is not strict signed exact-\((2,2)\).

Peers MERGE-FILTER239 and OCCURRENCE-LAZY-MERGE176 already established repeated
and distinct-cube overcount plus a no-table first-resolution UP closure.  The
new contribution is the exact-overlap counting classification and its
source-parent RUP/criticality audit; it does not supersede their closure
theorems.  Sources:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_2148_MAIN_FORBIDDEN-CUBE-COUNTING-REFEREE213.tex},
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_2148_FORBIDDEN_CUBE_COUNTING_REFEREE213_repro.py},
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_2148_FORBIDDEN_CUBE_COUNTING_REFEREE213_raw.json},
and
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-29_2148_LSPV5_TO_FIVE_ROLE_FORBIDDEN_CUBE_COUNTING213.md}.
P versus NP remains \OPEN.

\paragraph{Highest-value next lemma.}
For a supplied bounded-treewidth graph of first-generation forbidden cubes,
prove an exact overlap-and-marginal dynamic program with construction,
certificate lookup, residual conditioning, witness reconstruction and
simultaneous peak space charged.  Then determine whether the same graph width
remains bounded under adaptive residual conditioning.  Until an arbitrary-input
coverage and same-execution bridge is proved, neither the Horn counting
classification nor the finite policy data bears on general SAT complexity.
P versus NP remains \OPEN.


\section{Clause-Extreme Compatibility Firewall and Issue~\#3 Reconciliation}

\PROVED\ (exact local target matrix; adopted prior work).
For a signed occurrence \(x_{i,j}\) with negation bit \(\nu_{ij}\), local
\(S_j\) demands \(a_i=1\oplus\nu_{ij}\), while local \(U_j\) demands
\(a_i=\nu_{ij}\); inversion swaps the two.  For clauses \(j,k\), \(SS,UU\)
are feasible exactly when no shared variable has opposite polarity, and
\(SU,US\) are feasible exactly when no shared variable has the same polarity.
LSPV-1 EXTREME-SIGNED-CYCLE240 further characterizes simultaneous extreme
targets by balance of the signed clause-overlap multigraph.  These are
occurrence-target identities, not SAT or UNSAT tests.

\PROVED\ (coherent-search firewall; independent red-team theorem).
Let \(\alpha\) be any coherent partial assignment to the original source
variables.  If fully assigned clauses realize local \(S\) or \(U\) modes, all
their occurrence demands are exactly the values already in \(\alpha\).
Therefore every pair and the whole realized set pass the compatibility test.
Adding only the \(SS/SU/US/UU\) guard to a DPLL search over source variables
rejects no node and cannot shrink that search tree.  It may prune only an
alternative representation that independently guesses occurrence-level
targets, whose construction and reconstruction must be charged.

\FALSIFIED\ (dense extreme incompatibility implies decision difficulty).
For \(r\) duplicates each of
\((x\vee y\vee z)\) and \((x\vee y\vee\neg z)\), all \(r^2\) cross pairs
have no feasible extreme mode, yet the source is equivalent to two clauses
and has six of eight assignments as models.  Duplicates can make the
incompatibility graph arbitrarily dense without increasing decision
difficulty.  Likewise the dense overlapping XOR control has primal graph
\(K_6\), 840 all-four-incompatible pairs, and one model; it is affine and
Gaussian-solvable.

\PROVED\ (source-parent cube certificate; known resolution rule).
Opposite source parents \((x\vee A),(\neg x\vee B)\) yield the resolvent
\(A\vee B\).  If non-tautological, its falsifying partial cube cannot extend
to satisfy both parents.  This is genuine source-variable pruning, unlike the
raw compatibility guard.  LSPV-2 EXTREME-CYCLE-UP240 shows that its balanced
extreme-count threshold supplies no additional pruning after saturated UP in
the declared exact22 regime.  LSPV-3 PAIRED-EXTREME-CONTROLLER241 gives a
recognizable paired-pivot class equivalent to 2SAT and also shows strong
branch-order dependence; neither result covers general SAT.

\COMPUTATIONAL.
The run emitted every source clause and occurrence with explicit sign,
\(S\), inverted \(S\!\to U\), \(U\), and inverted \(U\!\to S\) targets;
all pair matrices; bounded multi-clause modes; both global endpoints;
complements; intermediate flip trails; and provenance-carrying prohibited
cubes.  Across twelve SAT/UNSAT hostile families, 967,957 exact checks passed.
The guarded and unguarded fixed-order UP--DPLL had identical node counts and
zero guard rejections on every input.  On dense XOR \(K_6\), guard work rose
from 911 to 1,101 units; charged bounded resolution kept three nodes but raised
work to 39,531.  On the contradictory padded-unit core, resolution reduced
nodes \(19\to3\) and work \(589\to573\).  Thus successful-path pruning and
total discovery work remain distinct.

\paragraph{Highest-value next lemma.}
Find a polynomially recognizable partial-assignment regime where a signed
extreme graph plus a certified middle-state bound derives a non-tautological
source clause not already absorbed by UP.  Charge recognition, proof checking,
failed policies, SAT witness validation, UNSAT certification and peak state,
and compare against best branch ordering and an affine/Horn/2SAT switch.
Until such a source-level consequence and arbitrary-input coverage are proved,
the extreme matrix is a representation firewall rather than a general SAT
shortcut.  P versus NP remains \OPEN.


\section{Cross-agent issue-3 synthesis and absorption selector (29 October 2026)}
\PROVED\ (verified recovery).  The default-branch \texttt{main.tex} had become a 5,344-byte marker-free placeholder containing the literal text ``full previous content retained.''  This living document is recovered from the readback-verified substantive snapshot at commit
\path{34b54a221f293c9e11ae600b1aa424dae06ce4bd}; no missing manuscript text was invented.  Verified later reports remain standalone evidence and are summarized here without changing their authorship.

\PROVED\ (current restricted theorem).  Let two proper 3-clause parents share exactly one variable with opposite polarity, and let \(s\) be their number of same-polarity shared tail variables.  Their non-tautological direct resolvent is absorbed by the two-parent formula iff \(s=0\).  For \(s=0\), falsifying all but any target resolvent literal makes the other parent unit on the pivot and then forces the target.  For \(s\ge1\), choosing a shared same-sign target reduces the parents to
\[
 (p\vee\ell)\wedge(\neg p\vee\ell),
\]
which unit propagation does not close.  Thus \(s\ge1\) is an exact parent-level non-absorption selector, not a full-formula selector.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ (charged hostile controls).  Across eleven SAT/UNSAT controls, 1,866,238 exact checks passed.  The local selector reduced fixed-order UP--DPLL charged work on W1 UNSAT from \(15371\) to \(12575\), but raised S15 SAT work from \(729\) to \(2904\).  On dense XOR \(K_6\), 60 locally selected candidates were all absorbed by the full formula; proving this by per-candidate UP probes cost \(903797\) work units versus baseline \(911\).  Therefore full-formula novelty discovery is not free, and no uniform benefit theorem is established.

\PROVED/\COMPUTATIONAL\ (verified peer ledger).  LSPV-1's two-sided-cover report gives conditional-expectation SAT criteria and a second-order Bonferroni UNSAT certificate only on pinned subcubes.  LSPV-2 proves the strong syntactic 2CNF-backdoor floor \(n/3\) and shows that equality does not synchronize complement residual answers.  LSPV-3 proves a Hamming-ball exclusion from pairwise disjoint falsified clauses, while its charged controller did not improve its finite controls.  LSPV-4 proves that a direct proper-3CNF resolvent is non-tautological exactly when the number of opposite-polarity common variables is one; its width is \(4-s\).  These are restricted clause-structure results, not arbitrary-algorithm bridges.

\FALSIFIED\ (overstatements).  Same-polarity overlap does not characterize full-formula non-absorption; adding every sound direct resolvent need not reduce charged work; and exact full-formula absorption selection cannot be treated as free preprocessing.  Endpoint failure, opposite local preferences, and incompatible local extremes remain non-certificates for formula UNSAT.

The next \OPEN\ lemma is a cheap sufficient full-formula non-absorption test, or an amortized one-program selector that improves charged total work on matched infinite SAT and UNSAT families without receiving an answer-class bit.  P versus NP remains OPEN.

\section{Signed NAE occurrence-two coverage and issue~\#3 red-team correction}

\PROVED\ (verified recovery).  The default-branch \texttt{main.tex} had again
become a 5,344-byte marker-free placeholder after the verified run215
reconciliation.  This document is recovered from the exact readback-verified
run215 commit \path{18cde6fda2d34ff0a56b3b8dfa746080ad9a7238}; no omitted text was
invented.  The later placeholder's standalone GROKPROOF pointer remains an
external artifact and is not silently promoted into synthesis.

\KNOWN\ theorem transfer and \PROVED\ restricted corollary.  Let
\[
 F=\bigwedge_p(D_p\wedge\overline{D_p}),
\]
where every \(D_p\) is a proper signed 3-clause and every source variable
belongs to at most two pairs.  Satisfying both members of pair \(p\) is
equivalent to NAE-satisfying \(D_p\).  Split opposite-sign occurrences into
two literal-value vertices and join them by a binary NAE edge; same-sign
occurrences share a vertex.  The binary edges form a matching.  The known
NAE-SAT(2) obstruction theorem reduces non-two-colourability to an odd
component of the residual binary graph, so the matching cannot obstruct.
Consequently every formula in this paired occurrence-two class is SAT and a
witness is constructible by the published linear-time algorithm.

\FALSIFIED\ (necessary signed-dual balance).  The balance condition in
SIGNED-NAE-DUAL-MATCH242 remains sufficient for its matching construction but
is unnecessary for SAT coverage.  Exact enumeration checked all 64 K4, all
512 prism, all 512 \(K_{3,3}\), and a seeded 32-class Petersen sample:
all 1,120 classes were SAT, including 1,048 unbalanced classes.  This is a
correction within the paired class, not a theorem about arbitrary strict
exact-\((2,2)\) formulas.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ (clause-indexed issue~\#3 audit).  Eleven SAT/UNSAT hostile
controls record every source occurrence's \(S/U\) targets and inversions,
pair and bounded multi-clause compatibility, \(0^n/1^n\), complement and flip
trails, authenticated resolvent cubes, exact models, and charged fixed-order
UP--DPLL work.  There were 1,576,960 sign-class assignment evaluations and
833,501 additional assertion-accounted checks in 5.379 seconds.  Resolution
reduced the padded-unit core from 19 to 3 nodes and charged work from 589 to
573, but raised paired balanced K4 work from 196 to 544 and planted dense SAT
from 1,034 to 6,394.  Endpoint failure, unbalanced signs, local extreme
incompatibility and node reduction therefore remain non-certificates of
UNSAT or uniform total-work improvement.

Sources:

Characterize coherent partial-assignment residuals of the paired class.

contradiction, with UP/2SAT/XOR switching, failed probes, reconstruction and

remain \OPEN.


\section{Pinned NAE residual firewall and issue~\#3 peer reconciliation}

\PROVED\ (verified recovery).  The default-branch \texttt{main.tex} was a
43-byte literal placeholder with no ownership regions, scientific body or
sentinel.  This document is recovered from the exact readback-verified run216
commit \path{7588a195bc88f936fbc00e66d19fd137b9ca39df}, blob
\path{f2c4039cea65c8ebe1b04ddf7ca021ea506f7389}.  No missing placeholder text
is invented.

\PROVED\ (exact pinned-NAE calculus; known strong-backdoor overlap).
For a signed ternary NAE clause hit by a coherent partial assignment, mixed
pinned literal values satisfy the clause; equal pins leave one ordinary clause
requiring a free literal to take the opposite truth value.  Therefore any
partial assignment hitting every designated NAE clause leaves an exact 2CNF
residual.  SCC decides extension and authenticates an inextendible cube.

\FALSIFIED\ (raw binary-disequality interpretation).  Pinning one literal of
\(\operatorname{NAE}(x,y,z)\) does not create \(y\ne z\).  At \(x=0\) the exact
residual is \(y\vee z\), and \(y=z=1\) is valid.  Across every hitting partial
assignment of all 64 signed paired-K4 classes, exact completion agreed with
the generated 2CNF on 40,704 cubes; the disequality replacement falsely
rejected 2,304 extendible cubes.  There were no false acceptances because it
only adds clauses.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ (full clause-indexed hostile audit).  Eleven controls record
every original occurrence's \(S/U\) targets and inversions, pair and bounded
multi-clause modes, coherent endpoints, complements, flip trails,
source-parent resolvents, prohibited cubes, models and charged fixed-order
UP--DPLL work.  The K4 audit classified 26,592 extendible and 14,112
inextendible hitting cubes, with 95,964 external completion checks.  The full
benchmark audit added 328,074 checks in 1.67 seconds.  Resolution reduced nodes
on endpoint plateau \(H_1\) and UNSAT controls but increased charged work
except on the padded-unit core.

\paragraph{Verified peer ledger after run216.}
LSPV-4 ENDPOINT-PLATEAU243 constructs connected strict22 SAT sources where
both endpoints and all one-flips preserve the number of false clauses.
LSPV-1 BALANCED-OCCURRENCE-FIREWALL243 proves its first-order count is
UP-dominated and equality leaves exact 2CNF.  LSPV-2
SECOND-ORDER-FLIP-SPECTRUM243 compiles aggregate two-flip histograms in linear
indexed-RAM work but does not certify a general branch.  LSPV-3
MATCHING-HAMMING-FIREWALL244 computes a minimum hitting repair for initially
false clauses and proves ordinary partial-cube exclusion is UP-dominated.
LSPV-1 EXTREME-FOREST-MODELCOUNT244 supplies a restricted Hunter-bound model
certificate but costs more than baseline on its controls.  These are
restricted upper bounds and firewalls, not arbitrary-algorithm bridges.

\section{Exact twenty-clause strict22 floor and selector red-team correction}

\KNOWN\ theorem and \PROVED\ exact corollary.  Zhang--Peitl--Szeider's
SAT~2024 classification gives $\mu(3,2,2)=20$ for proper 3CNF with at most
two positive and two negative occurrences per variable.  Every strict signed
exact-$(2+,2-)$ source is in that class.  Conversely, the connected original
$W_1$ source has $15$ variables and $20$ distinct proper clauses, exact
two-per-polarity degrees, and zero models under independent enumeration of all
$2^{15}$ coherent assignments.  Therefore the minimum number of clauses of an
UNSAT strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ source is exactly $20$, even with
connectedness required.  This sharpens the project's eight-clause and
low-defect floors; it is an extremal clause-count theorem, not a SAT runtime
lower bound or algorithm.

\PROVED/\COMPUTATIONAL\ selector audit.  LSPV-1's eight-pattern selector
resolution is sound: eight private-pivot resolutions, four $x_3$, two $x_2$
and one $x_1$ resolution derive the unit $(s)$, and deleting any pattern admits
an explicit $s=0$ witness.  The construction is not strict22: the selector and
shared pattern variables have much larger occurrence counts.  Its measured
node advantage is branch-order dependent.  With $s$ first, the independent
source-UP--DPLL replay refutes the opposing double selector in $15$ nodes,
rather than the peer order's $4103$; the multi-parent proof remains valid.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ original-clause audit.  The full trace records every signed
source occurrence's $S/U$ targets and inversions, indexed
$SS/SU/US/UU$ matrices, bounded triple modes, coherent endpoints,
complements, flip trails, non-tautological source resolvents and prohibited
cubes.  W1 and its strict22 SAT twin have identical endpoint histograms
$(1,10,7,2)$ and $(2,7,10,1)$, but respectively zero and $54$ models.
Thus endpoint failure and aggregate local conflicts remain non-certificates of
UNSAT.  The independent checker reports PASS on $2407$ recomputed checks.

\section{LSPV-5 failed-literal/RUP referee: short proof versus charged discovery}

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, source-specific.}
For the authenticated 15-variable strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ SAT source
SWAP6, the source entails \(x_2\).  Under \(\neg x_2\), a 28-addition
resolution/RUP refutation uses only original clauses, the assumption, and
earlier additions; its maximum derived width is three and it introduces no
auxiliary variable.  The independent checker replays all parents, pivots,
resolvents, non-tautology tests, RUP conflicts, and the final empty clause.
This is a shorter source-specific proof object than the earlier
542-compatible-subset inclusion--exclusion certificate; it neither replaces
that general identity nor supplies an unbounded-family theorem.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED.}
A short discovered certificate cannot be treated as a free shortcut.  The
uniform width-three saturation that found the certificate considered 39,288
pivot pairs, derived 957 clauses before the empty clause, and performed
3,088,548 naive subsumption tests.  On SWAP6 the charged controller used
3,168,278 selected events versus 5,693 for the common UP--DPLL baseline.
Verification plus the forced-literal backend uses only 4,192 selected events
when the proof is supplied, but supplying it hides acquisition and is
nonuniform.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT representation audit.}
Variable-labelled same/opposite relations between signed occurrences
reconstruct the signing up to independent variable complementation.  A star
of \(d_v-1\) relation bits per variable is necessary and sufficient for
arbitrary \(d_v\) occurrences modulo complementation.  For strict exact22,
the six two-positive signings collapse into three complement classes, so one
trit per variable is necessary and sufficient.  In contrast, aggregation is
insufficient: W1 (UNSAT) and SWAP6 (SAT with 54 models) have identical
\(0^n/1^n\) endpoint histograms and the same aggregate pair-mode histogram
\((117,13,43,17)\).  Full labelled relations are complete only because they
re-encode source signs up to a satisfiability-preserving polarity renaming.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
A separate replay passed 417,047 checks over nine SAT/UNSAT controls,
including every clause-indexed \(S/U\) target and inversion, 5,304 pair-mode
checks, 99,480 complement identities, 310,736 forbidden-resolvent-cube
exclusions, complete W1/SWAP6 counts, and the 28 proof steps.  These Python
selected-event counts are not RAM, Turing-machine, CDCL, or arbitrary-SAT
lower bounds.

\textbf{Highest-value next lemma.}
Find a polynomial-time recognizable unbounded connected strict22 SAT-and-UNSAT
family whose post-UP forced literals have polynomially discoverable
bounded-width RUP certificates and whose fully charged uniform controller
improves total work on both sides; otherwise prove a model-matched separation
between short-proof existence and discovery on such a family.  P versus NP
remains \OPEN.

\section{LSPV-5 bounded Kronecker referee and sparse-support firewall}

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted.}
For a proper sign-balanced ternary source with \(m=2h\), every coherent
complete-assignment profile obeys
\[
 N_1=h-2N_0+N_3,\qquad N_2=h+N_0-2N_3.
\]
The algebraically permitted \((N_0,N_3)\) pairs number exactly
\[
 \left\lfloor h^2/2+h+1\right\rfloor
 =\left\lfloor m^2/8+m/2+1\right\rfloor.
\]
At \(m=20\) this gives 61 permitted profiles; W1 realizes 24 and SWAP6
realizes 30.  Permitted profiles are not asserted realizable by one source.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT representation correction.}
The complete bounded four-category profile also admits a collision-free
one-exponent encoding.  With \(M=h+1\),
\[
 \kappa(N_0,N_3)=N_0+MN_3
\]
decodes by radix division, after which the displayed identities recover
\(N_1,N_2\).  For Laurent differences in \([-h,h]^2\),
\(u+(2h+1)v\) is injective.  This is bounded, source-size-dependent
Kronecker substitution.  It does not contradict two-dimensional lattice
rank: no fixed map of this form is injective on all of \(\mathbb Z^2\).

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED.}
It is false that only a two-variable Laurent representation can return the
full bounded histogram.  LSPV-2 BALANCED-TWO-COORD249's two-generator
quotient remains correct and natural, and its rank-two witness remains valid
for the unbounded lattice interpretation.  Collision-free packing is a
bijection on the stored support, however, so it preserves nonzero coefficient
count and direct sparse term-pair generation.  The packed exponent reaches
\(\Theta(m^2)\); its bit length is asymptotically the combined length of the
two original coordinates.  Thus one displayed coordinate supplies no
automatic sparse-state or SAT-time contraction.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
A separate checker passed 387,160 claims on nine SAT/UNSAT controls:
every source \(S/U\) target and inversion, 5,304 indexed pair modes,
coherent endpoints and complements, 379,024 resolvent-cube completions,
83 balanced identities/decodings, six state-count formulas, exact model
counts, and same-answer controller checks.  The full-profile prepass did not
prune the common UP--DPLL backend: W1 used 1,975,190 selected events versus
9,110 baseline, and SWAP6 1,970,693 versus 4,613.  These Python counters are
not a RAM, tape, CDCL, or arbitrary-algorithm lower bound.  Gaussian, Horn,
2SAT and source-retention switches remain legal.

\textbf{Highest-value next lemma.}
Determine whether conditioned selected-prefix connected-log updates admit
polynomially bounded bit complexity and positive fully charged total-work
improvement on one unbounded connected strict22 SAT-and-UNSAT family, while
allowing Kronecker packing, source retention, representation switches,
failed prefixes and explicit reconstruction.  P versus NP remains \OPEN.

\section{Verified restoration and current clause-extreme reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-hitting-cube-221}
\noindent\textbf{Recovery provenance (PROVED/EXACT).}
The repository-root file had become the 26-byte literal
\texttt{PLACEHOLDER\_TO\_BE\_REPLACED} (blob
\texttt{da6eedc6b37ba9ee10787108a2ac3078d97f0b1e}).  This snapshot restores
the last verified substantive source from commit
\texttt{b6ccd7d2578dd12268ea240539cc34ae91db2717}, preserves all five
ownership regions and prior authorship, and adds only this labelled reconciliation.
No missing manuscript prose was reconstructed.  Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain
\textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{Current specialist evidence.}
LSPV-1 EXTREME-LOCAL-SUPPORT249 proves a bounded-variable false-cube cover
criterion but exhibits a 14-variable SWAP6 residual obstruction to smaller
source support.  LSPV-2 DYNAMIC-CLUSTER250 gives fixed-radius local histogram
updates after preprocessing but no decision speedup.  LSPV-3
ENDPOINT-ROOTED251 gives sound original-parent resolvents and a complete fallback;
its charged full S/U diagnostic can exceed baseline work.  LSPV-4
NEAR-FULL-TRUE-FPT249 gives a bounded-middle FPT theorem but proves that strict22
SAT witnesses require at least $3m/4$ middle clauses, preventing small-$k$
promotion.

\paragraph{LSPV-5 independent hitting firewall.}
For a hitting CNF, distinct clause-falsifying cubes are pairwise disjoint, so
\[
 \#\mathrm{SAT}(F)=2^n\left(1-\sum_{C\in F}2^{-|C|}\right).
\]
This is Iwama's known theorem.  Its proper width-three corollary says $m\le8$
and UNSAT iff $m=8$, with polynomial recognition, exact counting, decision and
witness self-reduction on this restricted class.  The sharp seven-clause control
has conflict graph $K_7$, fails both $0^3$ and $1^3$, yet has unique model $001$
with $(N_0,N_1,N_2,N_3)=(0,3,3,1)$.  Therefore complete source conflict,
two failed endpoints, or a failed small-middle search does not certify UNSAT.
Ten-source replay made 361,809 independent assertions, all passing; the charged
recognizer was slower than fixed UP--DPLL on the tiny controls.  The full report is
\texttt{research/main/2026-10-30\_0548\_MAIN\_HITTING-CUBE-FIREWALL-REFEREE221.tex}.

\section{06:48 synthesis: exact full-boundary composition and current firewalls}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; KNOWN THEOREM TRANSFER.}
For a supplied and verified tree of hitting CNF modules, with original clauses
assigned once, running intersection for every variable, and full module interface

and witness reconstruction use standard junction-tree/project--join messages in
\[
 D_{\rm verify}+N^{O(1)}\sum_t2^{|B_t|}
\]
bit operations.  Thus polynomial discovery, polynomially many modules and
\(|B_t|=O(\log N)\) give a polynomial algorithm on this restricted encoded
class.  Hitting supplies an exact local false-cube count; the global recurrence

opposite ends of a path whose middle bag omits \(x\) gives a positive naive

\section{11:48 integrated ARC/FLMV red-team reconciliation}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, conditional black-box necessary condition.}
Assume \(P=NP\) and let \(B\) be a polynomial SAT decider.  For fixed \(D\),
the guest \(A_D\) delays \(m^D\) steps (or touches \(m^D\) writable cells) on
an \(m\)-bit query before returning \(B\)'s exact answer.  If a wrapper-oblivious
compiler claiming
\[
 K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda K\},\qquad
 L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda L\}
\]
unavoidably completes \(A_D\) on a physical query of length
\(m\ge N^{\eta-o(1)}\) with \(\eta>\lambda\), then sufficiently large fixed
\(D\) gives actual exponent \(\eta D\) above both claimed envelopes.  Hence
every unavoidable completed black-box guest query must be
\(N^{\lambda+o(1)}\) or shorter.  This does not exclude code analysis,
preemption, guest avoidance, or a source-only solver.  Report:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1148_MAIN_ARC-DELAY-QUERY-FIREWALL223.tex}.

\]

\section*{LSPV-5 Reconciliation: ARC-EXPONENT-DISCOVERY-FIREWALL224}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a conditional computability theorem.}
Assume one fixed correct polynomial-time ordinary-SAT decider \(B\) exists,
with finite joint time/space exponents.  For every integer \(d\) above those
exponents, there is no total computable code analyzer which, even under the
promise that every supplied program is a correct polynomial-time SAT decider,
always distinguishes
\[
  T_A,S_A=O(N^d)
  \qquad\hbox{from}\qquad
  T_A,S_A=\Omega(N^{d+1}).
\]
Given a machine \(M\) and word \(w\), the wrapper simulates \(M(w)\) for
\(N\) steps, sequentially fills \(N^{d+1}\) writable cells iff a halt is
seen, and then returns \(B\)'s exact answer.  A classifier would decide the
halting problem.  Therefore the universal residual controller may not receive
a free exact/tight \(K,L\) threshold bit from arbitrary guest code.  This does
not refute an exponent-oblivious \(\Phi\), and it does not prevent an external
proof from quantifying over the unknown actual exponents.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT finite-prefix firewall.}
For every finite benchmark cutoff, a late-trigger wrapper is identical to a
benign wrapper on every tested input length but eventually has exponent
\(d+1\).  Finite timing or regression data cannot certify the ARC exponent.
The safe repair is a structural exponent-oblivious interface bound or a
separately verified syntactic resource certificate whose restriction is made
explicit.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The source-indexed producer passed 460 assertions and the independent checker
passed 52 groups on three SAT and one UNSAT width-three sources, retaining all
original-clause \(S/U\) targets, inversions, pair modes, coherent endpoints
and complement identities.  Symbolic fill counts were not physically
executed.  Report, code, raw SHA-256 and handoff are readback-verified at
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1248_MAIN_ARC-EXPONENT-DISCOVERY-FIREWALL224.tex},
\path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1248_ARC_EXPONENT_DISCOVERY_FIREWALL224_repro.py},
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_1248_ARC_EXPONENT_DISCOVERY_FIREWALL224_manifest.md},
and
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-30_1248_LSPV-5_ARC-EXPONENT-DISCOVERY-FIREWALL224_HANDOFF.md}.

The integrated peer state is retained: LSPV-1's completed-call spectrum,
LSPV-2's fixed-\(B\) policy-entry frontier, LSPV-3's SAT-only selector
equivalence, and LSPV-4's far-address/materialization guard are restricted
necessary conditions or firewalls, not a constructed all-input contractor.
The single weakest arrow remains a hereditary both-label ordinary-SAT stage

\section*{LSPV-5 Reconciliation: ARC-GUEST-REENTRY-FIREWALL225}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted arbitrary-guest simulation falsifier.}
Let \(B\) be any total exact ordinary-SAT decider.  A fixed wrapper \(A_B\)
finds the physical input length \(m\), indexes its read-only input head to
\(m+1\), moves left twice and records the symbol at \(m-1\), then indexes
directly to \(m-1\) and records that symbol again.  It returns \(B\)'s answer
when the symbols agree and its complement otherwise.  On every nonempty
physical input they agree, so \(A_B\) is correct on SAT and UNSAT and adds
only \(O(m)\) time and \(O(\log m)\) writable space.

A virtual-child simulator that handles the far indexed jump by setting a
sticky out-of-bounds state and returns blank after subsequent ordinary head
moves until another indexed jump is incorrect: the first recorded symbol is
blank, the later valid jump reads the actual last bit, and on an encoding
ending in \(1\) the wrapper reverses \(B\).  Thus the existing far-address
comparison is necessary but not sufficient.  A complete simulator must also
reproduce ordinary moves, boundary re-entry, index erasure, guest work/index
tapes and exact blank semantics inside the charged multiplicative access
tariff.  This is a restricted compiler counterexample, not a SAT lower bound
and not a contradiction to FLMV's complete on-demand Cook encoding.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
One fixed identity emitter and wrapper were checked on a proper-3CNF SAT
source and the proper-3CNF UNSAT source containing all eight sign patterns.
Physical/exact re-entry returned the exhaustive labels; sticky OOB and a
valid-domain-only wraparound accessor reversed both.  The audit also passed
7,990 bounded address-shadow membership checks and 7,990 erasure resets.
These logical checks are not native FLMV timings.  Artifacts:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1354_MAIN_ARC-GUEST-REENTRY-FIREWALL225.tex},
\path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1348_guarded_virtual_input_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_1348_guarded_virtual_input_audit.json}.

LSPV-1 ONEHOT-CHILD-SELECT253 proves a restricted source-probe floor for one
output bit but permits a full-scan/cache escape.  LSPV-2
VIRTUAL-SORT-ACCESS254 gives an exact polylogarithmic indexed-bit accessor for
an intermediate existential sorting consistency CNF, but the physical
ordinary encoding expands and is not power-short.  LSPV-3

\[
 K_*=\max\{1.1+\pi,1.2+2\delta\},\qquad L_*=1/2+\eta
\]

\section*{LSPV-5 Reconciliation: ARC-TOTAL-VIRTUAL-INPUT-RECONCILIATION226}
\KNOWN\ (FLMV Theorem 3.4; explicit head accounting independently reconstructed).
Let a fixed deterministic indexed random-access guest \(A\) run on a complete
ordinary logical child \(G(x)\) of exact length \(m(x)\).  Suppose a total
charged accessor returns \(G(x)_j\) for \(j<m(x)\) and the ordinary blank
symbol otherwise.  A same-model simulator stores the exact logical guest input
head \(h\): copy the input-index address into \(h\) before indexed-jump
erasure, update \(h\) on ordinary moves, compare with \(m(x)\) on every
read, and invoke the accessor only in range.  If the guest uses \(S(m)\)
counted space and \(T(m)\) time, then
\[
 \operatorname{bitlength}(h)\le S(m)+\lceil\log_2(T(m)+2)\rceil+O(1).
\]
For compiler setup \(P,U\) and accessor time/scratch \(R,V\), this gives
\[
 T_{\rm sim}=O\!\left(P+T(m)(R+\log(m+T(m)+2))\right),\qquad
 S_{\rm sim}=O\!\left(U+V+S(m)+\log(T(m)+2)\right).
\]
The fixed simulator does not receive the guest exponent, a SAT label, or a
best-policy oracle.  This closes the read-only logical-input model bridge
\emph{under the complete total-access hypotheses}; it does not construct
those hypotheses or a smaller child.

\PROVED\ (implementation firewall).
Naive child materialization on a work tape, sticky out-of-range state, and an
accessor defined only on valid addresses remain invalid.  Far-jump/re-entry
counterexamples attack those partial implementations, not FLMV's total
on-demand simulation.  In matched SAT/UNSAT finite controls, 49,216 exhaustive
or seeded deterministic guest traces had identical complete states under
physical and total-virtual input, while sticky and valid-domain-only variants
failed on both labels.  These are \COMPUTATIONAL\ interface controls, not
native FLMV timings.

\section{LSPV-5 persistent-child source-traffic reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-batch-union-227}
\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted fixed-schedule theorem).}
For a source permutation \(\pi\in S_m\), suppose one persistent deterministic
controller retains \(s\) source-dependent bits and serves any number of child
rounds whose fixed public query union contains \(t\) distinct inverse demands.
If \(Q\) is the total number of source-word probes across the whole execution,
then
\[
  2^s m^Q\ge (m)_t,
  \qquad
  s+Q\log_2m\ge\log_2 (m)_t.
\]
The child-round count is absent.  Sixteen children repeatedly asking only
\(\pi^{-1}(0),\pi^{-1}(1)\) give an explicit countercontrol: one scan and a
six-bit cache answer all later children.  Exhaustion of all \(8!\) sources gives
2--8 probes (mean 6), whereas an invalid sixteenfold multiplication would demand
\(16\log_2 56\approx92.918\) bits.  Therefore per-child tensorization under
persistent caching is \FALSIFIED/\RETIRED unless a fresh-independent-demand,
state-erasure, or model-matched direct-sum theorem is separately proved.

\textbf{FLMV accounting consequence.}
Charge actual total source traffic and retained cache simultaneously.  A
controller may cache and pay space or discard and pay re-probes; it may not
multiply a one-child lower bound by the child count while retaining answers for
free.  This restricted metadata theorem neither supplies SAT hardness nor
constructs a complete power-short ordinary-SAT child.

\textbf{Current specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1 ARC-HEAD-HORIZON-REPLAY255 gives a uniform exponent-oblivious replay
schedule under bounded horizon, but no smaller child.  LSPV-2
AFFINE-GUARD257 proves that complete sign blocks yield exact XOR equations and
also gives same-core opposite-label controls; partial extracted affine theory is
not decision-complete.  LSPV-3 FRONTIER-TWO-SWEEP259 supplies useful restricted
frontier-DP evidence when order, width and table costs are paid, but the inspected
canonical report lacked its required final verification sentinel and therefore
needs a verified republish before serving as synthesis authority.  LSPV-4
ARC-SOURCE-PROBE-TRANSCRIPT257's one-batch transcript theorem is retained; the
new persistent-round audit blocks only an unjustified tensorization of it.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The accompanying audit exhausts 120,960 permutation/schedule instances and
records same-source SAT and UNSAT clause-extreme and complete-DPLL controls.
These host-language counts are neither native FLMV transition counts nor an
arbitrary-algorithm lower bound.

\section{LSPV-5 reusable boundary-summary reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-reusable-boundary-228}
\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted deterministic one-way theorem).}
For every \(f:\{0,1\}^b\to\{0,1\}\) there is a uniform proper 3-CNF
\(A_f(X,Y)\) with
\[
  \exists Y\,A_f(x,Y)=f(x)
\]
and fixed explicit encoding length \(N_b=\Theta(b^2 2^b)\).  Proper-3CNF
continuations \(B_a\) pin \(X=a\), hence
\[
  \operatorname{SAT}(A_f\land B_a)=f(a).
\]
If a deterministic preprocessor sees \(A_f\) before the continuation, stores a
summary \(S_f\), and every point continuation must be answered from
\((S_f,B_a)\) without rereading \(A_f\), then \(f\mapsto S_f\) is injective.
There are \(2^{2^b}\) functions, so some summary has at least
\[
  2^b=\Omega\!\left(\frac{N_b}{(\log N_b)^2}\right)=N_b^{1-o(1)}
\]
bits.  Thus no fixed \(N^\lambda\), \(\lambda<1\), gives a universal exact
reusable pre-continuation frontier summary on all CNFs.

\textbf{Scope firewall.}
This is the deterministic one-way INDEX/knowledge-compilation lower bound in an
explicit proper-3CNF realization.  It is not exponential in source length and
does not lower-bound a solver that sees both sides jointly, retains or rereads
the source, recomputes, creates continuation-specific children, or avoids
frontier summaries.  It therefore cannot be imported as a general SAT runtime
lower bound or an FLMV contradiction.  It retires only the declared universal
reusable-summary architecture.

\textbf{Current specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1 ARC-JUMP-RESET-BISIM256 corrects the earlier no-reentry sentence and
retains exact total jump/reset input-head semantics; no smaller child follows.
LSPV-2 AFFINE-INTERFACE258 proves an exact recognized-source rank quotient with
same-skeleton SAT/UNSAT controls, but emits no \(N^{1/2}\) physical child and its
charged hybrid increased measured total work.  LSPV-3
RECURSIVE-FRONTIER-REPLAY260 gives a valid paid Hirschberg-style replay schedule;
the reusable-summary theorem above blocks promotion of its frontier function to
a universally power-short pre-continuation message, while leaving its restricted
upper bound intact.  LSPV-4 ARC-RESPONSE-WIDTH258 gives the standard prescribed-
order residual-function state floor; paired order, replay and source access are
explicit escapes and must be charged.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The reproducer exhausts all 65,792 functions for \(b=3,4\), performs 1,050,624
point-query checks, 87,360 existential chain checks through \(b=8\), and 32
proper-3CNF pin checks.  Matched 32-clause SAT/UNSAT controls retain every
original-clause \(S/U\) target and inversion, 992 pair compatibility records,
endpoints, flip trails and common-DPLL work.  These are host-language
construction checks, not native FLMV measurements.
